### [1] [DUET: Decoupled Upper- and Lower-Body Execution for Task-Agnostic Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23872)
*Kapalik Khanal,Biswash Khatiwada,Stephen Afrifa,Fan Wu,Ramesh Bahadur Bist*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DUET，一个将移动与操作解耦的机器人执行框架：强化学习移动策略只控制12个腿部关节和1个腰部偏航关节，手臂关节仅作为观测输入，从而使得任意兼容的上肢操作策略（如VLA模型）可即插即用地替换而无需重训练平衡控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人更换操作（manipulation）策略时，不应被迫重新训练维持平衡的控制器；现有方法通常需要共享训练或显式协调，导致模块耦合、替换成本高。

Method: 在23自由度Unitree G1上，用强化学习训练移动策略控制12个腿部关节与1个腰部偏航关节，跟踪平面速度、转向率和骨盆高度（可深蹲至直立）；10个手臂关节被排除在动作空间外，仅以位置和速度作为观测，两策略的命令只在机器人接口层合并。硬件上用同一移动检查点分别搭配GR00T N1.7与pi0.5两个视觉-语言-动作模型验证模块化。并通过消融实验（重点考察上肢姿态课程）与鲁棒性测试（外力推挤、负载）评估。

Result: 仿真中移动控制器未发生跌倒，平面速度、偏航率、骨盆高度跟踪误差分别为0.186 m/s、0.142 rad/s、0.016 m。消融显示上肢姿态课程为最关键组件，去除后回合存活时间从20.0 s降至7.4 s，跌倒率升至每环境分钟6.06次。鲁棒性测试：在训练负载范围内、最高1.0 m/s推挤下无跌倒；在负载上限两倍时，0.7 m/s以下推挤亦无跌倒。

Conclusion: 移动与操作可在接口层解耦组合，操作策略能够在不重训练、不重启、不触碰保持直立层的情况下被直接替换，验证了模块化人形机器人控制的可行性。

Abstract: Changing a humanoid robot's manipulation policy should not require retraining the controller that maintains the robot's balance. This study introduces DUET, a decoupled execution framework that enables locomotion and manipulation controllers to operate simultaneously by combining their commands only at the robot interface. A reinforcement-learning locomotion policy controls the 12 leg joints and a waist yaw joint, tracking planar velocity, turning rate, and pelvis height from deep crouching to upright standing on a 23-degree-of-freedom Unitree G1. The 10 arm joints are excluded from the action space, while their positions and velocities are included only as observations, so that any compatible manipulation policy that generates arm-joint targets can control the upper body, and the locomotion policy keeps the lower body stable without requiring shared training or direct coordination. This study demonstrates the modular approach on hardware by pairing the same locomotion checkpoint with two different vision-language-action models, GR00T N1.7 and pi0.5. In simulation evaluation, the locomotion controller kept the robot's lower body stable with no falls while tracking the commanded planar velocity, yaw rate, and pelvis height with errors of 0.186 m/s, 0.142 rad/s, and 0.016 m, respectively. Ablation studies identify the upper-body pose curriculum as the dominant component, and removing this training step reduces episode survival from 20.0 to 7.4 seconds and increases the fall rate to 6.06 per environment minute. Robustness tests show no falls under pushes of up to 1.0 m/s within the trained payload range, and no falls at twice the payload limit for pushes below 0.7 m/s. These results show that a manipulation policy can be exchanged without retraining, restarting, or otherwise touching the layer that keeps the robot upright.

</details>


### [2] [Context-Aware RGB-Thermal-Radar Obstacle Perception for Resource-Constrained ASVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23873)
*Pranav Kedia,Edwin Redhead,Heiko Hamann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合RGB、长波红外、FMCW毫米波雷达与惯性信息的上下文感知感知架构，面向小型自主水面艇，在真实湖泊昼夜条件下完成闭环航行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型自主水面艇的障碍感知面临反射与眩光干扰可见光相机、艇体运动导致水线漂移、夜间失去主导感知模态等困难，多模态融合不应只被视为静态精度增益，而应在模态失效时保持可用并实现可预测的性能退化。

Method: 结合RGB与长波红外（LWIR）图像、FMCW毫米波雷达以及惯性上下文，设计上下文感知融合架构；输出方位扇区接口，向导航控制器提供经校准的障碍概率、雷达距离和传感器状态，并可区分传感器掉线与"有效观测但无障碍"两种情况；使用新采集的多模态湖泊数据，在非受控湖面条件下开展24段闭环试航（16段白天、4段黄昏、4段夜间，共351米），其中夜间段RGB不可用、仅依赖LWIR与雷达。

Result: 在8段留出的低光照记录上，校准融合模型的平均精度AP为0.869、期望校准误差为0.108，而规则融合为AP 0.673、门控融合为0.620，纯RGB为AP 0.903。结果揭示核心权衡：RGB可用时排序性能最好，融合则在RGB失效时仍能保留可用的导航输入。

Conclusion: 该工作使感知在多日自主水面艇任务中保持可用且性能退化可预测，是迈向这一目标的一步。

Abstract: Reliable obstacle perception is challenging for small autonomous surface vessels (ASVs): reflections and glare confound visible-light cameras, vessel motion shifts the apparent waterline, and darkness removes the dominant sensing modality altogether. Rather than treating multimodal fusion as a static accuracy gain, we propose a context-aware architecture that combines RGB and long-wave infrared (LWIR) imagery, frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) mmWave radar, and inertial context. A bearing-sector interface provides the navigation controller with calibrated obstacle probabilities, radar ranges, and sensor status. It also distinguishes sensor dropout from a valid observation with no detected obstacle. Using new multimodal lake recordings, we deployed the pipeline in a 24-leg closed-loop pilot under uncontrolled lake conditions (16 day, 4 dusk, and 4 night; 351 m), including night legs in which RGB was unusable and the vessel drove on LWIR and radar evidence alone. On eight held-out low-light recordings, the calibrated fusion model achieved average precision (AP) 0.869 and expected calibration error 0.108, compared with AP 0.673 for rule-based fusion, 0.620 for gated fusion, and 0.903 for RGB alone. Our results expose a central tradeoff: RGB ranks best when usable, whereas fusion preserves a navigation input when RGB fails. This work is a step toward perception that remains usable and degrades predictably across multi-day ASV missions.

</details>


### [3] [Adaptive Question Generation for Ambiguous Robot Instructions Based on Information Gain and Human Cognitive Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23874)
*Ryo Terashima,Yuga Yano,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应提问生成机制，在概率判别效率基础上融入人类认知特性，使服务机器人围绕视觉属性生成用户易回答的问题，在120个场景数据集上达到88.5%的目标识别成功率，并获得较高的回答容易度主观评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人的自然语言指令常含歧义，在多相似物体场景中需通过提问确定目标物体。现有提问生成方法仅关注概率判别效率，忽略用户回答的难易程度，可能导致回答错误或不确定，进而增加澄清轮次、降低对话效率。

Method: 先用视觉语言模型（VLM）提取候选物体的视觉属性；再计算各属性的信息增益（IG），选择最能降低候选集不确定性的属性；当多个属性IG相同时，基于认知特性的视觉属性过滤器选出用户最易直觉感知的属性；最后用所选属性及各候选物体的属性值提示VLM生成简洁的自然语言问题，循环此过程直至唯一确定目标。

Result: 在包含120个场景的数据集评估中，目标识别成功率达88.5%；在回答容易度的主观评价中也获得较高评分。

Conclusion: 将概率判别效率与人类认知特性相结合的自适应提问生成机制，可在保持较高识别成功率的同时提升用户回答的直觉性与便捷性，改善人机澄清对话效率。

Abstract: In this study, we propose an adaptive question generation mechanism that addresses uncertainty arising from ambiguous instructions. Natural-language instructions for service robots often contain ambiguities. In scenes with multiple similar objects, the robot must identify the target object by asking questions. However, existing question generation methods focus on probabilistic discrimination efficiency and do not sufficiently account for user ease of response. Asking questions without considering the user's ease of response may cause incorrect or uncertain answers. Such incorrect or uncertain answers lead to additional clarification turns and degraded dialogue efficiency. The proposed mechanism integrates probabilistic discrimination efficiency and human cognitive characteristics to generate questions that users can intuitively and easily answer. First, the proposed mechanism extracts visual attributes of candidate objects using a vision-language model (VLM). Next, the proposed mechanism computes the information gain (IG) of each attribute and selects the visual attribute (e.g., color, shape) that most reduces uncertainty in the candidate set. When multiple attributes have the same IG, the proposed mechanism applies a visual attribute filter based on cognitive characteristics to select the visual attribute that users can most intuitively perceive. The proposed mechanism then prompts the VLM with the selected attribute and the corresponding attribute values of each candidate object to generate a concise natural-language question. This process is repeated until the target object is uniquely identified. The proposed mechanism achieved a target identification success rate of 88.5% in the evaluation using a dataset of 120 scenes. Furthermore, the proposed mechanism received high ratings in subjective evaluations of response ease.

</details>


### [4] [Stress-Query and Temporal Reliability of Vision-Language Models as copilots in Autonomous UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23877)
*Hiago Sodre,Sebastian Barcelona,Vincent Sandin,Pablo Moraes,Andre S. Kelbouscas,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过系统化基准测试，评估视觉语言模型（VLM）规模、硬件约束与飞行可靠性之间的权衡，发现轻量模型产生结构性幻觉破坏确定性状态机，重量模型因推理延迟导致轨迹过度修正，中等规模架构可在不同硬件上取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将VLM引入无人机自主导航虽能带来高层语义推理能力，但会引入关键的时间瓶颈，可能破坏飞行控制的稳定性。现有工作缺乏对模型规模、硬件约束与飞行可靠性三者操作层面权衡的系统评估。

Method: 构建系统性基准测试框架，在高性能工作站与受限集成显卡笔记本两类硬件上对系统进行大量压力测试，将推理延迟的动力学影响与推理准确性进行解耦分析，并对比轻量、中等、重量三类模型架构的表现。

Result: 呈现明显的性能二分现象：轻量模型易产生结构性幻觉，破坏确定性状态机的运行；重量模型因推理滞后引发轨迹过度修正。中型架构在退化环境中成功恢复导航能力，且在不同硬件配置下均不损害实时稳定性，达到最优平衡。

Conclusion: VLM驱动的无人机导航不存在"越大越好"的单一最优解，模型规模需与硬件算力和控制实时性需求匹配；中等规模架构是兼顾语义推理质量与飞行稳定性的实用选择，所提出的基准方法可作为此类系统部署的评估参考。

Abstract: Integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into autonomous UAV navigation enables high-level semantic reasoning but introduces critical temporal bottlenecks that can destabilize flight control. This paper presents a systematic benchmark that evaluates the operational trade-offs among model scale, hardware constraints, and flight reliability. By subjecting the system to extensive stress tests on both a high-performance workstation and a constrained integrated-graphics laptop, we isolate the kinetic impact of inference latency against reasoning accuracy. Our findings reveal a performance dichotomy: lightweight models struggle with structural hallucinations that break deterministic state machines, while heavyweight models induce trajectory overcorrections due to inference lag. We demonstrate that a mid-weight architecture strikes the optimal balance, successfully rehabilitating navigation in degraded environments without compromising real-time stability across different hardware profiles.

</details>


### [5] [A Heterogeneous UAV UGV Methodology for Aerial Tracking of Mini Autonomous Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23878)
*Santiago Fernandez,Angel da Silva,Hiago Sodre,Pablo Moraes,Bruna Gutterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无人机—无人车异构融合方法，在ROS 2与Gazebo仿真中实现小型地面车辆的低空持续跟踪，视觉检测与UGV状态信息互补，在视觉中断时仍能维持目标估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定式外部感知基础设施缺失时，无人机空中跟踪自主地面车辆可提供态势感知；但纯视觉跟踪受相机视场、视角变化、遮挡及目标暂时不可见等限制，需要利用地面车辆的自身状态信息弥补视觉间隙。

Method: 基于ROS 2与Gazebo Sim搭建Tello类无人机（前置RGB相机）与多台LIMO类UGV的仿真系统；对比三种方案：免训练颜色标记基线、YOLO视觉基线与所提融合方法。融合方法采用与YOLO基线相同的视觉检测器，并将视觉检测结果与UGV状态信息结合，在视觉缺失时维持目标估计。评测覆盖单车跟踪、交叉轨迹、遮挡、无人机辅助感知四个场景，共600次仿真运行。

Result: 融合方法在全部场景中实现近乎连续的跟踪覆盖，定位误差低于YOLO基线。相对纯视觉YOLO方法，四场景RMSE分别降低37.3%、64.2%、62.6%和61.8%；在交叉场景与无人机辅助感知场景中分别实现10.20 s与9.07 s的冲突预警提前量。

Conclusion: 异构UAV—UGV融合可在视觉观测间歇时提升空中跟踪的连续性与鲁棒性，为缺乏固定感知设施环境下的态势感知提供可行方案。

Abstract: Aerial tracking of autonomous ground vehicles can provide useful situational awareness in environments where fixed external perception infrastructure is unavailable. However, visual-only tracking from an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) is limited by camera field of view, viewpoint changes, occlusions, and temporary loss of target visibility. This paper presents a heterogeneous UAV--UGV methodology for aerial tracking of small-scale autonomous ground vehicles in simulation. The system is implemented in ROS~2 and Gazebo Sim using a Tello-like UAV equipped with a front-facing RGB camera and multiple LIMO-like unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs). Three approaches are evaluated: a training-free color-marker baseline, a YOLO-based visual baseline, and a proposed UAV--UGV fusion method. The proposed method uses the same visual detector as the YOLO baseline, but combines visual detections with UGV state information to maintain the target estimate during visual gaps. The methods are evaluated across four scenarios: single-car tracking, crossing trajectories, occlusion, and UAV-assisted awareness. Results over 600 simulation runs show that the proposed fusion method achieves nearly continuous track coverage across all scenarios while reducing localization error relative to the YOLO baseline. Compared with the visual-only YOLO approach, the fusion method reduces RMSE by 37.3\%, 64.2\%, 62.6\%, and 61.8\% in the four evaluated scenarios, respectively. The method also enables conflict-awareness lead times of 10.20~s in the crossing scenario and 9.07~s in the UAV-assisted awareness scenario. These results indicate that heterogeneous UAV--UGV fusion can improve aerial tracking continuity and robustness when visual observations are intermittent.

</details>


### [6] [Adaptive Rejection of Unknown Hybrid Sinusoidal Disturbances with Application to Humanoid Walking under Unknown Ground Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23879)
*I-Chia Chang,Jacob Stewart,Petros A. Ioannou,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将足式机器人在非惯性环境中的行走控制建模为受混合正弦扰动（离散事件处相位跳变的正弦分量线性组合）的混合线性系统未知扰动抑制问题，利用Youla-Kucera参数化设计输出反馈控制器并配自适应律，在双足地面运动实验中跟踪精度提升超60%（仿真）和最高47%（实物）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人控制在非惯性环境中尚不实用：地面与周围环境相对惯性系加速，带来未知、时变、持续的扰动，并与腿式机器人的混合动力学耦合，使控制困难。

Method: （1）将机器人行走控制表述为受混合正弦扰动的混合线性系统的未知扰动抑制问题，推导问题可解性的充要条件并刻画能抑制扰动的控制输入；（2）借助Youla-Kucera参数化给出可抑制扰动的输出反馈控制器；（3）在扰动及其参数未知的实际情形下，提出自适应律在线估计可抑制扰动的控制器参数。

Result: 在双足机器人在运动地面行走的实验中，机身位置跟踪精度显著提升，仿真平均提升超过60%，实物实验最高提升47%。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合扰动抑制与自适应输出反馈框架能有效应对非惯性环境中的未知时变扰动，提升双足行走的机身位置跟踪性能，为足式机器人在非惯性环境中的实用化提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Legged robot control is not yet practical in non-inertial environments, where the ground and surroundings accelerate in the inertial Earth frame, mainly because of the unknown, time-varying, persistent perturbations caused by environmental motion, compounded by the associated hybrid robotic dynamics. We formulate the problem of robot walking control as unknown-disturbance rejection for hybrid linear systems subject to hybrid sinusoidal disturbances (i.e., a linear combination of sinusoidal components with instantaneous phase jumps at discrete events). First, necessary and sufficient conditions for solvability of the disturbance rejection problem are derived and used to characterize the control input that rejects the disturbances. Second, with the Youla-Kucera parametrization, an output feedback controller capable of rejecting the disturbance is introduced. Third, an adaptive law is proposed to estimate the controller parameters that reject the disturbance in the practical case where the disturbance and its parameters are unknown. Experiments on bipedal walking during ground motion demonstrate substantially improved body-position tracking accuracy, with an average improvement of over 60% in simulations and up to 47% in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [7] [AutoPruner: Agentic Depth Pruning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23880)
*Tao Lin,Yikeng Chen,Qiwei Liang,Zinan Xie,Zhengtu Liang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AutoPruner，一个由LLM智能体驱动的闭环实验框架，联合搜索VLA模型的层剪枝结构与性能恢复配方，在保持90%以上任务成功率的同时削减16.7–87.5%骨干层，实现最高2.50×推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层剪枝可降低视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型规模与推理成本，但可部署配置的选择需要同时决定删除哪些层以及如何恢复性能；这两个决策依赖具体模型与任务，且一次失败的试验难以区分是删除集合不当还是恢复不足，因此需要联合搜索。

Method: AutoPruner采用智能体驱动的闭环实验框架：先通过离线层干预测量动作输出变化，提供功能性证据；再由LLM智能体结合功能证据、评估历史和保留的诊断信息，在搜索预算内提出删除集合与恢复配方；每个提案在目标任务上执行并评估，反馈用于指导下一轮实验。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT、StarVLA、FastWAM三个模型与四个LIBERO套件上，AutoPruner在保持任务成功率高于90%的同时删除16.7–87.5%的骨干层，实测推理加速最高达2.50×。进一步分析表明，功能探针能在表示相似度很高的情况下识别出对动作敏感的层，且恢复策略可使原本失败的剪枝结构变得可行。

Conclusion: 联合搜索剪枝结构与恢复配方优于单独决策，基于动作输出的功能探针是判断层重要性的有效依据，恢复环节对剪枝可行性具有关键作用。

Abstract: Layer pruning reduces the size and inference cost of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but selecting a deployable configuration requires deciding both which layers to remove and how to recover task performance. These decisions depend on the model and task, and a failed trial can reflect either the deletion set or insufficient recovery. We present AutoPruner, an agent-driven framework that jointly searches pruning structures and recovery recipes through closed-loop experimentation. Offline layer interventions measure changes in action output to provide functional evidence. An LLM agent combines this evidence with evaluation history and retained diagnoses to propose deletion sets and recovery recipes within a search budget. Each proposal is executed and evaluated on the target tasks, and the resulting feedback guides the next experiment. Across OpenVLA-OFT, StarVLA, and FastWAM on four LIBERO suites, AutoPruner removes 16.7--87.5% of backbone layers while maintaining task success above 90%, with measured inference speedups of up to 2.50$\times$. Further analysis shows that functional probes identify action-sensitive layers despite high representation similarity, and that recovery can make previously failing structures viable.

</details>


### [8] [Learning Whole-Body Kinetic Coordination for Wheeled-Quadruped Long-Range Throwing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23883)
*Qinglian Wu,Yidian Hu,Weiqi Fan,Yutian Zhang,Siyuan Ma,Jiaqi Zhai,Wei Dong,Li Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文面向轮足式移动机械臂，提出"动力学链建模+基于SQP的轨迹优化+参考引导强化学习全身协调"的框架，实现非结构化地形上的远距离抛掷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在配送与取回任务中需要远距离抛掷能力，但这要求高出手速度、全身协调运动以及对地形扰动的鲁棒性，单一机械臂运动难以满足。

Method: 先将机械臂简化为动力学链模型，在运动学与动力学约束下用SQP优化生成高速抛掷参考轨迹；再以该参考引导强化学习策略，协调轮足平台基座运动实现全身发力。

Result: 静止基座下优化臂轨迹出手速度达12.75 m/s、抛掷距离16.68 m；启用全身协调后提升至16.64 m/s与28.25 m。并在平地、草地、斜坡、崎岖路面完成真机验证。

Conclusion: 动力学链优化与全身协调相结合，可在多种非结构化地形上实现鲁棒的远距离抛掷，验证了轮足机械臂平台的抛掷作业潜力。

Abstract: Long-range throwing on unstructured terrain is a key capability for mobile robots in delivery and retrieval tasks, but it requires high release velocity, coordinated whole-body motion, and robustness to terrain-induced disturbances. We present a whole-body kinetic-coordination framework for a wheeled-quadruped manipulator that combines kinetic-chain arm modeling with SQP-based trajectory optimization and a reference-guided reinforcement learning policy for coordinated base motion. The manipulator is first reduced to a kinetic-chain model and optimized under kinematic and dynamic constraints to produce a high-speed throwing reference. On a stationary base, the optimized arm trajectory reaches a release speed of 12.75 m/s and a throwing distance of 16.68 m. When whole-body coordination is enabled, the system further increases the release speed to 16.64 m/s and the throwing distance to 28.25 m. We then integrate the mobility of the wheeled-quadruped platform and validate the complete policy through hardware experiments on flat ground, grass, slopes, and rough roads. The results show that kinetic-chain optimization and whole-body coordination together enable robust long-range throwing across diverse unstructured terrains.

</details>


### [9] [PrismPlan: A Shared GPU Substrate for Motion Planning Across Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23884)
*Hong Wu,ChaoXuan Liu,ShuJie Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrismPlan 将批处理碰撞检测与正向运动学构建为共享的 GPU 底层原语，为采样、搜索、优化三类规划器提供统一后端，从而在相同硬件代价下首次对三种范式进行公平对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GPU 加速运动规划器各自围绕单一范式构建，跨库比较时实际上也在比较不同的碰撞检测器，导致采样、搜索、优化三类范式之间无法进行同条件对比。

Method: 识别出三类范式共同的 GPU 瓶颈——批处理碰撞检测及其底层的批处理正向运动学，并将其一次性实现为按球体划分的碰撞-运动学设备代码共享底座，所有规划器调用同一设备代码。在 MotionBenchMaker 的 1800 个问题、三种机器人（7–14 DoF，含双臂全身复合体）上以 2s 等预算运行六个规划器。

Result: 在 7-DoF Panda 上，采样法（pRRTC）以 98% 成功率最可靠，并行搜索（wPA*SE）相差约 4 个百分点但中位延迟低 3–4 倍，局部优化器从头求解时明显落后；在 14-DoF 双臂复合体上，采样法仍能求解约 90% 的可行问题，而搜索与优化规划器均不足三分之一。

Conclusion: 通过固定共享的 GPU 底层原语，可实现范式间的同条件对比；实验表明采样类方法在高自由度与复合机器人问题上可靠性优势显著，搜索法在延迟上更具竞争力，而局部优化器难以从零求解。代码已开源。

Abstract: GPU acceleration has made motion planning one to two orders of magnitude faster, but it has not made the three paradigms---sampling, search, and optimization---comparable to one another: every GPU-accelerated planner is built around a single paradigm, so comparing across libraries also compares their collision checkers. All three, however, bottleneck on the same GPU primitive: batched collision detection and the batched forward kinematics beneath it. PrismPlan builds that primitive once, as a shared substrate of per-sphere collision--kinematics device code that every planner consumes. Because they all execute the same device code, the backend is held fixed by construction and the paradigms can be compared head-to-head. We run six planners over 1800 MotionBenchMaker problems on three robots (7--14 DoF, one a dual-arm whole-body composite) at an equal 2s budget. On the 7-DoF Panda, sampling (pRRTC) is the most reliable paradigm at 98%, while parallel search (wPA*SE) follows within four points at a 3-4 times$ lower median latency, and the local optimizers trail well behind when made to solve from scratch. On the 14-DoF dual-arm composite, sampling still solves about 90% of feasible problems, while no search or optimization planner clears a third of them. Our open-source code is available at https://github.com/XiaoyubotMotionTeam/PrismPlan.

</details>


### [10] [From Likelihood to Utility: Quality-Axis Diffusion for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23885)
*Junjie Yang,Ziyi Shi,Shuqi Shen,Xinhu Zheng,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 QualiDrive，将扩散过程沿“驾驶质量轴”而非高斯噪声轴构建，通过按行驶效用排序轨迹构造场景相关的质量阶梯，并学习从低质量到高质量的有序精炼，在 NAVSIM 上取得 94.5 PDMS（v1）与 92.74 EPDMS（v2）的 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶扩散规划器沿高斯噪声轴组织轨迹生成，驾驶效用仅作为辅助引导或筛选信号，未被纳入扩散过程的组织核心，导致生成过程与驾驶质量脱钩。

Method: 构造场景特定的质量阶梯：按可测行驶效用对观测轨迹排序；学习逆转该有序退化过程，实现从低质量到高质量的有序精炼。每个逆向步骤以场景、自车状态、当前轨迹与目标质量等级为条件，逐步将轨迹精炼至更高质量。

Result: NAVSIM v1 上 PDMS 达 94.5，v2 上 EPDMS 达 92.74（SOTA）。相较仅端点质量监督，Oracle EPDMS 仅小幅提升（98.52→98.57），但 EPDMS 高于 0.95/0.98 的候选比例由 12.94%/9.71% 提升至 21.53%/15.93%，最终选中 EPDMS 由 92.63 升至 92.74。

Conclusion: 扩散不仅是去噪过程，更是按质量有序进行的渐进式精炼过程；以质量轴组织扩散可有效提升候选轨迹整体质量分布与最终选择性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving, yet existing planners organize trajectory generation along the Gaussian-noise axis, treating driving utility only as an auxiliary guidance or selection signal. We instead formulate diffusion along a driving-quality axis. We propose QualiDrive, which constructs scene-specific quality ladders by ranking observed trajectories according to measured driving utility and learns to reverse this ordered degradation through ordered low-to-high-quality refinement. Each reverse step is conditioned on the scene, ego state, current trajectory, and target quality level, progressively refining trajectories toward higher driving quality. On NAVSIM, QualiDrive achieves 94.5 PDMS on v1 and a state-of-the-art 92.74 EPDMS on v2. QualiDrive improves Oracle EPDMS only modestly over endpoint-only quality supervision (98.52 to 98.57), but substantially increases the fractions of candidates above 0.95 and 0.98 EPDMS from 12.94%/9.71% to 21.53%/15.93%, raising selected EPDMS from 92.63 to 92.74. These results suggest that diffusion is not merely a process of denoising, but one of progressive quality-ordered refinement.

</details>


### [11] [EvoAD: Self-Evolving Autonomous Driving via Latent World-Model Guided Post-hoc Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23886)
*Junjie Yang,Qiming Zhang,Yuyan Li,Shuqi Shen,Yusong Huang,Yanggang Sheng,Wenpeng Xu,Ziyi Shi,Tianshun Li,Yijie Chen,Yujia Wang,Jun Ma,Xinhu Zheng,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EvoAD，一个通过潜世界模型引导的事后反思实现部署后自演化的自动驾驶框架，无需重训练基座策略即可持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶系统一旦部署便基本"冻结"：相似场景重复相似决策，失败只能靠昂贵的数据采集与重训练来修正，与人类驾驶员通过经验、记忆和反思终身学习形成鲜明对比。作者希望探索自动驾驶系统能否实现类似人类的部署后自演化。

Method: EvoAD 将驾驶经验存入鸟瞰图（BEV）特征记忆池，按几何与语义相似度检索场景相关先例；再用潜世界模型在面向规划的紧凑空间中评估候选轨迹的未来后果；把已执行的驾驶结果转化为可复用的改进信号，而非将记忆作为外部文本上下文、也不仅把世界模型用于动作前仿真，从而诊断以往轨迹如何改进并在相似未来场景中优化行为，且不重训练基础驾驶策略。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 与 v2 上，随着反思轮次增加取得一致的性能提升，并达到当前最优（state-of-the-art）水平。

Conclusion: 结果表明世界模型可作为自动驾驶部署后自演化的反思机制，使系统在无需重训练的情况下持续从自身经验中学习改进。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems have achieved rapid progress by learning unified policies that map sensor observations directly to driving trajectories. However, once deployed, these systems typically remain frozen: they may repeat similar decisions in similar scenes, and failures can only be addressed through costly data collection and retraining. This sharply contrasts with human drivers, who improve lifelong through experience, memory, and reflection. In this work, we ask whether autonomous driving systems can achieve similar post-deployment self-evolution to human drivers. We propose EvoAD, a self-evolving autonomous driving framework via latent world-model guided post-hoc reflection. EvoAD stores driving experiences in a bird's-eye-view feature memory pool, retrieves scene-relevant precedents according to geometric and semantic similarity, and uses a latent world model to evaluate the future consequences of candidate trajectories in a compact planning-oriented space. Instead of treating memory as external textual contexts or using world models only for pre-action simulation, EvoAD transforms executed driving outcomes into reusable improvement signals for future decisions. This enables the system to diagnose how prior trajectories could have been improved and to refine behavior in similar future scenarios without retraining the base driving policy. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 show consistent improvements across reflection rounds and state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the potential of world models as reflective mechanisms for post-deployment self-evolution in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [12] [ContactKernels: Adaptive Proposals using Geometry-COnditioned Wrenches for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23887)
*Aditya Kamireddypalli,Matías Mattamala,João Moura,Sethu Vijayakumar,Steve Tonneau,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ContactKernels：一种接触自适应提议核，用几何条件化的力/力矩测量估计接触位置与局部接触几何，并据此调整采样分布（位置偏置搜索方向、法向塑造采样协方差），与Predictive Sampling和MPPI结合，在仿真与真机上显著提升插入成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的插入任务需在感知、几何与控制不确定性下实现精确几何对齐。当不确定性导致意外接触时，接触本身提供了可指导纠正运动的局部信息。基于采样的模型预测控制（SMPC）无需对接触动力学求梯度，适合该场景，但标准SMPC的提议分布是通用的，没有利用观测到的接触来决定下一步搜索方向。

Method: 提出ContactKernels，一个接触自适应提议核：利用几何条件化的wrench（力/力矩）测量估计接触点位置与局部接触几何（如接触法向），再将估计的接触状态直接用于调整SMPC的提议分布——接触位置偏置搜索以便生成纠正性动作，接触法向塑造采样协方差。分别与Predictive Sampling（PS）和Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）控制结合，在多种插入几何的仿真环境和真实机器人上评估。

Result: 仿真中，ContactKernels在两种控制器上均将平均插入成功率提升超过50个百分点；在真实机器人上，插入成功率从53.3%提升至90.0%，平均插入时间缩短33%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，接触信息不仅能作为反馈信号，还可以通过直接决定控制器在何处搜索采样来改善基于采样的控制，为接触丰富操作中的不确定性处理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Contact-rich insertion requires precise geometric alignment despite uncertainty in perception, geometry, and con- trol. When this uncertainty causes unintended contact, the re- sulting contact provides local information that can guide correc- tive motion. Sampling-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) is well suited to this setting because it optimizes through forward simulation without requiring gradients through contact dynamics. However, standard SMPC methods typically use generic proposal distributions that do not exploit observed contact to determine where to search next. We introduce ContactKernels, a contact-adaptive proposal kernel that uses geometry-conditioned wrench measurements to estimate contact location and local contact geometry. The estimated contact state directly adapts the proposal distribution: contact location biases the search toward corrective motions, while the contact normal shapes the sampling covariance. We evaluate ContactKernels with Predictive Sampling (PS) and Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control across multiple insertion geometries in simulation and on a real robot. In simulation, ContactKernels improves mean insertion success by over 50 % points across both controllers. On the real robot, it increases insertion success from 53.3 % to 90.0 % and reduces mean time to insertion by 33 %. These results show that contact information can improve sampling-based control not only as feedback, but also by directly determining where the controller searches

</details>


### [13] [MoDex: Towards Universal Multi-Object Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23888)
*Jing Xiong,Jiale Xie,Haoqi Yuan,Chaoyi Xu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MoDex，一个数据高效框架，首个统一解决多种空间布局下通用多物体灵巧抓取的方案：将以参考轨迹的残差修正为核心的单步决策问题建模，并引入空间自适应双策略（近邻物体一次性抓取、分离物体顺序交互）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体抓取是基础但困难的问题，受限于数据采集成本高、空间布局多变、需要广泛泛化能力。

Method: 将任务建模为学习对参考轨迹的残差修正的单步决策问题；引入空间自适应双策略：对近邻物体执行单次抓取，对分离物体执行顺序交互；并训练基于视觉的策略（仅用仿真数据）。

Result: 在近邻配置下显著优于基线，对未见过物体成功率达 61.24%；在分离布局中取得高成功率；实验还显示跨机器人本体的泛化性、可扩展性，以及仅用仿真数据训练的视觉策略实现零样本 sim-to-real 部署。

Conclusion: MoDex 提供了统一、数据高效的多物体灵巧抓取方案，兼顾不同空间布局，并具备泛化与真实部署能力；代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Multi-object grasping is a fundamental yet challenging problem, constrained by data collection costs, varying spatial layouts, and the need for broad generalization. To address these challenges, we propose MoDex, a data-efficient framework that provides the first unified solution for universal multi-object dexterous grasping across diverse spatial layouts. MoDex accomplishes this by formulating the task as a single-step decision problem of learning residual corrections to a reference trajectory, and introducing a spatially adaptive dual-strategy that performs single-shot grasping for proximal objects and sequential interaction for separated objects. Extensive evaluations show that MoDex significantly outperforms baselines in proximal configurations, achieving 61.24% success on unseen objects, and achieves high success rates in separated layouts. Further experiments demonstrate its generalization across robot embodiments, scalability and zero-shot sim-to-real deployment through a vision-based policy trained solely on simulation data. Code and pretrained models are available at https://github.com/Aurora-Xiong/MoDex.

</details>


### [14] [When to Make a Decision: Task-Sufficient Active Perception for Mobile Manipulator Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23891)
*Mariana da Silva Barros,Matthias Kerzel,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合神经-符号框架，用于未知环境中的"任务充分主动感知"，使机器人能够判断何时信息已足够并停止感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未知环境中执行任务前需收集足够信息，但"信息何时充分"缺乏明确判据；现有方法多关注"如何"获取信息，缺少"何时停止"的决策机制。

Method: 框架先解析任务并生成初始计划，随后迭代重评估：每轮可选择获取更优视角、检查/操作候选物体、向用户请求澄清或直接执行任务。神经感知模块维护以物体为中心的信念状态，符号决策层则判断剩余不确定性是否值得继续收集信息。将在由人形躯干与移动底盘组成的移动机械臂上实现，并在仿真中做补充评估。

Result: 摘要未给出实验结果，仅说明将进行实机部署与仿真评估的设想。

Conclusion: 该框架旨在让机器人不仅知道如何收集额外信息，还能判断何时不再需要进一步感知，从而实现任务充分的主动感知。

Abstract: A robotic agent operating in an unknown environment must gather sufficient task-relevant information before acting. However, determining when the available information is sufficient remains a challenge. We propose a conceptual hybrid neuro-symbolic framework for task-sufficient active perception in unknown environments. After interpreting a requested task, the agent generates an initial plan and iteratively reevaluates it as new information becomes available. At each iteration, it can acquire a more informative viewpoint, inspect or manipulate candidate objects, ask the user for clarification, or perform the task. Neural perception modules update an object-centric belief state, while a symbolic decision layer determines whether the remaining uncertainty justifies further information gathering. The framework will be implemented on a mobile manipulator comprising a humanoid torso and a mobile base, with complementary evaluation in simulation. The proposed approach aims to enable robots to determine not only how to gather additional information, but also when further perception is no longer necessary.

</details>


### [15] [Language-Forcing: Advancing Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Semantic Alignment of Whole-Body World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23894)
*Yanjun Li,Jiarui Ye,Weiyu Guo,Zixiao He,Kangjie Ye,Dihan Wang,Yishuo Cheng,Zishang Xiang,Mingju Gao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LANGUAGE-FORCING 训练策略，用语言引导全身世界动作模型（WAM）的未来视觉表征聚焦任务相关变化，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）联合预测动作与未来观测，但其视觉预测目标没有显式区分任务相关的变化（身体姿态、接触、物体状态）与无关的场景变化，而这对全身策略的任务进展判断至关重要。

Method: 给定当前帧与真实短时未来帧，用冻结的视觉语言模型评估任务进展并预测合理的后续状态变化，得到语言描述；用冻结文本编码器编码，并与中间层的、动作条件下的未来视觉特征对齐，作为原视觉与动作预测目标的补充。所有辅助组件仅在训练时使用，推理零额外开销。

Result: HumanoidArena 上平均成功率 79.17%，比最强对比策略高 9.46 个百分点，比无语言对齐的同款 WAM 高 10.77 个百分点；在五个真实世界任务上，平均成功率从 53.33% 提升到 71.33%。

Conclusion: 用语言作为任务相关性的监督信号来对齐未来视觉表征，是一种无需推理成本的通用增强手段，可有效提升全身世界动作模型的控制性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict robot actions and future observations. However, their visual prediction objectives do not explicitly distinguish task-relevant transitions from incidental scene changes. For whole-body policies, this distinction matters because task progress depends on coordinated changes in body pose, contact, and object state. We introduce LANGUAGE-FORCING, a training strategy that uses language to emphasize task-relevant changes in the future visual representations of a whole-body WAM. Given paired current and ground- truth short-horizon future frames, a frozen vision-language model assesses task progress and predicts plausible subsequent state changes. We encode these descriptions with a frozen text encoder and align their embeddings with intermediate, action-conditioned future-visual features, complementing the original visual and action prediction objectives. All auxiliary components are used only during training, introducing no additional inference overhead. On HumanoidArena, LANGUAGE-FORCING achieves an average success rate of 79.17%, exceeding the strongest competing policy by 9.46 percentage points and the same WAM without language alignment by 10.77 percentage points. Across five real-world tasks, language alignment raises average success from 53.33% to 71.33% over the same WAM baseline.

</details>


### [16] [InJePA: Intention-First Latent Planning with Coupled JEPA World Models for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23896)
*Siming Zhou,Bowen Ma,Ruiran Du,Pengcheng Zheng,Sheng Zheng,Dongshen Han,Zhijie Li,Wei Dong,Fan Mo,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出InJepa，一种以潜在意图为先的规划范式：目标条件JEPA生成潜在意图，动作条件JEPA持续预测未来潜在状态，二者在共享潜空间中对齐，使预测结果反馈进入下一步意图提议与动作推断，形成"意图—动作—后果"闭环。在Habitat MP3D闭环图像目标导航任务上验证了多步导航有效性及跨编码器适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航世界模型方法通常先完整生成候选动作，再预测后果用于选择或搜索更新；对每个候选而言，预测主要用于评价动作，而非逐步引导候选的构建过程。作者希望让世界模型从"评估者"转变为"构建引导者"。

Method: 1) 目标条件JEPA提出潜在意图（latent intention）；2) 动作条件JEPA在共享潜空间中持续预测未来潜在状态；3) 两个JEPA在同一潜空间中对齐其预测表示；4) 在每个候选内部，预测后果被回馈到下一次意图提议与动作推断中，形成意图—动作—后果闭环。

Result: 在Habitat MP3D上开展闭环图像目标导航评测，结果表明方法能通过意图引导的动作生成与后果反馈实现有效的多步导航；在多种视觉编码器上的实验进一步说明其可适用于不同视觉表示。

Conclusion: 潜在意图可以作为规划的中心对象；世界模型能够通过对意图与后果的协同推理，直接引导规划构建，而不仅限于事后评价。

Abstract: Visual navigation requires goal directed planning informed by predictions of environment dynamics. Representative navigation world model methods propose complete action candidates before predicting consequences for selection or search updates. For each candidate, predictions primarily evaluate proposed actions rather than guide its stepwise construction. To this end, we propose InJepa, an intention first planning paradigm centered on latent states. A goal conditioned JEPA proposes latent intentions, and an action conditioned JEPA continually predicts future latent states. Both operate in a shared latent space to align their predictive representations. Within each candidate, predicted consequences feed into the next intention proposal and action inference, forming a closed loop of intention, action, and consequence. This extends the world model's role beyond evaluation to leading candidate construction through latent intentions. We evaluate InJepa through closed loop image goal navigation in Habitat MP3D. The results show effective multistep navigation through intention guided action generation and consequence feedback. Experiments across encoders further support applicability across visual representations. These findings support latent intentions as the central planning object and show that world models can directly guide plan construction through coordinated reasoning about intentions and consequences. Code is available at https://github.com/zhuxun132/InJepa.

</details>


### [17] [MicroSplat: Towards Automating 3D Small-Scale Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23899)
*Roman Mineyev,Robert Azarcon,Suvrat Jain,Chuizheng Kong,Shreyas Kousik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MicroSplat 系统：用低成本消费级显微镜搭配机械臂，在单次物体定位后可无人值守地完成标定、无碰撞视角规划、每个视角的焦点堆栈采集与融合、跨视角物体分割，并以机械臂位姿热启动优化 3DGS，从而实现小物体的亚毫米级细节重建；同时发现用受控焦点扫描直接测量清晰度优于用去模糊方法推断清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路板缺陷检测、标本数字化与存档等任务需要小物体的亚毫米细节重建。3DGS 仅靠带位姿图像即可实现逼真重建，但宏观光学破坏了其两个前提：视场与景深缩小到毫米级，导致视点误差可能使样本出画，且每帧仅薄焦平面清晰。现有自动化宏观采集装置虽用逐视角焦点堆叠应对浅景深，却依赖传统摄影测量和离线、需要人工介入的流程。

Method: 在机械臂上搭载低成本消费级显微镜进行采集。流程为：先做一次物体定位，随后全自动执行相机/机械臂标定、规划无碰撞视点、在每个视点采集并融合焦点堆栈、在所有视角上分割物体，最后以机械臂自身位姿作为热启动优化 3DGS 模型。此外，将所提方法与模糊感知（blur-aware）3DGS 方法进行对比，后者从散焦图像中推断清晰场景。

Result: 实验表明，使用受控焦点扫描直接测量清晰度的方案优于从散焦图像推断清晰度的方法。作者同时提供真实与合成数据集（包含机器人及相机参数）以及拟合得到的 3DGS 重建结果。

Conclusion: MicroSplat 将消费级显微镜与机械臂结合，实现了低成本、无人值守的宏观小物体高细节 3DGS 重建，并通过"测量清晰度优于推断清晰度"的发现为浅景深条件下的宏观重建提供了有效方案，同时公开数据集与重建结果以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Reconstructing sub-millimeter detail of small objects underpins tasks such as circuit-board defect detection, specimen digitization, and archiving. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) makes photorealistic reconstruction possible from posed images alone, but macro optics violate two conditions it depends on. The field of view (FOV) and the depth of field (DoF) shrink to millimeters, so viewpoint error can take a sample out of frame, and only a thin focal plane is sharp in frame. Existing automated macro-capture rigs handle the shallow DoF with per-view focus stacking, but typically rely on classical photogrammetry and offline, human-in-the-loop processing. We present MicroSplat, which generates a 3DGS using a low-cost consumer microscope on a manipulator arm. After a single up-front object-localization step, the system runs unattended. It performs calibration, plans collision-free viewpoints, captures and fuses a focal stack at each viewpoint, segments the object across all views, and optimizes a 3DGS model warm-started with the arm's own poses. We further compare against blur-aware 3DGS methods, which infer sharp scenes from defocused images, and find that measuring sharpness with a controlled focal sweep outperforms inferring it. We provide real and synthetic datasets (including robot and camera parameters) and the fitted 3DGS reconstructions.

</details>


### [18] [UMI-WM: Interactive World Modeling for Bimanual Manipulation from Wrist Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23913)
*Jingxiang Guo,Haoyu Zhao,Xidong Zhang,Yuhang Zheng,Hao Wu,Chen Gao,Jianshu Zhou,Shuicheng Yan,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出UMI-WM：一个以双臂动作为条件的双臂世界模型，可从观测历史、各臂相对相机位姿和夹爪开合宽度联合生成左右手腕两路视频，兼顾跨视角一致性、动作跟随与视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMI数据（手腕相机视频+相机轨迹+夹爪开合）为学习双臂动作如何改变共享场景提供了可扩展、无需机器人的数据；但现有交互式世界模型多依赖文本或单相机控制，难以同时跟随两臂动作并保持两个手腕视角间的一致性。

Method: 将每臂动作注入各自的手腕视频流，仅在两流间通过稀疏hub token交换场景信息；使用相机感知的几何条件注入与相机引导的噪声实现逐视角运动控制与跨视角几何信息交换；将模型改造为块因果生成，并把30步Teacher蒸馏为4步Student；训练数据为自采集的开放世界与影棚UMI录制数据加公开操作数据；用逆动力学模型（IDM）从生成视频中恢复相对相机位姿与夹爪宽度，并提出双臂动作保真度（BAF）指标衡量恢复动作与条件动作的匹配度。

Result: 在全部五项参考视频保真度指标上取得最优；在VBench与WorldArena上达到最佳或具有竞争力；Teacher与Student在动作跟随方面均优于所有对比基线；4步Student生成成对腕部视频的速度比Teacher快6.9倍。

Conclusion: UMI-WM验证了以双臂动作为条件、双流+稀疏交互的双臂世界模型在跨视角一致性、动作可控性与视觉质量上的有效性，且经少步蒸馏后可在显著加速的同时保持动作跟随优势，为基于UMI数据的可扩展双臂世界建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) pairs wrist-camera videos with camera trajectories and gripper openings, providing scalable, robot-free data for learning how bimanual actions change a shared scene. Existing interactive world models, however, typically rely on text or single-camera control and struggle to follow each arm's actions while maintaining consistency between the two wrist views. We introduce UMI-WM, an action-conditioned bimanual world model that jointly generates both wrist views from observation history, per-arm relative camera poses, and gripper widths, with cross-view consistency, action following, and visual fidelity. UMI-WM injects each arm's actions into its own wrist stream, while sparse hub tokens exchange scene information between the two streams. Camera-aware geometry conditioning and camera-guided noise further support per-view motion control and cross-view geometric exchange. We adapt the model for block-causal generation and distill the 30-step Teacher into a Student with four denoising steps. We train UMI-WM on self-collected open-world and in-studio UMI recordings combined with public manipulation data. To evaluate action following, we use an inverse dynamics model (IDM) to recover relative camera poses and gripper widths from generated videos, and introduce Bimanual Action Fidelity (BAF) to measure how closely these recovered actions match the conditioning actions. UMI-WM achieves the best results on all five reference-video fidelity metrics and the best or competitive performance on VBench and WorldArena. The Teacher and Student outperform all compared baselines in action following, while the four-step Student generates paired wrist videos 6.9 times faster than the Teacher.

</details>


### [19] [ESCAPE: Egocentric Sensing for Collision Avoidance and Policy Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23943)
*Weiqing Shen,Wenhao Huang,Baokang Zhang,Shuo Shang,Yanmiao Hu,Haojun Li,Yifan Han,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ESCAPE——一个仅依赖本体（自中心）分布式深度传感器的在线感知-规划框架，通过聚合稀疏深度测量构建障碍感知点云，并用流匹配生成式策略输出无碰撞轨迹，纯仿真训练即可零样本迁移到真实硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的运动规划是机器人落地的瓶颈。现有视觉-运动策略主要依赖外部相机，存在感知盲区，并因遮挡、杂乱和标定约束而脆弱。作者希望摆脱对外部视角的依赖，用本体感知实现安全规划。

Method: 1）聚合分布式深度传感器的稀疏测量，转换为结构化的、障碍感知的点云表示；2）策略采用基于流匹配（flow matching）的生成式框架；3）构建含10万+安全关键轨迹的高质量数据集进行训练；4）联合优化到达目标与避障两个目标，直接从自中心输入生成安全轨迹。

Result: 完全在仿真中训练，无需任何真实世界微调即零样本迁移到实体硬件；在仿真与真实环境的大规模评测中，仅依靠自中心感知即可实现稳定的运动规划，性能优于外部视角基线和体素化规划基线。

Conclusion: ESCAPE表明自中心稀疏深度感知结合生成式流匹配策略足以支撑安全、可迁移的运动规划，为非结构化环境下的机器人部署提供了一条降低对外部相机与标定依赖的可行路径。

Abstract: Motion planning in unstructured environments is a bottleneck for robot deployment. Current visuomotor policies predominantly rely on external cameras, introducing perceptual blind spots and fragility due to occlusions, clutter, and calibration constraints. To overcome these limitations, we propose ESCAPE, an online egocentric sensing framework for collision-free trajectory generation. ESCAPE aggregates sparse measurements from distributed depth sensors, converting them into a structured, obstacle-aware point cloud representation. Our policy leverages a flow matching-based generative framework, and trains on a high-quality dataset comprising over 100K safety-critical trajectories. With joint optimization of goal-reaching and collision objectives, it produces safe trajectories directly from these egocentric inputs. Crucially, ESCAPE is trained entirely in simulation and transfers zero-shot to physical hardware without any real-world fine-tuning. Extensive simulation and real-world evaluations demonstrate that ESCAPE achieves stable motion planning using only egocentric perception, outperforming external-view and voxelized-planning baselines across both simulation and real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [20] [REAP: Learning General Rearrangement Heuristics from Motion-Planner Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23973)
*Daniel Swoboda,Hector Geffner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REAP 通过关系图神经网络在简单符号抽象上学习启发式，并用运动规划器在训练中"引出"隐藏的几何前置条件，从而在重排任务中显著减少运动规划查询并提升求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人重排任务既要满足高层符号规范，又要保证几何可行。符号抽象使组合结构可处理，但难以表达决定动作是否真正可执行的几何约束；这些约束难以直接写入符号模型。

Method: 提出 REAP 框架：在简化符号抽象上学习关系图神经网络启发式；训练时由运动规划器充当监督者，标定/引出问题的隐藏几何前置条件；部署时与贪心最佳优先搜索结合，并支持多领域联合训练。

Result: 在物体数量、规划长度和物体布局上实现分布外泛化；相比经典启发式搜索与基于学习的基线，求解问题更多，运动规划器查询量约少一个数量级；排序第一的动作在 94% 的采样状态中几何可行，随机选择仅为 22%。

Conclusion: 将符号抽象、图神经启发式与运动规划器监督相结合，可有效弥补符号模型对几何可行性的表达不足，为几何可行的重排规划提供高效且可泛化的方案。

Abstract: Robotic rearrangement tasks require planning sequences of actions that fulfill high-level specifications while also being geometrically feasible. Symbolic abstractions make the combinatorial structure of rearrangement tasks tractable, but they usually do not fully capture the geometric constraints that determine which actions a robot can actually realize. These constraints are hard to express in the symbolic model. We propose REAP (Rearrangement with Elicited Action Preconditions), a framework in which a relational graph neural network heuristic is learned over a simple symbolic abstraction. A motion planner serves as a supervisor that elicits the hidden geometric preconditions of the problem during training. Deployed with greedy best-first search, the learned heuristic generalizes out-of-distribution along object count, plan length, and object arrangement, and can be trained jointly on several domains. Compared to classical heuristic search and learning-based baselines, REAP solves more problems, requires roughly an order of magnitude fewer motion-planner queries than comparable methods, and the action it ranks first is geometrically feasible in 94% of sampled states, against 22% for a random choice.

</details>


### [21] [Weakly Supervised Discovery of Motion Styles for Compositional Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24008)
*Heedo Woo,Antonio Marino,Eduardo Sebastián,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CODES框架，从多样录制数据中自动发现连贯的风格库，并与独立任务指令组合，实现四足机器人的风格化运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自由度多于任务需求时，同一目标可由多种运动风格实现；组合式控制需在保留选定风格的同时执行独立任务指令，两者存在竞争，需有效平衡。

Method: 三步流程：1）运动重定向将录制动作映射为目标机器人可执行的运动；2）弱监督一致性编码与基于密度的聚类，在无需预设数量条件下识别单条及跨条录制中的风格；3）强化学习训练策略，将发现的风格与独立指令组合。

Result: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上、跨两个仿真器与真实硬件评估：相比基线显著提升运动学连贯性；控制器可跟踪超出录制数据支撑范围的速度指令，并在跨仿真器与真机部署中表现出稳健且可区分的风格特征。

Conclusion: CODES能以无监督方式发现风格库并实现风格与任务的解耦组合，具备良好的泛化性与实机可部署性。

Abstract: When a robot possesses more degrees of freedom than a task requires, multiple trajectories can achieve the same objective using distinct motion styles. Compositional robot control aims to preserve a selected style while simultaneously executing an independent task command, effectively balancing these potentially competing objectives. In this paper, we present CODES, a framework for compositional robot control that discovers a coherent style repertoire from diverse recordings and composes it with independent commands. First, motion retargeting maps the recordings to motions executable by the target robot; next, weakly supervised consensus encoding and density-based clustering identify styles within and across recordings without a prescribed count; finally, reinforcement learning trains a policy to compose the discovered styles with independent commands. We evaluate CODES on a Unitree Go2 quadruped across two simulators and physical hardware. Compared to baselines, CODES significantly improves kinematic coherence. Furthermore, our controller successfully tracks velocity commands that generalize beyond the support of the recorded data, exhibiting robust and distinguishable style characteristics during both cross-simulator and real-world hardware deployments.
  Project website: https://www.proroklab.org/compositional-motion-styles/

</details>


### [22] [ChairNav: Cross-Embodiment Pre-training and Personalization for Long-Horizon Wheelchair Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24012)
*Junhua Huang,Zhizheng Liu,Honglin He,Xiang Zhang,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ChairNav框架，把通用视觉导航基础模型通过"三阶段数据金字塔"适配到个人电动轮椅的户外人行道自主导航，在低成本RGB-D+Jetson硬件上实现93.4%的自主驾驶时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外人行道长距离自主导航对无障碍个人出行至关重要，但现有方法要么局限于室内基于地图的导航，要么依赖昂贵复杂的传感器堆栈实现全自主，缺乏兼顾泛化性与轻量硬件的方案。

Method: 基于视觉导航基础模型与大规模导航数据集，构建三阶段数据金字塔：1）在大规模跨本体数据集（转换为轮椅域）上预训练；2）用少量轮椅演示做中训练以消除剩余域差距；3）后训练阶段利用用户干预数据对部署模型进行个性化微调。系统部署于配备RGB-D相机与NVIDIA Jetson板载计算模块的商用电动轮椅。

Result: 各阶段均有效：预训练使横向ADE降低18%；后训练将用户干预的平均间隔距离从29.2 m提升至57.4 m；在千米级城市人行道路线上的评估显示系统在93.4%的总驾驶时间内自主运行。

Conclusion: 利用视觉导航基础模型与分层数据策略，可在轻量硬件上实现具备强泛化能力的户外人行道轮椅自主导航，展示了其服务日常无障碍出行的潜力。

Abstract: Developing autonomous electric wheelchairs capable of long-horizon sidewalk navigation is critical for accessible personal mobility, yet existing approaches either focus on indoor map-based navigation or rely on expensive, complex sensor stacks to achieve full autonomy. In this work, we propose to tackle outdoor sidewalk navigation by leveraging visual navigation foundation models and large-scale navigation datasets, which offer strong generalization using lightweight hardware. To this end, we present ChairNav, a novel framework that adapts a general visual navigation model into a personalized electric wheelchair navigation system using a three-stage data pyramid. To address the scarcity of in-domain data, ChairNav first pre-trains on large-scale cross-embodiment datasets transformed into the wheelchair domain, followed by mid-training on a small number of wheelchair demonstrations to eliminate remaining domain gaps. Finally, a post-training stage refines the deployed model from user interventions to personalize its behavior. We deploy ChairNav on a commercial electric wheelchair equipped with an RGB-D camera and an onboard NVIDIA Jetson compute module. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of each stage in our data pyramid: pre-training reduces lateral ADE by 18%, and post-training increases the mean distance between user interventions from 29.2 m to 57.4 m. Further evaluation on kilometer-scale urban sidewalk routes shows that our system operates autonomously for 93.4% of total driving time, demonstrating its potential for everyday accessible mobility. Demo video and more details are at https://vail.cs.ucla.edu/ChairNav/.

</details>


### [23] [CLARO: Clutter Aware Robot Orchestrator for Open-Vocabulary Grasping using VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24016)
*Sungsu Kim,Vineet Bhat,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Ramesh Karri,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CLARO，一个基于 VLM 的智能体，编排开放词汇检测、分割、6-DoF 抓取与非抓取推挤等技能，在杂乱环境中定位并取出语言指定的目标物体；仿真平均成功率 89.1%，实机 88.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实家庭、仓库、实验室环境高度杂乱且多样，机器人需在多步规划、执行与重规划之间循环；单一抓取策略难以应对遮挡与不可达目标，因此需要能调度多种感知与操作技能、并具备长程记忆的通用编排方案。

Method: CLARO 以 VLM 智能体为核心，组合四类技能：开放词汇目标检测、分割、6-DoF 抓取、非抓取推挤（用于暴露被遮挡目标）；维护历史动作记忆以支撑长程执行；输出抓取位姿/3D 路点等操作目标，从而实现跨机械臂迁移且无需任务特定训练。评估在动态仿真环境与标准图像基准上进行，并与多种方法对比；进而沿 grounding 与 grasping 两个维度做细粒度分析；最后在真实机械臂上部署验证 sim2real。

Result: 仿真中平均成功率 89.1%，在高杂乱场景相对最强基线提升超过 16%；对物体类别、光照与杂乱程度表现出较强泛化；实机部署成功率 88.8%，显示良好的仿真到现实迁移能力；grounding/grasping 分解分析刻画了各方法的成败来源。

Conclusion: 以 VLM 智能体编排通用感知与操作技能、并配合动作记忆与位姿级输出，是在杂乱环境中实现语言指令目标检索的有效且可迁移的路径，且具备较强 sim2real 泛化性；分解式评估有助于定位方法瓶颈。

Abstract: Robots that follow natural-language instructions unlock a wide range of autonomous applications in households, warehouses, and labs. Real-world environments are diverse and often heavily cluttered, requiring robots to plan over multiple steps, act, and replan as they work toward a goal. We present CLARO (CLutter-Aware Robot Orchestrator), a VLM agent that orchestrates a set of perception and manipulation skills: open-vocabulary object detection, segmentation, 6-DoF grasping, and non-prehensile pushing to locate and retrieve a language-specified target in clutter. CLARO maintains a memory of its past actions to support long-horizon execution, and emits manipulation targets (grasp poses/3D waypoints) allowing transfer across robot arms without task-specific training. We evaluate CLARO against a range of methods on a dynamic simulation environment and standard image-based benchmarks, demonstrating strong generalization across object classes, lighting, and clutter levels. CLARO attains an average success rate of 89.1%, a relative improvement of over 16% on the strongest competing method in high cluttered scenes. We further analyze performance along grounding and grasping axes to characterize where each method succeeds and fails. Finally, we deploy CLARO on a robot arm across diverse cluttered scenes, achieving a success rate of 88.8%, and demonstrating strong sim2real generalizability.

</details>


### [24] [Switch-Time-Aware Hybrid Model Predictive Control Using the Hybrid Minimum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24125)
*Valentin Gaucher,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种时间感知混合MPC框架，将事件切换时刻提升为显式优化变量，与连续控制输入联合优化，单步仅需求解一个稀疏QP即可实时获得混合敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混合MPC依赖固定时间网格，切换时刻被离散化锁定，导致着陆慢、控制能耗高、出现碰撞后回弹，且通过saltation矩阵计算混合敏感度较为繁琐。

Method: 构建含切换时刻的决策向量，得到光滑非线性规划，其KKT条件与HMP的事件时刻平稳性条件一致，从而通过KKT系统隐式恢复一阶混合敏感度；每个MPC步求解单个稀疏QP，实时联合优化控制输入与碰撞时刻。

Result: 在弹跳质量块落于振荡平台的算例中，附着时间缩短一半，累计控制能量降低30%，消除碰撞后回弹，单周期求解时间增加15%；累计能量与离线时间感知求解器相差4%以内，每周期2.4 ms，对比互补性建模的281 ms，其中固定求解器下10.6倍加速来自transcription本身。

Conclusion: 将事件时刻作为显式优化变量并借KKT系统隐式处理混合敏感度，可在单QP框架下实现高效、低能耗、无回弹的实时混合MPC，性能接近离线时间感知方法而计算代价显著降低。

Abstract: This article presents a time-aware hybrid model predictive control (MPC) framework that simultaneously optimizes the objective with respect to both continuous control inputs and event switching times. Rather than relying on a fixed time grid, the switching instant is lifted into the decision vector and treated as an explicit optimization variable. The resulting transcription is a smooth nonlinear program whose Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions are consistent with the Hybrid Minimum Principle (HMP) event-time stationarity condition, so first-order hybrid sensitivities are recovered implicitly through the KKT system rather than by evaluating saltation matrices. Each MPC step solves a single sparse Quadratic Program (QP), jointly optimizing control inputs and impact timing in real time. On a bouncing mass landing on an oscillating platform, the method attaches in half the time of fixed-grid saltation MPC, reduces cumulative control effort by 30%, and removes the post-impact rebounds, at a 15% increase in per-cycle solve time. It matches the cumulative effort of offline time-aware solvers to within 4% while running at 2.4 ms per cycle against 281 ms for a complementarity-based formulation of the same problem, of which a 10.6x reduction is attributable to the transcription alone at a fixed solver.

</details>


### [25] [Rollbot 2: Agile Locomotion by a Single-Actuator Spherical Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24129)
*Jingxian Wang*,Chen Yu*,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rollbot 2 保留单执行器球形机器人设计，通过重新设计质量分布实现近直线滚动，结合机载惯性/UWB 传感与学习控制，实现无动捕的航点跟随和信标寻的，速度较原版提升 70 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少执行器数量可简化机器人硬件，但会失去独立的操控输入。原版 Rollbot 依赖调制准稳定螺旋轨道转向，速度慢且依赖外部动作捕捉系统，因此需要在不增加执行器的前提下提升机动性与自主性。

Method: 重新设计质量分布，使单执行器机器人能近直线滚动并更直接地朝目标行进；搭载机载惯性传感与超宽带（UWB）传感；用单一电机速度指令配合学习型控制实现转向、信标寻的与航点跟随。

Result: 无需动捕即可自主接近 UWB 信标；完成 468 m 室内手动指令路线，速度 0.87 m/s；在 4.8 m "N" 形航点基准上 16 次试验全部完成，平均通过时间 10.8 s，平均时间平均偏差 7 cm，最快 8.0 s，比原版 564 s 快 70 倍以上（存在少量协议差异）。

Conclusion: 机械重设计、机载传感与学习控制三者结合，可显著提升单执行器球形机器人的敏捷性与自主性。

Abstract: Reducing actuator count simplifies robot hardware but also removes independent inputs for maneuvering. The original Rollbot steered by modulating a quasi-stable helical orbit, but moved slowly and relied on external motion capture. Rollbot 2 preserves the single-actuator design while its redesigned mass distribution enables near-straight rolling and more direct travel toward commanded paths and beacon targets. Onboard inertial and ultra-wideband sensing support learned steering, beacon seeking, and waypoint following through one motor-velocity command. Rollbot 2 autonomously approaches UWB beacons without motion capture and completes a 468~m manually commanded indoor route at 0.87~m/s. On the shared 4.8~m ``N'' waypoint benchmark, all 16 trials complete the path with a mean traversal time of 10.8~s and mean time-averaged deviation of 7~cm. Its fastest traversal takes 8.0~s, over 70 times faster than the original Rollbot's 564~s run despite minor protocol differences. These results show how mechanical redesign, onboard sensing, and learning-based control improve the agility and autonomy of a single-actuator spherical robot.

</details>


### [26] [FLASH-CBF-RRT: Fast Lipschitz-Adaptive Safe Horizon CBF Steering for Certified Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24149)
*Mani Amani,Hesam Mojtahedi,Reza Akhavian,Nikolay Atanasov,Ki Myung Brian Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLASH-CBF-RRT通过闭式运动学碰撞间隙界估计CBF滤波速度可保持不变的时间视界，实现自适应步长的局部转向，在保持100%成功率的同时将中位规划时间减少3.82倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速运动规划既需要样本高效的探索，也需要计算高效的局部转向。直线转向计算便宜但会因拒绝碰撞扩展而浪费样本；带控制屏障函数（CBF）约束的二次规划控制器可实现有效局部转向，但昂贵的序列化求解会显著增加规划时间。

Method: 利用碰撞间隙的闭式运动学界，计算出一个时间视界，在该视界内CBF滤波后的速度可以恒定施加；据此设计自适应转向步长（障碍附近步长短、开阔空间步长长），并将该转向过程作为RRT-Connect的扩展实现在OMPL中。

Result: 在689个MotionBenchMaker问题上，FLASH-CBF-RRT达到与RRT-Connect相同的100%成功率，中位规划时间降低3.82倍，比固定步长CBF-RRT快3.88倍；在17自由度移动机械臂硬件实验中，规划速度快2.2倍、评估次数减少4.2倍。

Conclusion: 通过闭式运动学界减少CBF序列评估次数并采用自适应步长，可在不牺牲成功率的前提下显著加速基于采样的运动规划，并在高自由度真实平台上得到验证。

Abstract: Fast motion planning requires both sample-efficient exploration and computationally efficient local steering. Conventional straight-line steering is inexpensive but wastes samples by rejecting colliding extensions. Quadratic program controllers with control barrier function (CBF) constraints can be used to achieve effective local steering but can increase the planning time due to the expensive sequential evaluations. We develop a method to accelerate CBF steering by computing a time horizon within which CBF-filtered velocity may be applied constantly using closed-form kinematic bounds on collision-clearance. Our method, FLASH-CBF-RRT, significantly reduces the number of evaluations compared to prior CBF-RRT approaches because the planner can take adaptive steering step sizes that are shorter near obstacles and longer in open space. We implement this steering procedure as an extension of RRT-Connect in OMPL. Across 689 MotionBenchMaker problems, FLASH-CBF-RRT matches RRT-Connect's 100% success rate while reducing median planning time by 3.82x, and is 3.88x faster than fixed-step CBF-RRT. Hardware experiments on a 17-DoF mobile manipulator demonstrate 2.2x faster planning and 4.2x fewer evaluations. Videos and results are available at: https://mamani828.github.io/FLASH-CBF-RRT/.

</details>


### [27] [Multimodal Interaction Estimation for Contact-Aware Adaptive Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24235)
*Mateusz Jaszczuk,Paris Perdikaris,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出任务无关的交互估计与自适应控制框架，融合力历史与3D点云（基于Transformer的分类器，带模态dropout与仿真到真机微调），区分未知负载与人类推挤，并驱动系统辨识补偿负载、变阻抗控制器在自适应与反应模式间切换，兼顾下游交互力/意图估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在制造车间、家庭等以人为中心的环境部署时，既要补偿未知负载实现安全精确操作，又要在人机交互中保持柔顺。单一模态难以可靠区分交互类型：力信号无法区分负载与人的推力，视觉易受遮挡。

Method: 1) 基于Transformer的分类器，融合力历史与3D点云进行交互类型估计；2) 训练时采用模态dropout以增强遮挡鲁棒性，并从仿真微调到真机；3) 分类结果驱动系统辨识框架，选择性补偿未知负载；4) 变阻抗控制器在自适应模式与反应模式间切换；5) 同时估计交互力以支持人类意图估计等下游任务。

Result: 分类器达到91.89%准确率，在单一模态完全遮挡时仍保持90%以上准确率。在外部扰动与未知负载下，通过单臂操作、结合模仿学习策略的部署、意图驱动的目标切换，以及在两台Franka机械臂上抬起未知质量箱子的零样本双臂部署进行验证，展示了对闭链运动学与未见设置的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 多模态融合的交互估计配合自适应控制，能够在遮挡与未知负载条件下实现安全、精确且柔顺的机器人部署，并具备向闭链任务与未见过场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: With advancements in learning-based policies, robots are increasingly being deployed in human-centric environments, such as manufacturing floors and homes, where safe and precise operation requires compensating for unknown loads while remaining compliant during human interactions. Reliably distinguishing between these interaction types is challenging with a single sensing modality, as force signals cannot separate payloads from human pushes, and vision is susceptible to occlusions. We propose a task-agnostic interaction estimation and adaptive control framework that addresses this challenge by fusing force history and 3D point cloud through a transformer-based classifier. Trained with modality dropout for occlusion resilience and fine-tuned from simulation to hardware, the classifier achieves 91.89% accuracy and maintains above 90% under complete occlusion of one modality. The classifier drives a system identification framework that selectively compensates for unknown payloads and a variable-impedance controller that switches between adaptive and reactive modes, while simultaneously estimating interaction forces for downstream tasks, such as human intent estimation. We validate the framework under external perturbations and unknown loads across single-arm manipulation, deployment with an imitation-learning-based policy, intent-driven target switching, and zero-shot bimanual deployment on two Franka arms lifting a box of unknown mass, demonstrating generalization to closed kinematic chains and unseen setups, enabling safe and precise deployment.

</details>


### [28] [Towards Reinforcement Learning for Space Robotics: Fast Training and Reliable Real-World Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24241)
*Abhishek Naik,Michael Wu,Michael O'Sullivan,Colin Bellinger,Yunli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于快速仿真的强化学习训练框架，仅需一台消费级GPU、不到20分钟训练即可让四足机器人习得可靠穿越多种真实地形的控制策略，并在类月面设施中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望降低强化学习训练控制策略的硬件门槛，并解决仿真到现实迁移的可靠性问题，从而推动RL成为开发更具能力、更鲁棒的空间机器人系统的关键工具。

Method: 采用基于快速仿真的训练框架，通过强化学习在仿真中学习四足机器人的控制策略；训练硬件需求极低（单块消费级GPU），并将仿真中学到的策略直接部署到真实机器人。

Result: 在不到20分钟的单GPU训练后，四足机器人能够可靠地穿越多种真实世界地形，包括类月面（lunar-analog）设施中的复杂地形，仿真策略成功迁移到现实。

Conclusion: 结果表明低成本、短时间的仿真强化学习即可获得可迁移的鲁棒控制策略，展示了强化学习在未来空间机器人研发中成为核心工具的潜力。

Abstract: This work demonstrates the use of a fast simulation-based training framework to learn control policies using reinforcement learning. The hardware requirements are minimal and the simulation-learned policies transfer reliably to the real world. With this setup, we show that a quadruped robot can learn to traverse diverse real-world terrains reliably---including in a lunar-analog facility---after less than 20 minutes of training on a single consumer-grade GPU. These results provide a glimpse of a future where reinforcement learning is a crucial tool in developing more capable and robust space robotic systems. Video demonstrations are available at https://youtu.be/vUFJiZdJaQ0.

</details>


### [29] [TrajEdit: Real-Time Flow-Matching Trajectory Adaptation via FlowEdit and CBF Safety Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24256)
*Wondmgezahu Teshome,Arya Honarpisheh,Octavia Camps,Mario Sznaier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrajEdit是一个分层轨迹规划器，用于追逃场景下的动态避障：名义情况下沿当前计划执行，追捕者逼近时用FlowEdit对剩余轨迹进行编辑，当追捕者进入安全阈值内则切换到控制屏障函数(CBF)安全滤波器，在保持成功率的同时显著降低计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当动态障碍物主动追捕自主体时，安全实时的运动规划极为困难，环境快速变化要求智能体快速适应。传统方法在每一步都从零重新生成轨迹，计算开销大且浪费已有计划信息，因此需要一种能在保持安全性的同时降低重规划代价的规划框架。

Method: 提出TrajEdit分层轨迹规划器，融合流匹配(flow matching)、轨迹编辑与安全过滤三层机制：1）名义条件下遵循当前执行中的计划；2）当追捕者接近时，通过FlowEdit对当前轨迹的剩余部分进行局部自适应编辑，而非完全重新生成；3）若对抗智能体进入安全阈值范围，则回退到基于控制屏障函数(CBF)的安全滤波器以保证硬约束安全。整体构成"执行—编辑—安全回退"的分层结构。

Result: 在带静态障碍物和对抗性追捕者的2D追逃环境中评估TrajEdit，取得了较高的成功率，同时捕获率和碰撞率较低；与完全重规划基线相比，计算时间显著减少，且成功率与之相当。

Conclusion: 用FlowEdit进行轨迹编辑替代逐步完全重规划，能够在维持可比成功率的前提下降低计算开销，配合CBF安全滤波可有效保障追逃场景下的安全性，说明"局部轨迹编辑+安全回退"是实时追逃规划的一种高效可行范式。未来工作可扩展到更高维、更复杂动力学与多追捕者场景。

Abstract: Safe, real-time motion planning is especially difficult when a dynamic obstacle actively pursues the ego agent, requiring rapid adaptation as the environment changes. We present TrajEdit, a hierarchical trajectory planner for pursuit-evasion that combines flow matching, trajectory editing, and safety filtering. Rather than regenerating trajectories from scratch at every step, TrajEdit follows the current plan under nominal conditions and adapts its remaining portion through FlowEdit as the pursuer approaches, and falls back to a control barrier function safety filter if the adversarial agent is within a safety threshold. Our use of FlowEdit for trajectory editing reduces computation time while maintaining a comparable success rate. We evaluate TrajEdit in 2D pursuit-evasion with static obstacles and an adversarial pursuer, demonstrating high success rates with low capture and collision rates, while achieving computational time savings over full replanning baselines.

</details>


### [30] [Evaluating Lightweight Contact Feedback in Dual-Operator Collaborative Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24257)
*Austin Graves,Chuizheng Kong,Yunho Cho,Yuanshao Yang,Advaith Nidumukkala,Shreyas Kousik,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出XRT扩展现实遥操作框架，将机器人指尖接触以轻量振动触觉和/或听觉反馈回传给操作员，并搭建同步双人形机器人测试平台；16组双人完成协作搬箱翻箱实验。结果显示指尖振动触觉反馈被评价为比开耳声化更有效、更易理解，但各条件间任务绩效、工作负荷、不适感和伙伴感知未见差异，练习效应与个体内变异大于条件效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双机器人人形遥操作可实现协作操作，但操作员在缺乏物理交互时无法直接感知机器人与物体的接触状态，缺少关键的触觉信息通道。

Method: 提出XRT扩展现实遥操作框架，通过轻量振动触觉和/或听觉反馈回传机器人指尖接触信息，并构建面向两名操作员的同步双人形机器人测试平台；采用被试内平衡设计，16组双人（dyad）在仅视觉及三种反馈条件下完成协作搬箱翻转任务，并核验反馈通道实际送达。

Result: ①指尖定位振动触觉显示被评为优于开耳声化：32人全部将其排在声化之上，18人评为最有效，且其线索在可感知性与可解释性上评分远高于听觉线索；②完成时间、失败次数、工作负荷、不适感与伙伴意识未检出条件差异，完成时间置信区间较宽、较小效应量未能分辨；③练习效应显著，尤其较难的翻转任务中完成时间随区组改善，组内逐次尝试变异（SD×1.51）大于最大条件对比（×1.16）；探索性分析未发现反馈条件间即时抓握或运动响应差异。

Conclusion: 对新手协作遥操作而言，指尖振动触觉线索被评为比听觉线索更有效、更易解释，但其对任务绩效的影响仍未确定。

Abstract: Dual-operator humanoid teleoperation enables collaborative manipulation through two separate robots, but operators lack direct awareness of robot-object contact that is normally available during physical interaction. To address this, we present eXtended Reality Teleoperation (XRT), an extended reality teleoperation framework that returns a robot's fingertip contact through lightweight vibrotactile and/or auditory feedback, and a synchronized dual-humanoid testbed built on it for two operators. Sixteen dyads performed a collaborative crate lift-and-flip under vision only and the three feedback conditions in a counterbalanced within-subject design with verified feedback-channel delivery. Three results follow. First, operators rated our finger-localized vibrotactile display as more effective than our open-ear sonification: all 32 ranked it above the sonification, 18 ranked it most effective, and its cues were rated far easier to perceive and interpret than the auditory cues. Second, completion time, failures, workload, discomfort, and partner awareness showed no detected condition differences, with wide completion-time intervals and smaller meaningful effects unresolved. Third, performance changed substantially with practice, especially on the harder flip-over task: completion time improved across blocks, and attempt-to-attempt variation within a dyad (SD x1.51) was larger than the largest condition contrast (x1.16). Exploratory analyses detected no difference in immediate grip or motion responses between feedback conditions. For novice collaborative teleoperation, our fingertip vibrotactile cues were rated more effective and easier to interpret than our auditory cues, while their effect on task performance remains unresolved.

</details>


### [31] [How exposed is work to robotics? Occupational, industry, and geographic measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24267)
*Sebastian Sartor,Robert Seamans,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个基于能力（abilities-based）的机器人��物理AI暴露度指标，覆盖774种职业（ROE）、250个行业（RIE）及县级地理（RGE），发现机器人暴露集中于建筑、农业、运输、生产等体力密集型工作，且与生成式AI暴露的分布特征相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI暴露度指数要么只覆盖软件类AI，要么用专利和安装数据来代理机器人，反映的是过去几十年的工业机器人状况；缺乏针对机器人及物理AI、且同时跨职业、行业、地理三个维度的基于能力的暴露度测量工具。

Method: 沿用Felten等（2018, 2021）框架：从78个学术、产业和标准来源构建涵盖8大领域、16项能力的机器人能力分类体系；用大语言模型对每项能力与O*NET的52项工作能力的相关性打分；据此汇总为774种职业的机器人职业暴露度（ROE）、250个NAICS行业的机器人行业暴露度（RIE）以及县级机器人地理暴露度（RGE）。

Result: 机器人暴露集中于建筑、农业、运输、生产等体力密集型工作，以及伐木、采矿、卡车运输、仓储等行业。与生成式AI暴露不同，ROE在低工资、低学历要求、创造性能力含量较低的职业中更高。影响排名变动最大的能力包括物理协作与辅助、地形行走、抓取与操作、任务与功能推理，但无单一能力主导该指数。

Conclusion: 该指标衡量的是技术相关性而非准备度、成本或采用情况，因此应被解读为物理自动化可能影响工作的上限估计。

Abstract: Exposure indices that map technical capabilities onto occupational abilities have become a standard tool for anticipating how AI may reshape work, but existing measures either cover software AI or proxy robotics through patents and installation data that reflect the industrial robots of past decades. We construct the first abilities-based exposure measures for robotics and physical AI, and the first to span occupations, industries, and geographies. Following the framework of Felten et al. (2018, 2021), we build a robotics capability taxonomy of 16 capabilities in eight domains from 78 academic, industry, and standards sources, use a large language model to score the relatedness of each capability to the 52 ONET work abilities, and aggregate to Robotics Occupational Exposure (ROE) for 774 occupations, Robotics Industry Exposure (RIE) for 250 NAICS industries, and county-level Robotics Geographic Exposure (RGE). We find that robotics exposure concentrates in physically intensive work such as construction, farming, transportation, and production, and in industries such as logging, mining, trucking, and warehousing. Unlike generative-AI exposure, ROE is higher in occupations with lower wages, lower education requirements, and less creative-ability content. Physical collaboration and assistance, terrain-based locomotion, grasping and handling, and task and functional reasoning move the exposure ranking most, yet no single capability carries the index. Because the measures capture technical relatedness rather than readiness, cost, or adoption, they should be read as an upper bound on where physical automation could affect work.

</details>


### [32] [ACTS: Learning Visuo-Tactile World Models from Human Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24285)
*Yuxiang Ma,Weihan Xu,Pengfei Ye,Anna Mei,Yuxin Ray Song,Tingle Li,Haonan Chen,Edward H. Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACTS——仅用人类手持GelSight交互数据训练的视觉-触觉世界模型，动作表示为"运动+力变化"，可零微调迁移到机器人；触觉动力学迁移比视觉外观迁移更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作的动作条件世界模型需要捕捉接触形成、滑动与释放的数据，但机器人遥操作采集慢、缺力反馈、受限夹爪几何，因此探索能否从人类物理交互中学习接触动力学并迁移给机器人。

Method: 采集React数据集：操作者手持GelSight，同步记录第三人称与腕部视频、触觉图像、6-DoF传感器运动和触觉法向力，共4.9小时、3个物体的双臂数据；在触觉传感器处以"运动+力变化"增广定义动作，构建人机共享动作空间；训练ACTS联合预测未来场景、腕部与触觉观测。

Result: 在留出的人类数据上，ACTS的场景预测PSNR比在React上从零训练的两种视觉-触觉世界模型中最优者高0.7–2.0 dB；零微调用于机器人遥操作时，预测的触觉印记随真实接触出现和消失；视觉迁移较弱：PushT上优于帧复制，Rope上表现参差，Motherboard上低于帧复制，腕部视图存在模糊或结构幻觉。

Conclusion: 人类到机器人的迁移中，接触动力学比视觉外观更可靠；以力变化增广的运动作为共享动作空间是可行的跨具身建模范式。

Abstract: Abstract
  Action-conditioned world models for contact-rich manipulation require data that captures how contact forms, slides, and releases under varying force. Such data are slow to collect through robot teleoperation, which often lacks force feedback and is constrained by gripper geometry. We ask whether these dynamics can instead be learned from human physical interaction and transferred to a robot.
  We present ACTS, a visuo-tactile world model trained only on human interaction data. Operators manipulate handheld GelSight sensors while we record third-person and wrist video, tactile images, 6-DoF sensor motion, and tactile-derived normal force, yielding React, a 4.9-hour bimanual dataset across three objects. We represent actions at the tactile sensor as motion augmented by force change, providing a shared action space for human collection and robot execution. ACTS jointly predicts future scene, wrist, and tactile observations.
  On held-out human data, ACTS improves scene-view prediction by 0.7-2.0 dB PSNR over the stronger of two visuo-tactile world models trained from scratch on React. Without fine-tuning, in the robot teleoperation rollouts we inspected, predicted tactile imprints still appear and disappear with recorded contact. Visual transfer is less robust: camera predictions exceed frame copying on PushT, are mixed on Rope, and fall below it on Motherboard, while wrist views can blur or hallucinate structure. These results suggest that contact dynamics transfer more reliably than visual appearance from human to robot.

</details>


### [33] [Beyond Human-Derived Motion: Exploring Robot-Specific Execution for Construction Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24312)
*MinJun Chang,Taehyung Kim,Yong K. Cho,Francis Baek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究比较了人形机器人执行"人类衍生动作"与"机器人专属动作"的效率差异，发现机器人专属动作在施工搬运任务中执行时间、轨迹长度与机械功均显著更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑业生产率长期停滞，促使人们关注能在为人设计的工作空间与工具中作业的人形机器人。实现这一潜力需要有效的运动控制，而将人类示范动作捕捉并重定向到人形机器人是有前景的途径。但人类衍生动作未必能完全定义机器人应如何执行该动作——在传达任务相关信息之后，机器人或可利用自身形态做出偏离人类示范的动作，其效率优势尚待检验。

Method: 在成人尺寸的 Unitree H1-2 上开展实验。人类衍生动作通过视觉捕捉与重定向生成；机器人专属动作则采用逆运动学与最小加加速度（minimum-jerk）转移，在到达相同目标的同时偏离示范姿态，并用快速上肢评估（RULA）进行验证。任务为代表建筑材料搬运的抓取-堆叠序列，设置四个匹配的运动目标，每种条件三次试验。

Result: 机器人专属动作执行时间为 12.87 s（人类衍生动作为 25.03 s），轨迹长度为 0.92 m（对 4.33 m），双臂机械功为 58.7 J（对 247.3 J）。

Conclusion: 允许人形机器人偏离人类示范动作，可为更高效的运动执行提供额外机会；但尚需更广泛的施工任务来确定这些差异能否转化为生产率提升。

Abstract: Construction productivity has remained a per- sistent challenge, motivating growing interest in humanoid robots that can operate in workspaces and with tools designed for human workers. Realizing this potential requires effective motion control, for which human-derived motion transfer has emerged as a promising approach by capturing and retargeting human demonstrations to humanoid robots. While promising, however, human-derived motion transfer may not fully define how a humanoid should ultimately execute the transferred motion. Once task-relevant motion information has been con- veyed, the humanoid may also exploit its own embodiment through motions that depart from the human demonstration. This study examines whether such Robot-Specific Motion offers an advantage in motion execution efficiency over Human- Derived Motion. On an adult-sized Unitree H1-2, Human- Derived Motion was generated through vision-based capture and retargeting, whereas Robot-Specific Motion used inverse kinematics and minimum-jerk transfers to reach the same targets while departing from the demonstrated posture, as verified using Rapid Upper Limb Assessment (RULA). Across a pick-and-stack sequence representing construction material handling, with four matched motion targets and three trials per condition, Robot-Specific Motion achieved 12.87 s versus 25.03 s in execution time, 0.92 m versus 4.33 m in trajectory length, and 58.7 J versus 247.3 J in two-arm mechanical work. These findings suggest that allowing humanoids to depart from demonstrated human motion may offer additional opportunities for more efficient motion execution, while broader construction tasks are needed to determine whether such differences trans- late into productivity gains.

</details>


### [34] [Gen2Dex: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Simulation-Initialized Generated Video Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24392)
*Xiaowen Qiu,Yian Wang,Haoxiang You,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个端到端框架，将从模拟场景生成的视频（含几何畸变、接触不准等物理不一致）转换为物理可执行的灵巧机器人示范：先用基于模型的目标跟踪（密集基础模型特征+深度对齐）恢复人手运动，再用残差强化学习结合反向课程与有效重置状态缓存进行重定向与物理接地，在ARCTIC上性能具竞争力且对生成视频参考更鲁棒，并实现仿真中刚体与关节体任务及真机开环迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频示范能为灵巧操作学习提供丰富策略，而基于完全给定模拟场景生成视频可获知物体几何、关节结构与相机参数，并可控地设定任务与场景；但生成视频存在几何畸变、接触不准等物理不一致，使恢复的运动难以直接部署到机器人上，因此需要一种把这类"模拟接地视频"转化为物理可执行示范的方法。

Method: 端到端框架，分两部分：感知流水线利用已知场景进行基于模型的目标跟踪，结合密集基础模型特征与基于深度的手-物体对齐，恢复人手运动；随后将运动重定向并用残差强化学习进行物理接地，其中采用反向课程学习与物理有效重置状态缓存，使策略在保留示范操作策略的同时纠正不完美的视频参考。

Result: 在干净的ARCTIC示范上，性能与已有灵巧重定向方法相当；应用于生成视频提取的参考时鲁棒性更强。进一步在仿真中展示了多种刚体与关节体操作任务，并将优化后的轨迹成功开环迁移到真实灵巧机器人。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将带有物理不一致的模拟接地视频可靠地转化为物理可执行的灵巧机器人示范，兼具对参考质量的鲁棒性与真机可迁移性，为利用生成视频进行灵巧操作学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Human video demonstrations provide rich manipulation strategies for dexterous manipulation learning. We study a setting in which a fully specified simulated scene is used to generate videos that specify the demonstrated manipulation behavior. This setup provides access to scene information such as object geometry, articulation, and camera parameters, while allowing direct control over task and scene configuration. However, generated videos may contain geometric distortions, inaccurate contacts, and other physical inconsistencies, making the recovered motion difficult to execute directly on a robot.
  We present an end-to-end framework for converting such simulation-grounded videos into physically executable dexterous robot demonstrations. Our perception pipeline leverages the known scene through model-based object tracking with dense foundation-model features and depth-based hand-object alignment. The recovered human motion is then retargeted and physically grounded using residual reinforcement learning, where a backward curriculum and a cache of physically valid reset states enable the policy to correct imperfect video-derived references while preserving the demonstrated manipulation strategy. On clean ARCTIC demonstrations, our method achieves competitive performance with prior dexterous retargeting approaches and shows greater robustness when applied to references extracted from generated videos. We further demonstrate diverse rigid and articulated manipulation tasks in simulation and successful open-loop transfer of the optimized trajectories to a real dexterous robot.

</details>


### [35] [Tension Tells the Tail: Cable-Obstacle Interaction Sensing for Tethered Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24449)
*Ryuki Shimada,Koki Suzuki,Genya Ishigami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究以缆索张力作为一种传感模态，判别系绳移动机器人电缆接触到的障碍物是否可移动，用六个张力特征训练逻辑回归分类器，在接触后行进30 cm时达到95.5%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系绳移动机器人可通过电缆持续供电与稳定通信实现长时作业，但电缆与环境的交互会导致障碍物被意外推移、电缆被勾挂或缠绕甚至损坏，进而造成任务失败。为评估每一次电缆-障碍物接触的风险，需要一种方法判断被接触障碍物是可移动还是不可移动。

Method: 搭建系绳移动机器人实验平台，在不同机器人速度、障碍物与电缆的接触角、障碍物质量以及障碍物侧面材质条件下采集张力数据；观察到障碍物可移动性可由初始张力上升以及峰后张力是否松弛（还是保持高位）来表征；据此从张力曲线中选取六个特征，使用117组带标签试验训练逻辑回归分类器。

Result: 在接触后行进30 cm的条件下，所提方法区分可移动与不可移动障碍物的平衡准确率达到95.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明电缆张力可用于电缆-障碍物交互的风险评估，并朝在线、电缆感知的自主导航迈出了一步。

Abstract: Tethered mobile robots can sustain long-duration operation through continuous power supply and stable communication via their cables. However, cable-environment interaction causes unintended obstacle displacement, cable snagging or entanglement, and even cable damage, leading to mission failure. To assess the risk of individual cable-obstacle contacts, this study investigates cable tension as a sensing modality for estimating whether a contacted obstacle is movable or non-movable. We conducted experiments with a tethered mobile robot and collected tension data while varying robot velocity, contact angle between the obstacle and cable, obstacle mass, and obstacle side-surface material. The results show that obstacle movability is characterized by the initial tension rise and, more importantly, by whether the tension relaxes or remains high after its peak. Based on these observations, we select six features from the tension profiles and train a logistic-regression classifier using 117 labeled trials. The proposed method distinguishes movable and non-movable cases with a balanced accuracy of 95.5% after 30 cm of post-contact travel. These results demonstrate the potential of cable tension for risk assessment of cable-obstacle interactions and provide a step toward online, cable-aware autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [36] [Touch Where It Matters: Learning Local Tactile Policies for Fine-Grained Gripper Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24485)
*Mingyuan Ba,Yanan Zhou,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ContactPolicy：在视觉基础策略持续控制机械臂的同时，用一个基于触觉与力历史的局部学习控制器接管夹爪，实现接触丰富任务中的细粒度夹爪调节，并由Intent策略管理控制权交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿策略能组织任务动作，但接触丰富的物体使用需要超出"粗略开合"的夹爪调整，因此需要研究细粒度夹爪控制。

Method: 将细粒度夹爪控制建模为任务执行中的局部反馈问题：ContactPolicy融合触觉与力历史及当前机器人状态，联合预测夹爪增量及其调制量，并依据每次新观测更新夹爪目标；Intent策略负责视觉基础策略与ContactPolicy之间的控制权交接。同时进行了臂状态消融与失败分析。

Result: 在开容器、物体操作与工具使用等真实世界实验中，任务成功率优于Visuomotor Policy与Tactile-Force Policy基线；执行分析显示夹爪在任务过程中能保持目标、并随交互条件变化进行细粒度调整。

Conclusion: 结果支持在视觉操作框架内采用任务局部的触觉策略来实现细粒度夹爪控制。

Abstract: Visual imitation policies can organise task motion, yet contact-rich object use demands gripper adjustments beyond coarse opening and closing.
