We study fine-grained gripper control as a local feedback problem within ongoing task execution. Our approach assigns tactile feedback a dedicated control role during contact-rich task stages. We introduce ContactPolicy, a learned local controller that takes over the gripper while a visual base policy continues to control the arm. ContactPolicy combines tactile and force histories with current robot state to jointly predict gripper increments and their modulation, updating the gripper target from each new observation. An Intent policy manages control handover between the visual base policy and ContactPolicy. Real-world experiments on container opening, object handling, and tool operation show improved task success over both the Visuomotor Policy and Tactile-Force Policy baselines. Execution analysis illustrates how the gripper adjusts contact within an ongoing task, including periods of target retention and fine-grained changes as interaction conditions evolve. Arm-state ablations and failure analysis further examine how execution context supports local regulation. These results support task-local tactile policies for fine-grained gripper control within visual manipulation.

</details>


### [37] [GazeAct: A Human-Gaze-Annotated Manipulation Dataset for Attention-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24507)
*Anupam Pani,Wang Yuan,Qifan Wang,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GazeAct 是一个带视线（gaze）标注的真实机器人操作数据集与训练框架，通过屏幕遥操作采集与多相机观测对齐的 300Hz 人类视线数据，作为训练期软监督来约束 VLA 策略的语言条件视觉注意力，在 18 个真实任务上把平均成功率从 48.1% 提升到 58.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略需要把语言指令正确落到物体、接触点和目标位置上，才能可靠操作；人类视线天然提供这种"接地"信号，但现有机器人数据集很少把真实人类视线与策略所用的多相机观测配对。因此作者希望构建这样的数据资源，并探索如何把视线信号用作训练期监督。

Method: 采用基于屏幕的遥操作：操作者在机器人相机画面上产生视线，视线直接记录在该画面上，从而获得 40 个任务、5 个领域、共 12000 条演示，300Hz 视线与图像、本体感受、动作同步。随后将操作者所选视角上的视线迁移到其余同步相机，得到跨视角空间一致的视线目标；训练时用这些目标正则化语言条件视觉注意力，推理阶段不需要视线输入。

Result: 在 18 个真实世界任务上，平均成功率从 48.1% 提升至 58.6%，单项最高提升 25 个百分点。消融表明视线作为软监督效果最佳，且真实人类视线比通用注意力塑形先验提供更强的注意力目标。增益可迁移到 LIBERO-Spatial（57.9%→69.6%）以及不同 VLA 骨干网络。

Conclusion: 在机器人观测空间内采集的视线，可以作为有效的训练期目标信号，提升 VLA 操作的视觉接地能力，且不增加推理时的输入负担。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies must ground language instructions in the right objects, contact points, and target locations for reliable manipulation. Human gaze provides a natural signal for this grounding, but existing robot datasets rarely pair real human gaze with the multi-camera observations used by modern policies. We introduce GazeAct, a gaze-equipped real-robot manipulation dataset and training framework for multi-view VLA policies. Using screen-based teleoperation, we record gaze directly on the robot camera feed viewed by the operator, yielding 12000 demonstrations across 40 tasks and five domains with 300 Hz gaze synchronized with images, proprioception, and actions. We transfer gaze from the operator-selected view to the remaining synchronized cameras, producing spatially consistent targets across the policy's visual inputs. We then use these gaze-derived targets to regularize language-conditioned visual attention during training while keeping inference gaze-free. Across 18 real-world tasks, our method improves average success from 48.1% to 58.6%, with gains up to 25 percentage points. Ablations show that gaze works best as soft supervision and that real human gaze provides stronger attention targets than generic attention-shaping priors. The gains transfer to LIBERO-Spatial (57.9% to 69.6%) and different VLA backbones.These results show that gaze collected in the robot observation space can provide effective training-time targets for improving visual grounding in VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [38] [ContactBench 1.0: A Benchmark for Everyday Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24512)
*Jingyi Xiang,Yixuan Wang,Jeannette Bohg,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ContactBench 1.0，一个可扩展的接触丰富操作基准，基于日常人类操作归纳出八类可复现技能，并以36个参考任务、40余个可3D打印物体资产及带可调阻力的仿真模型实例化，用于可复现的泛化与策略评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作是日常活动核心，但其接触行为多样、跨实验室任务难以复现，导致机器人接触操作能力缺乏统一、可复现的评估手段。

Method: 从日常人类操作观察中归纳八类反复出现的技能类别作为研究框架；构建 ContactBench 1.0，实例化为36个参考任务与40余个物体资产；资产保留任务相关几何与接触特性，可由消费级3D打印机制造，并配套带可调阻力负载的仿真模型；通过受控任务变化评估策略泛化；在6个基准任务上评测近期基于视觉与视觉-触觉的策略学习方法。

Result: 评测显示，所测试的纯视觉策略难以感知或响应接触事件；所测试的视觉-触觉策略未带来实质性性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过提供共享任务与实体/仿真资产，ContactBench 1.0 为接触丰富操作研究建立了可复现评估的基础，并已开源。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation is essential to everyday activities, yet evaluating robots' ability to perform these activities remains challenging because of their diverse contact behaviors and the difficulty of reproducing tasks across laboratories. Drawing on observations of everyday human manipulation, we identify eight recurring skill categories that provide a practical framework for studying these capabilities. We introduce ContactBench 1.0, an extensible benchmark that instantiates these skills through 36 reference tasks and more than 40 object assets. The assets capture task-relevant geometry and contact behavior, support reproducible fabrication using consumer-grade 3D printers, and are paired with simulation models with tunable resistance loads. Controlled task variations enable evaluation of policy generalization. We evaluate recent vision-based and vision-tactile policy learning methods on six benchmark tasks. The results reveal that the evaluated vision-only policies struggle to perceive or react to contact events, while the evaluated visual-tactile policies do not meaningfully improve performance. By providing shared tasks and physical and simulated assets, ContactBench 1.0 establishes a foundation for reproducible evaluation of contact-rich manipulation research. The benchmark is open-sourced at https://jingyi-xiang.github.io/contactbench

</details>


### [39] [SocialCoAct: Enabling and Benchmarking Egocentric Social Co-Action for VLM-Based Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24559)
*Jiancong Ma,Kun Li,Jin Jiang,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SocialCoAct，一个以智能体为中心的统一框架，将基于 VLM 的第一人称社交协同动作建模为端到端任务，并配套构建 SocialCoAct-Bench 多选基准，用以评测机器人在多人社交场景中"边说话边行动"的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人要融入以人为中心的环境，必须能有效参与社交互动。但现有方法通常把预先生成的语音直接映射为连续动作轨迹，对复杂社交环境感知不足，也缺少显式的高层语义动作规划，难以在多人交互中协调语言与行为。

Method: （1）将第一人称社交协同动作形式化为端到端智能体任务；（2）提出 SocialCoAct-Agent，把第一人称视觉观测与对话历史转换为语言回复和原子动作序列，并将每个动作与其对应的话语片段对齐，输出可在 MuJoCo 与实体人形机器人上执行的行为；（3）基于自然多人互动的连续第一人称录像构建 SocialCoAct-Bench，含 423 道经人工核验的多选题，覆盖长片段理解（LEU）、说话人意图理解（SIU）与协同动作规划（CAP）三个子任务。

Result: 在涵盖闭源与开放权重、通用与第一人称的多种 VLM 上评测，最高总体准确率为 77.54%，难题子集仅 46.51%。扩展推理使 Qwen3-VL-2B 与 Qwen3-VL-4B 分别提升 19.62 和 17.02 个百分点，但推理吞吐分别下降 53.6 倍与 20.2 倍。视觉输入消融显示视觉上下文带来的收益因模型而异。

Conclusion: SocialCoAct 为开发与评测"在复杂社交互动中协同上下文感知语音与动作"的 VLM 人形智能体提供了基础框架与基准；结果表明现有模型在该任务上仍有明显不足，尤其在高难度样本与推理效率之间存在显著权衡。代码与基准将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Enabling humanoid robots to participate effectively in social interactions is important for their integration into human-centered environments. Existing methods, however, typically map pre-generated speech directly to continuous motion trajectories but have limited awareness of complex social environments and lack explicit high-level semantic action planning. We introduce SocialCoAct, a unified agent-centered framework for VLM-based egocentric social co-action. We formulate egocentric social co-action as an end-to-end agent task and develop SocialCoAct-Agent, which transforms egocentric observations and dialogue history into verbal responses and atomic action sequences, aligns each action with its corresponding utterance span, and executes the resulting behaviors in MuJoCo and on a physical humanoid robot. To evaluate the VLM capabilities required by this task, we construct SocialCoAct-Bench from continuous egocentric recordings of natural multi-party interactions. The benchmark comprises 423 human-verified multiple-choice questions across three subtasks: Long Episode Understanding (LEU), Speaker Intention Understanding (SIU), and Coordinated Action Planning (CAP). Across a diverse set of closed-source and open-weight VLMs, including general and egocentric models, the highest overall accuracy is 77.54%, while accuracy on the hard subset reaches only 46.51%. Extended reasoning improves Qwen3-VL-2B and Qwen3-VL-4B by 19.62 and 17.02 percentage points, respectively, but reduces inference throughput by 53.6$\times$ and 20.2$\times$. Visual-input ablations further show that the benefits of visual context vary across models. SocialCoAct provides a foundation for developing and evaluating VLM-based humanoid agents that coordinate context-aware speech and action in complex social interactions. The codebase and benchmark will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [40] [Retrieval-triggered Action Projection Adjustment for Frozen VLA Policies: An Empirical Study and Failure Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24573)
*Jinming Wang,Ruiheng Zhang,Yong Wang,Zipan Xiong,Keyuan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出检索触发的投影校正（RTPC），即一种用于冻结OpenVLA策略的可审计外部记忆包装器；但其宣称的事件率下降未能被独立复现，校正方向也与随机同族方向无法区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略无法在不更新模型的前提下利用过往遭遇的失败经验，因此作者希望以外部记忆的形式为冻结策略注入经验而无需重新训练。

Method: RTPC检索压力采集的局部深度记录，仅对动作的平移分量施加投影校正，保持旋转与夹爪指令不变；在CALVIN单任务操作上采用主队列与独立复现队列进行评估。

Result: 主评估中基于深度的近碰撞/停滞代理事件率下降，但任务成功数未变；该下降在独立复现中未重现，且检索得到的校正方向与随机同族方向不可区分。

Conclusion: 分析指出端点耦合、批次敏感性以及缺少触发匹配的无记忆基线是归因于检索式校正的主要限制；结果不能证明物理安全性提升或记忆特异性收益，但提供了检索条件干预冻结VLA策略的实证评估与失效分析。

Abstract: Frozen Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies cannot incorporate previously encountered failures without updating the underlying model. We present retrieval-triggered projection correction (RTPC), an auditable external-memory wrapper for frozen OpenVLA policies that retrieves stress-collected local-depth records and applies translation-only projection correction while keeping rotation and gripper commands unchanged. RTPC is evaluated on CALVIN single-task manipulation using primary and independent replication cohorts. In the primary evaluation, RTPC reduced a depth-based near-collision or stall proxy event rate without changing task-success count. However, this reduction was not reproduced in the independent replication, and retrieved correction directions were not distinguishable from random same-family directions. Analysis identifies endpoint coupling, batch sensitivity, and the lack of a trigger-matched memory-free baseline as limitations for attributing effects to retrieval-based correction. These findings do not establish physical safety improvement or memory-specific benefits, but provide an empirical evaluation and failure analysis of retrieval-conditioned intervention for frozen VLA policies.

</details>


### [41] [LIBERO-Dynamic: Benchmarking Robotic Manipulation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24579)
*Shaojie Ren,Jingwen Sun,Zezhi Liu,Hongjin Chen,Xin Jin,Wei Zou,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-Dynamic，将全部 40 个 LIBERO 任务扩展为动态目标场景，并显式建模模型特定的推理延迟，用于评估与训练移动目标下的机器人操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作仿真基准假设目标物体静止、策略推理由瞬时完成，掩盖了目标在计算期间如何运动、以及动作执行时观测已过时多久这两个关键问题。

Method: 通过接触摩擦驱动目标沿三类轨迹族与不同速度档运动，并按校准的模型特定推理延迟推进物理世界；同一机制支持训练：回放经验证的静态演示并在线补偿目标位移，生成 9000 条质量过滤的动态轨迹；在每配置 1800 个动态条件下评测 6 个预训练策略，比较执行策略与动作视界，并用动态/混合数据微调 3 种架构；另做小规模真机验证。

Result: 运动越快所有策略性能均下降但不均匀；执行时机与视界会改变同一策略的行为；异步重规划仅在动作队列不发生欠载时才从高频反馈中获益；动态演示在至少一种配方下提升每种架构，增益随时间条件建模、数据组成与训练预算变化；真机实验显示出与仿真一致的任务相关执行策略差异。

Conclusion: LIBERO-Dynamic 将物体运动、推理延迟与动作执行变为显式可控变量，为移动目标操作策略的评估与改进提供了统一基准。

Abstract: Most simulation benchmarks for robotic manipulation keep target objects static and treat policy inference as instantaneous. These assumptions simplify evaluation but hide how a moving target evolves during computation and how stale an observation is when an action is executed. We introduce LIBERO-Dynamic, a dynamic extension of all 40 LIBERO tasks. It drives objects through contact friction along three trajectory families and speed tiers, while advancing the physical world according to calibrated, model-specific inference latency. The same mechanism supports training by replaying verified static demonstrations and compensating robot actions online for target displacement, producing 9,000 quality-filtered dynamic trajectories. We evaluate six pretrained policies on 1,800 dynamic conditions per configuration, compare execution strategies and action horizons with fixed checkpoints, and fine-tune three architectures using dynamic and mixed data. Faster motion degrades every policy but not uniformly; execution timing and horizon can change the behavior of the same policy; and asynchronous replanning benefits from frequent feedback only when the action queue avoids underflow. Dynamic demonstrations improve each architecture under at least one recipe, with gains varying by temporal conditioning, data composition, and training budget. A small-scale physical study shows task-dependent execution-strategy differences consistent with simulation. LIBERO-Dynamic makes object motion, inference latency, and action execution explicit and controllable for evaluating and improving robot policies in moving-target manipulation.

</details>


### [42] [LIBERO-Pro2.0: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Robustness of Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24587)
*Xueyang Zhou,Yangming Xu,Qianjiang Li,Guiyao Tie,Junhao Wu,Ziheng Huang,Guowen Zhang,Zhijie Yuan,Di Wu,Duanfeng Chu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-Pro2.0 鲁棒性基准，涵盖 22 种静态扰动与 20 种运行时干预（6 个维度、1,648 个验证过的偏移任务实例、39,390 条训练轨迹），评测 17 种代表性机器人策略，发现运行时分布偏移下平均性能下降 26.5 个百分点、最大达 32.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIBERO 虽被广泛用于评测机器人操作策略，但其高分并不代表真实的具身能力：模型可能利用基准自身的规律（基准特定模式）而非泛化到多样化运行条件。因此需要系统评估具身基础模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建 LIBERO-Pro2.0 基准：设计 22 种静态扰动与 20 种运行时干预，覆盖 Condition、Environment、Observation、Execution、Robot State Configuration 与 Language 六个维度，生成 1,648 个经过验证的偏移任务实例；通过模型生成与人工示范收集 39,390 条训练轨迹以支持鲁棒性导向学习；所有实例经六轮迭代人工审查与修正以保证物理合理性与任务有效性；评测 17 种代表性策略，涵盖视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型及鲁棒性导向策略。

Result: 所有被评测范式均存在显著鲁棒性差距。在运行时分布偏移下，模型平均性能下降 26.5 个百分点，单项最大下降达 32.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 名义基准性能与分布偏移下的鲁棒性之间存在明显鸿沟，说明仅凭 LIBERO 分数不足以判断具身能力；需要面向分布偏移对具身基础模型开展系统性鲁棒性评测。

Abstract: LIBERO has emerged as a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating robotic manipulation policies. However, strong performance on LIBERO does not necessarily indicate robust embodied capabilities, as models may exploit benchmark-specific regularities rather than generalize to diverse operating conditions. In this work, we introduce LIBERO-Pro2.0, a comprehensive benchmark for systematically evaluating the robustness of embodied foundation models under distribution shifts. LIBERO-Pro2.0 encompasses 22 static perturbations and 20 runtime interventions across six dimensions: Condition, Environment, Observation, Execution, Robot State Configuration, and Language, yielding 1,648 validated shifted task instances. To support robustness-oriented learning, we further collect 39,390 training trajectories through model-generated and human demonstrations. All shifted task instances undergo six rounds of iterative human review and correction to ensure physical plausibility and task validity. We evaluate 17 representative robot policies spanning vision-language-action models, world-action models, and robustness-oriented policies. Our results reveal substantial robustness gaps across all evaluated paradigms: under runtime distribution shifts, the evaluated models exhibit an average performance drop of 26.5 percentage points, with individual drops reaching 32.5 points. These findings highlight the gap between nominal benchmark performance and robustness under distribution shifts, underscoring the need for systematic robustness evaluation of embodied foundation models.

</details>


### [43] [Refuse the Harm, Complete the Task via Graph-of-Rules Planning for LLM-Enabled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24603)
*Ali Taheri,Alireza Taban,Qizhou Wang,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"安全任务完成"任务：LLM机器人应拒绝指令中的有害部分、同时完成其合法部分；并给出RuleGraph——多智能体"规则图"规划框架，将安全约束分散到多个推理与验证阶段。两个基准上ASR降至0%，TSR最高（RoboGuard 98.57%，长程复合指令84.54%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的具身智能体在物理部署中易受越狱攻击而执行不安全行为。现有推理期防御多在"单一危害的短指令"上评估，而真实任务常将安全与不安全子任务交错、隐藏危险步骤，或把单个安全动作组合成不安全结果。这类复合指令使单次集中式安全判断难以区分危险与合法子任务，容易漏判或整条拒绝。

Method: RuleGraph为多智能体图式规则规划框架，把安全约束分散到多个推理与验证阶段：五个协作智能体将通用安全原则落地为具体世界规则、在上下文中评估指令各子任务、并从句合法子任务推导安全目标；随后面向该目标的规划被组织为"规则专用节点"构成的图，高层节点继承父节点规则的并集，候选计划被逐步验证与聚合，直到存在单一计划满足所有相关规则，从而降低对单一集中式安全决策的依赖。

Result: 在两个环境的两个基准上，按纯攻击指令与复合指令分别用攻击成功率（ASR）与任务成功率（TSR）评估。RuleGraph在两个基准上均将ASR降至0%，并在所有方法中取得最高TSR：RoboGuard上98.57%，自建长程复合指令上84.54%。

Conclusion: 面向复合指令的部分拒绝是具身LLM安全的关键问题；通过将安全检查分解到多智能体规则接地与规则图式逐步验证，可在零攻击成功率的同时保持较高任务完成率，优于依赖单点安全判决的方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in embodied AI for robotic manipulation and planning, but their deployment in physical systems exposes vulnerabilities such as jailbreak attacks that induce unsafe behavior. Existing inference-time defenses are mainly evaluated on short instructions containing a single hazard, whereas realistic tasks may interleave safe and unsafe sub-tasks, conceal hazardous steps, or combine individually safe actions into unsafe outcomes. Such compound instructions make it harder for a single safety decision to distinguish hazardous sub-tasks from legitimate ones, risking either overlooking the hazard or rejecting the whole instruction. We introduce safe task completion, a task that requires an LLM-enabled robot to refuse the harmful part of an instruction while completing its legitimate part. To address this problem, we propose RuleGraph, a multi-agent Graph-of-Rules planning framework that distributes safety enforcement across multiple reasoning and verification stages. Five cooperating agents are used to ground general safety principles into world-specific rules, assess the instruction's sub-tasks in context, and derive a safe goal from the legitimate ones. Planning toward this goal is then organized as a graph of rule-specific nodes, where higher-level nodes inherit the union of their parents' rules, and candidate plans are progressively verified and aggregated until a single plan satisfies every relevant rule. In this way, RuleGraph reduces reliance on a single centralized safety decision. We evaluate RuleGraph on pure-attack and compound instructions across two benchmarks in two environments using Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Task Success Rate (TSR). RuleGraph reduces ASR to 0% on both benchmarks while achieving the highest TSR among all methods, reaching 98.57% on the RoboGuard benchmark and 84.54% on our long-horizon compound instructions.

</details>


### [44] [RoMo-GS: Efficient Online 3D Gaussian Splatting for Robot Mapping via Exposure-Integrated Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24605)
*Sangwoo Jung,Di Deng,Jiahui Fu,Laura Herlant,Ayoung Kim,Yafei Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoMo-GS是一个面向机器人应用的在线3DGS系统，通过解析积分处理运动模糊、用Hessian剪枝控制高斯数量，在渲染质量上匹配甚至超越离线去模糊方法而速度快15–150倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线3DGS在真实机器人平台上部署面临两大挑战：机器人快速机动导致图像运动模糊，破坏重建几何并造成相机位姿漂移；同时在线建图过程中高斯数量无限增长。

Method: 不再像以往运动模糊感知3DGS那样对N个离散子帧取平均，而是对每个投影高斯在曝光区间上做解析积分，使每个高斯复杂度为O(1)；在此基础上用基于Hessian的剪枝得分限制高斯增长；同时用Adam联合优化位姿、速度和高斯，并对高斯施加在线LM步骤。

Result: 在多个公开数据集和自采集数据集上，RoMo-GS的渲染质量匹配或超越离线去模糊3DGS方法，速度快15–150倍；在线运行时质量具竞争力，且高斯数量比在线3DGS基线少约20%。

Conclusion: RoMo-GS验证了将解析曝光积分、Hessian剪枝与位姿–速度–高斯联合优化结合的在线3DGS方案，能在保证渲染质量的同时兼顾实时性与内存可控性，适用于机器人在线建图场景。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is emerging as a practical way to build a representation for robotics applications. However, deploying it on real-world platforms exposes multiple challenges. Rapid maneuvers of a robot induce motion-blurred images, which corrupt the reconstructed geometry and cause camera pose drift. Furthermore, the number of Gaussians grows without bound during continuous online mapping. In this study, we present RoMo-GS, an online 3DGS system designed to address these challenges. Instead of averaging N discrete sub-frames as in prior motion blur-aware 3DGS, we analytically integrate each projected Gaussian over the exposure interval, yielding an O(1) complexity per Gaussian. Building on this, we bound the Gaussians' growth with a Hessian-based pruning score, while jointly refining pose, velocity, and Gaussians with Adam and an online LM step over the Gaussians. On multiple public datasets and a self-collected dataset, RoMo-GS matches or exceeds offline deblurring 3DGS methods in rendering quality while running 15--150x faster, and operates online at competitive quality with about 20% fewer Gaussians than the online 3DGS baseline.

</details>


### [45] [Solving a Giant Rubik's Cube with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24610)
*Lukas Amiet,Gabriel Sánchez-Blanco Repgen,Mayank Mittal,Victor Klemm,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter,Cesar Cadena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文展示四足机器人仅用足部自主求解巨型魔方，通过机载RGB-D感知估计魔方位姿与完整颜色状态，规划器生成技能序列，强化学习训练全身"pedipulation"技能并蒸馏为可部署策略，硬件上完成三次约30步、8-9分钟的自主求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作独立支撑的关节物体需要同时控制其位姿与内部构型并保持平衡，而少见的足部操作（pedipulation）研究缺乏对长时程、多步、高精度物理任务的验证。魔方求解中任何非预期的层间转动都会使后续操作失效，因此它是检验机载感知与足部精细协调能力的理想测试平台。

Method: 1）机载RGB-D感知管线：通过移动与翻动获取部分视角，估计魔方的度量位姿与完整颜色状态；2）规划器将求解方案转换为机器人可执行的技能序列；3）两类学习型全身技能：在保持层构型的前提下重定向魔方，以及"一足固定、另一足推动"的转层操作；4）采用带特权观测的强化学习训练，并蒸馏为从机载深度与本体感觉映射到关节目标的策略。

Result: 部署的策略能够容忍机器人与魔方相对放置的差异并从外部扰动中恢复；系统在硬件上完成三次完全自主求解，每次约30步、耗时8-9分钟。

Conclusion: 将机载感知与协调的足部操作相结合，可使四足机器人完成长时程、多步的物理任务，为腿式机器人操作独立关节物体提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Pedipulating a freestanding articulated object requires controlling its pose and internal configuration while maintaining balance. We demonstrate this capability by autonomously solving a giant Rubik's Cube with a quadruped using only its feet. The task demands repeated precise interactions, since unintended layer motion can invalidate subsequent operations. We develop an onboard RGB-D perception pipeline that estimates the cube's metric pose and its full color state from partial views acquired through locomotion and flipping. A planner converts the computed solve into robot-executable skill sequences. Learned whole-body skills reorient the cube while preserving its layer configuration and turn its layers with one foot pinning the cube as the other pushes. We train these skills using reinforcement learning with privileged observations and distill them into policies that map onboard depth and proprioception to joint targets. The deployed policies tolerate variations in relative robot-cube placement and recover from external disturbances. The system completes three autonomous hardware solves, each about 30 steps over 8-9 minutes. These results show that combining onboard perception with coordinated pedipulation enables a quadruped to complete extended multi-step physical tasks. Additional material can be found at: https://leggedrobotics.github.io/rubik-skills/

</details>


### [46] [Noise-Invariant Spatial Encoding for 3D Manipulation Policies under Depth Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24674)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiaju He,Ruiteng Zhao,Anqi Cheng,Haiyue Zhu,Kezhi Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对商品级RGB-D传感器深度噪声导致3D操作策略性能骤降的问题，提出NISE架构，通过几何跨视图自监督与高斯边缘化3D注意力，在训练和部署中显式建模深度不确定性，从而在保持干净深度性能的同时提升噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D操作策略依赖显式场景几何进行度量距离、物体关系与可达性推理，但透明、反光、薄壁或无纹理物体处，商品RGB-D传感器会给出缺失值、边界伪影和"看似合理但错误"的表面。实验发现即使在匹配噪声深度上训练，现代3D策略仍存在"深度噪声悬崖"式的残余性能落差，说明噪声污染与token坐标、几何特征发生了纠缠，而非单纯的数据分布问题。

Method: 提出Noise-Invariant Spatial Encoding（NISE）3D策略架构，包含两个核心组件：(1) Geometric Cross-View Self-Supervision，用标定后的多视角深度监督来塑造共享策略特征；(2) Gaussian Marginalized 3D Attention，将位置不确定性以高斯形式传播至部署时的注意力计算中，而非把每个lifted token当作精确点处理。

Result: 在含传感器级真实损坏的仿真基准上，以及涉及透明与反光材质的真机任务中，NISE均提升了深度噪声鲁棒性，同时保留了干净深度下的原有性能。

Conclusion: 3D策略应面向商品RGB-D的实际部署设计，而非仅针对干净深度基准调优；在空间编码阶段显式建模深度不确定性是弥合深度噪声落差的有效途径。

Abstract: 3D manipulation policies derive actions from explicit scene geometry rather than color images alone, enabling them to reason over metric distances, object relations, and robot-scene reachability. This geometric interface is also a vulnerability: commodity RGB-D sensors return missing measurements, boundary artifacts, and plausible but wrong surfaces around transparent, reflective, thin, or textureless objects. We study this failure under realistic depth corruption and observe a sharp depth-noise cliff across modern 3D policies, even when they are trained with matched noisy depth. This residual gap motivates a spatial-encoding approach: corrupted depth becomes entangled with the token coordinates and geometry features consumed by the action expert. We propose Noise-Invariant Spatial Encoding (NISE), a 3D policy architecture that learns trustworthy geometry features even facing imperfect depth. NISE uses Geometric Cross-View Self-Supervision to shape shared policy features with calibrated multi-view depth, and Gaussian Marginalized 3D Attention to carry position uncertainty into deployed attention rather than treating every lifted token as exact. Across simulated benchmarks with sensor-realistic corruption and real-robot tasks involving transparent and reflective materials, NISE improves depth-noise robustness while preserving clean-depth performance, supporting 3D policies designed for commodity RGB-D deployment rather than clean-depth benchmarks alone.

</details>


### [47] [Action-Aligned Latent Planning for Versatile Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24675)
*Yutang Lin,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FIBER 框架，利用冻结行为基础模型（BFM）的可执行隐空间作为异构交互技能的统一规划表征，通过潜策略投影（LPP）将任务特定策略映射到该空间，并离线训练单一感知流规划器，同时提出自适应时域 BFM（AH-BFM），在仿真与真实 Unitree G1 上验证了统一人形 loco-manipulation 的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多样化人形 loco-manipulation 学习单一感知策略的难点在于：既要统一高层任务决策，又不能丢失使每种交互成功的关键状态相关物理响应。现有表征各有缺陷——运动学或接触目标虽紧凑但不直接编码交互特异的控制响应；直接预测低层动作则迫使规划器建模高频闭环反馈，负担过重。因此需要一个介于两者之间、既紧凑又可执行的共享规划表征。

Method: 1) 以冻结的行为基础模型（BFM）的可执行隐空间作为共享规划表征；2) 提出潜策略投影（LPP），将独立获取的各任务专用策略映射到该隐空间，具体做法是寻找其诱导动作与策略局部控制响应相匹配的隐命令，从而实现低频规划并保留 BFM 的高频状态反馈；3) 汇聚所有技能的闭环隐式 rollout，完全离线训练单一感知流规划器；4) 提出 AH-BFM，用多 gamma 前向-后向（FB）学习训练自适应时域 BFM，兼顾精确控制与长时序全身行为。

Result: 在仿真中对拾取箱子、开门、抓取物体三类任务各 128 种变化进行评测：FIBER 平均成功率达 42.0%，是投影隐式回放（11.6%）的三倍以上，并显著优于运动学规划（3.5%）。真实 Unitree G1 人形机器人实验进一步展示了 sim-to-real 迁移、技能串联以及通过同一统一规划器实现的条件引导。

Conclusion: 以行为基础模型的隐空间为中介、配合潜策略投影与自适应时域 FB 学习，可在不牺牲高频反馈的前提下实现低频、统一的感知规划，为多样化人形 loco-manipulation 提供可迁移、可组合、可控的单策略框架。

Abstract: Learning a single perceptive policy for diverse humanoid loco-manipulation requires unifying high-level task decisions without losing the state-dependent physical responses that make each interaction succeed. This makes the representation between high-level planning and whole-body control critical: kinematic or contact targets provide compact abstractions but do not directly encode interaction-specific control responses, while directly predicting low-level actions requires the planner to model high-frequency closed-loop feedback. We propose FIBER, a framework that uses the executable latent space of a frozen behavioral foundation model (BFM) as a shared planning representation for heterogeneous interaction skills. Our latent policy projection (LPP) maps independently acquired task-specific policies into this representation by identifying latent commands whose induced actions match their local control responses, enabling lower-rate planning while retaining the BFM's high-frequency state feedback. After projection, we aggregate closed-loop latent rollouts from all skills and train a single perceptive flow planner entirely offline. We further introduce AH-BFM, an adaptive-horizon BFM trained with multi-gamma forward-backward (FB) learning to support both precise control and temporally extended whole-body behavior. We evaluate FIBER on box pickup, door opening, and object grasping in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Across 128 diverse variations per task in simulation, FIBER achieves 42.0% mean success, more than tripling projected latent replay (11.6%) and substantially outperforming kinematic planning (3.5%). Real-world experiments further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer, skill chaining, and conditional steering through the same unified planner.

</details>


### [48] [Relational Spatial Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24705)
*Chaoran Zhu,Hengyi Wang,Chenglin Cui,Yazhe Wan,Taein Kwon,Changjae Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出，将VLA模型表示与预训练3D基础模型做直接几何对齐，本质上相当于在特征空间中施加低秩正则，导致预训练VLA表示发生严重秩坍塌（rank collapse）；作者转而从成对关系对齐入手，提出一种在注入3D空间感知的同时不损害原模型表达能力的表示对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间理解对机器人感知环境并执行动作至关重要。现有做法是把3D感知直接编码进视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，即让VLA表示与预训练3D基础模型的表示逐点对齐。作者观察到这种直接几何特征对齐实际上把3D基础先验当作特征空间中的低秩正则来用，从而造成预训练VLA表示严重秩坍塌，压缩了模型原有的表达能力。因此需要一种既能注入3D空间知识、又不牺牲表示丰富性的对齐方式。

Method: 将关注点从"逐点相等（point-wise equality）"转为"成对关系对齐（pair-wise relationships）"：不再强制VLA表示与3D基础模型特征在逐点数值上一致，而是对齐样本/特征之间的关系结构，从而在注入3D空间感知的同时避免低秩约束带来的秩坍塌，保持模型原有表达能力。

Result: 在公开基准、作者新构建的空间推理基准以及真实世界操作场景上开展了大量实验。结果表明该方法能有效把3D空间表示融入VLA推理，在多样化操作任务上取得稳健性能并增强了空间感知能力。

Conclusion: 直接几何特征对齐会因低秩正则效应导致VLA表示秩坍塌，并非注入3D空间知识的合适方式；改用以成对关系对齐为核心的表示对齐方法，可在不损失表达力的前提下有效注入3D空间感知，并在基准与真实操作任务中验证了其有效性与稳健性。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is essential for robots to perceive their surrounding environments and execute actions. Existing approaches typically encode 3D awareness into vision-language-action (VLA) models by directly aligning their representations with pre-trained 3D foundation models. However, we observe that such direct geometric feature alignment essentially exploits 3D foundation priors as a form of low-rank regularization in the feature space, resulting in severe rank collapse of the pre-trained VLA representations. In contrast, we shift the focus from enforcing point-wise equality to aligning pair-wise relationships. We propose a representation-alignment method that enables the injection of 3D spatial awareness without compromising the model's original expressivity. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks, our new spatial reasoning benchmark, and real-world manipulation scenarios demonstrate that our approach effectively incorporates 3D spatial representations into VLA reasoning, achieving robust performance and enhanced spatial awareness across diverse manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [49] [EndoVLA-OOV: Language-Conditioned Out-of-View Target Acquisition in Robotic Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24747)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Tianchun Wu,Zhiqing Tang,Hanlin Zhao,Siu Hing Lui,Tao Yang,Jingsong Lin,Huxin Gao,Yeung Trevor,Raymond Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 EndoVLA-OOV，一个语言条件视觉-语言-动作策略，用于机器人内窥镜中把先前观察到的可疑区域重新带回视野（out-of-view target acquisition）；并开源了含 1,050 条轨迹、约 262k 观测-动作对的 VLA 数据集。跨本体评估中全量数据策略成功率 93.3%（28/30），数据量从 312 增至 1,050 条时成功率由 40.0% 升至 90.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上消化道内窥镜的"扫描—复检"流程需要将先前看到过的可疑区域重新纳入视野细查，但该任务要求在受限、可变形的环境中仅凭部分观测，把解剖与外观描述转化为相机运动，难度大且缺乏数据支撑。

Method: 构建语言条件 VLA 策略：微调预训练骨干网络，输出连续二自由度远端弯曲指令；融合初始、中间与当前图像形成时序上下文以引导搜索；同时开源 1,050 条轨迹（6 类任务、约 262k 观测-动作对）的数据集。实验覆盖湿镜头与低光条件、两个未见胃模型，以及向未见离体环境的跨本体迁移，并做指令与观测干预及训练数据量对照。

Result: 跨本体评估中全量数据策略目标获取成功率 93.3%（28/30）；在相同模型、训练与测试配置下，数据从 312 条增至 1,050 条使成功率由 40.0% 提升至 90.0%；指令与观测干预实验支持语言与时序上下文在所评估任务中的作用。

Conclusion: 在所评估的环境中，语言引导的内窥镜复访在多种机器人本体上可行，且扩充任务专用演示数据能显著提升目标获取性能。

Abstract: The scan-then-revisit workflow of upper gastrointestinal endoscopy requires bringing a previously observed suspicious region back into view for closer inspection. Acquiring such targets requires translating anatomical and appearance descriptions into camera motions from partial observations of a confined, deformable environment. We present EndoVLA-OOV, a language-conditioned vision-language-action policy for out-of-view target acquisition in robotic endoscopy. The policy adapts a pretrained backbone to continuous two-degree-of-freedom distal bending commands and combines initial, intermediate, and current images to guide search. To support policy learning, we provide an open-source VLA dataset containing 1,050 episodes across six tasks and approximately 262k observation-action pairs. Physical evaluation spans wet-lens and low-light conditions, two unseen stomach phantoms, and cross-embodiment transfer to an unseen ex-vivo environment. In the cross-embodiment evaluation, the full-data policy achieves 93.3% target acquisition success (28/30). In a controlled training-data comparison, expanding the corpus from 312 to 1,050 episodes increases acquisition success from 40.0% to 90.0% under the same model, training, and test configuration. Instruction and observation interventions further support the use of language and temporal context in the evaluated acquisition tasks. These results support language-guided endoscopic revisitation across the evaluated environments and robot embodiments and demonstrate the benefit of expanded task-specific demonstrations.

</details>


### [50] [Learning Structured Locomotion References for Optimization-Based Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24779)
*Ömer Tarık Banuş,Uluç Saranlı,M. Mert Ankaralı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层四足运动控制器：由强化学习自适应调节步态、落足等结构化参考，底层保留步态调度、反应式落足规划与全身冲量控制执行高频任务，在六向扰动网格下平均存活率77.29%，优于基于模型参考控制器的50.35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足运动需协调身体运动、接触力、落足位置与步态时序，单一端到端策略或纯模型控制各有局限；作者希望让学习只负责"何时、如何"修改一个已能独立工作的基于模型控制器，从而降低学习难度并保留高频执行的可靠性。

Method: 构建分层控制器：RL策略输出对结构化运动参考的调整，底层由步态调度器、反应式落足规划器和全身冲量控制器负责执行；底层模型骨架可独立维持名义小跑。在向量化"控制器在环"环境中，采用六方向扰动课程训练完整层级，并以通道消融实验分析各自适应通道贡献。

Result: 在同一仿真器与评测协议下，所提控制器在测试扰动网格上平均存活率77.29%，基于模型的参考控制器为50.35%；消融显示自适应落足放置是网格存活率的最大边际贡献通道，步态频率自适应呈现方向相关效应。

Conclusion: 结果支持该分层架构的可行性：通过结构化运动参考，可将学习到的自适应能力与基于优化的全身控制有效结合。

Abstract: Quadruped locomotion requires coordinating body motion,contact forces, foothold placement, and gait timing. We present a hierarchical controller in which reinforcement learning adapts these structured locomotion references while a gait scheduler, reactive footstep planner, and whole-body impulse controller retain responsibility for high-frequency execution. The model-based backbone can sustain a nominal trot independently, allowing the policy to focus on when and how to modify an already functional locomotion controller. We train the complete hierarchy in a vectorized controller-in-the-loop environment using a six-direction disturbance curriculum. Under a common simulator and evaluation protocol, the proposed controller achieves 77.29% mean survival across the tested disturbance grid, compared with 50.35% for the model-based reference controller. Training-time channel ablations identify adaptive foothold placement as the largest marginal contributor to grid survival and reveal direction-dependent effects of gait-rate adaptation. Together, these results demonstrate the viability of the proposed hierarchical controller architecture for integrating learned adaptation with optimization-based whole-body control through structured locomotion references.

</details>


### [51] [When Better Policies Leave Harder Failures: Coverage Shift in VLM Success Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24801)
*Isabel Guan,Dingyuan Zhang,Shipeng Lyu,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在固定VLM评判器下，策略能力提升会改变"哪些失败被评判器看到"：任务成功率大幅上升，但残余失败的被接受率反而升高，作者称之为"覆盖偏移"（coverage shift），并呼吁同时报告绝对漏检、条件漏检与审计覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略评估往往把"任务成功率提升"等同于"系统整体变好"，但监控/评判器是固定的，学习过程会改变失败样本的分布与所在区域。因此需要检验：更好的策略是否反而更难以被监控，即固定评判器的条件监测敏感度是否会随策略改进而下降。

Method: 采用固定的视觉语言模型（VLM）作为成功评判器，在20个随机种子的托盘放置（tray-placement）受控实验中，仅用仿真器验证的成功样本进行训练；对比训练前（未训练采样）与训练后策略的执行结果。进一步做边界探针实验（below-rim 与 above-rim 悬停各160例），并在九种评判器、1392个刺激上做更广诊断；另在八种设置下开展评判器过滤实验，考察状态依赖接受效应的异质性。

Result: 成功率从66.8%升至97.0%，被接受的悬停失败从5.0%降至0.9%（占执行次数）。但评判器对残余悬停失败的接受率为50.0%，高于未训练采样的31.1%。残余失败更多位于托盘边缘以下，该位置可能遮挡被抓取方块下方的空隙。边界探针中，边缘以下悬停被接受148/160，边缘以上为0/160。评判器过滤实验显示状态依赖接受效应在八种设置下呈异质性。

Conclusion: 策略改进不等于失败可检测性提升，二者需区分。学习改变了到达固定评判器的失败类型，即"覆盖偏移"。建议评估时联合报告绝对漏检率、条件漏检率与审计覆盖率三项指标，而非仅报告任务成功率。

Abstract: Can a better robot policy become harder to monitor? We study a fixed vision-language model (VLM) that judges success while a policy improves. In a controlled 20-seed tray-placement experiment, training selects only simulator-verified successes. Success rises from 66.8% to 97.0%, and accepted hover failures fall from 5.0% to 0.9% of executions. Yet the judge accepts 50.0% of remaining hovers, versus 31.1% under untrained sampling. These residual failures more often lie below the tray rim, which can conceal the gap beneath a grasped cube. A separate boundary probe accepts 148/160 below-rim hovers and 0/160 above-rim hovers. We call this change in conditional monitoring sensitivity coverage shift: learning changes which failures reach a fixed judge. A broader diagnosis covers nine judges and 1,392 stimuli. Judge-filtered experiments across eight settings further show heterogeneous effects of state-dependent acceptance. The results distinguish task improvement from failure detectability and motivate reporting absolute misses, conditional misses, and audit coverage together.

</details>


### [52] [SARAM: Sparse Adapter Allocation with Reuse and Merging for Lifelong Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24804)
*SeongJun Jeong,Chungwoo Lee,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SARAM，一种机器人终身学习框架：仅在稀疏层引入新适配器并复用冻结的旧适配器，部署时用骨干激活统计生成的紧凑签名识别任务并合并进骨干，从而在存储、显存与每步推理开销上不随任务数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大规模预训练VLA骨干的适配器方法能缓解顺序学习中的遗忘问题，但任务专属适配器与选择模块随任务流累积，在固定机载资源预算下带来磁盘存储与部署成本的可扩展性难题。

Method: 1) 每个新任务复用已冻结的旧适配器，仅在稀疏选定的层新增适配器；2) 引入重复惩罚，促使新适配器捕获适配器库尚未表示的信息；3) 部署时由骨干激活统计生成紧凑签名，每回合识别一次任务；4) 将对应任务更新合并进骨干，控制回路中仅保留骨干。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件上，SARAM达到或超过适配器基线，且每任务磁盘状态最多少4.5倍；随任务流增长，GPU显存占用与控制频率与骨干相当；在采用不同骨干的真实UR5机械臂实验中，同样验证了连续任务流中的知识保持。

Conclusion: SARAM在保持或提升终身学习性能的同时，实现了存储增长受限、显存与推理开销与任务数无关的特性，并在仿真与真实机器人上均得到验证，为固定资源预算下的可扩展终身机器人学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Lifelong robot learning requires acquiring new tasks sequentially without forgetting earlier ones. With large pre-trained vision-language-action (VLA) backbones, adapter-based methods effectively mitigate this challenge. However, their task-specific adapters and selection modules accumulate with the task stream, creating a scalability challenge in on-disk storage and deployment cost under fixed onboard resource budgets. We propose SARAM, a lifelong learning framework that limits storage growth while keeping GPU memory and per-step inference cost independent of the task stream length. For each new task, SARAM reuses frozen adapters from previous tasks and introduces new adapters only at sparsely selected layers, with a duplication penalty encouraging them to capture information not already represented by the adapter library. At deployment, a compact signature derived from backbone activation statistics identifies the task once per episode, and the corresponding task update is merged into the backbone, leaving only the backbone in the control loop. Across four LIBERO suites, SARAM matches or exceeds adapter-based baselines while adding up to 4.5x less on-disk state per task. GPU memory usage and control frequency remain comparable to those of the backbone as the task stream grows. Experiments on a real UR5 arm with a different backbone further demonstrate retention throughout a sequential task stream.

</details>


### [53] [Escape Without a Recipe: Maneuver-Agnostic Mars Rover Recovery from Granular Entrapment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24827)
*Meraj Hossain Promit,Chandak Chakma,Md Jubair Ahmed Sourov,Sejuti Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需预设动作的循环策略（recurrent policy）来让陷入松散风化层（regolith）的轮式星球车自主脱困；在MPM仿真中验证了“颗粒捕获”陷阱构型，并取得62.5%的平均脱困率，远高于最强脚本控制器（7/32）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式星球车一旦陷入松散风化层便缺乏有依据的脱困控制器：脚本化操作依赖特定地形，而朴素驾驶会加深陷入。作者希望用学习型策略替代预设动作，并先建立一个可验证的“真陷阱”基准。

Method: 1) 在连续介质物质点法（MPM）仿真器中寻找经核实的陷阱构型：沉陷半径比 z/r=2.0，十个脚本控制器中最强者也仅 7/32 成功；2) 冻结的捕获检测器基于三种材料、六个沉陷层级共 81,771 个窗口训练，输出三状态信念并加入策略观测；3) 独立的仿真器机动锁存（mobility latch）用于门控训练奖励；4) 训练跨五个随机种子评估。

Result: 五个训练种子的平均动作评估脱困率为 62.5%（范围 28.1%–81.3%，每种子 32 次试验），而最强脚本控制器为 7/32，动作噪声为 0/32。数值审计另发现一耦合缓冲区故障，在 16 个并行环境时静默丢弃了 9.7% 的接触节点。

Conclusion: 结果表明在该仿真工况下脱困恢复是可行的，但物理迁移尚未验证；同时暴露了实现层面的数值缺陷，提示结论仅限所测仿真范围。

Abstract: Wheeled planetary rovers immobilised in loose regolith have no principled recovery controller: scripted maneuvers are terrain-specific, and naive driving deepens entrapment. We introduce a recurrent policy that learns to escape without a predefined maneuver. The framework rests on a verified trap: in a continuum material point method (MPM) simulator we identify a configuration at sinkage-to-radius ratio z/r = 2.0 where the strongest of ten scripted controllers succeeds in only 7/32 trials, yet the regolith retains traction and continues to shear, confirming granular entrapment rather than a numerical artifact. A frozen entrapment detector, trained on 81,771 windows spanning three materials and six sinkage levels, supplies a three-state belief to the policy observation. A separate simulator mobility latch gates training rewards. Across five training seeds, mean action evaluation achieves 62.5% escape (range 28.1-81.3%; 32 trials per seed) versus 7/32 for the strongest scripted controller and 0/32 for action noise. A numerical audit identifies a coupling-buffer fault that silently discarded 9.7% of contact nodes at 16 parallel environments. Results establish recovery in the tested simulation regime; physical transfer remains unverified.

</details>


### [54] [DiPLO: Self-distilled Diffusion Planning for Loco-manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24847)
*Shafeef Omar,Sebastian Sanokowski,Elham Daneshmand,Dian Yu,Daniel Cremers,Cordelia Schmid,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiPLO 将自蒸馏扩散规划器与全身跟踪策略结合，通过 SD:DPO 去除对强任务条件依赖、再用 DA:PPO 对齐冻结跟踪器能力，在搬运任务上把成功率从 45% 提升至 99%，并在 Unitree G1 实机上零微调部署成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人移动-操作任务需同时协调行走、平衡与物体交互，示教数据稀缺、纯强化学习从零学习困难。作者还发现预训练中"强任务条件"至关重要：若省略，下游微调无法恢复同等性能，因此需要一种能在保留预训练收益的同时摆脱该依赖的训练流程。

Method: 框架包含扩散规划器与低层全身跟踪策略：规划器用人体到人形的重定向运动做监督预训练，跟踪器用 RL 训练执行。后训练分两阶段：(1) 自蒸馏扩散策略优化 SD:DPO，去除规划器对强任务条件的依赖，仅依赖状态历史与目标；(2) 扩散增强近端策略优化 DA:PPO，一种面向扩散模型的最大熵 RL 方法，微调蒸馏后模型以提升任务成功率并使生成计划贴合冻结跟踪器的执行能力。

Result: 拾取-搬运-放置任务成功率达 99%，预训练模型仅 45%；规划器可为未见过的物体目标生成运动，并在踢球、推拉任务上做了进一步评估；在 Unitree G1 实机上用 DA:PPO 规划器-跟踪器流水线实现多次成功执行，且无需任何真实世界微调。

Conclusion: 表明"自蒸馏去除强条件 + 扩散最大熵 RL 对齐跟踪器"的两阶段后训练能显著提升人形 loco-manipulation 的规划性能，并具备从仿真到真机的零微调迁移能力。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation is particularly challenging because it requires coordinating locomotion, balance, and object interaction, making it difficult to learn from limited demonstrations or from scratch through Reinforcement Learning (RL). We introduce DiPLO (self-distilled Diffusion Planning for Loco-manipulation), a framework that combines a self-distilled diffusion planner with a low-level whole-body tracking policy. Our planner is pretrained via supervised learning on motions derived from human-to-humanoid retargeting, while the tracker is trained via RL to execute them. DiPLO centers around two complementary post-training stages. First, we introduce Self-Distillation Diffusion Policy Optimisation (SD:DPO) to remove the planner's dependence on strong task conditioning, which we identify as a crucial ingredient for effective pretraining, as downstream fine-tuning does not recover comparable performance when it is omitted.. We then fine-tune this distilled model with Diffusion-Augmented Proximal Policy Optimization (DA:PPO), a maximum-entropy RL method for diffusion models, to improve task success and align generated plans with the frozen tracker's execution capabilities. Together, these stages enable motion generation through state-history and goal conditioning alone, enabling 99% task success in pick-carry-place tasks, compared to just 45% for the pretrained model. The resulting planner also generates motions for unseen object goals, with further evaluations on kicking and pushing/pulling tasks. Finally, we deploy our pipeline on the hardware, using our DA:PPO planner-tracker on a Unitree G1, achieving repeated successful executions without any real-world fine-tuning.

</details>


### [55] [Anticipatory Gait Switching for Quadrupeds via Look-Ahead MDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24895)
*Kelvin Aladum,Justin Peel,Nisar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖高度图的轻量级步态监督器，将前视信息与运动约束统一在单一决策策略中，用前视马尔可夫决策过程进行步态选择，在Unitree Go2上减少了滞后切换、降低了无效步态耗时并提升了横滚稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人常通过切换步态来安全通过混合地形，但现有监督器多采用近场阈值做切换决策，当崎岖地形在局部高度图中临近接触时才被检测到，会导致切换承诺滞后；同时 dwell/guard 等硬件约束通常由独立的过渡处理器处理，未与决策统一。

Method: 构建以机器人为中心的高度图，划分为近、中、远三个场带，每个场带离散化为地形类别，并将场带演化建模为马尔可夫过程；把步态选择建模为前视马尔可夫决策过程，其转移模型与代价显式编码平台特定的切换约束。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2 平台的室内与户外实验中，相比经过调参的反应式基线，方法减少了滞后切换，降低了在崎岖地形上使用无效步态的时间，并改善了横滚稳定性。

Conclusion: 该 MDP 形式化带来了具备前瞻性和约束感知的步态切换能力，在无需针对特定路线重新调参的情况下提升了鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quadruped robots often switch gaits to traverse mixed terrain safely. Many practical supervisors enforce hardware constraints (e.g., dwell or guard intervals) using a transition handler. However, the switch decision itself is often driven by near-field thresholds, which can delay commitment when rough terrain is only detected shortly before contact in the local height map. We propose a lightweight height-map-only gait supervisor that unifies look-ahead and locomotion constraints in a single decision policy. A robot-centered height map is partitioned into near-, mid-, and far-field bands, each discretized into a terrain class, and band evolution is modeled as a Markov process. Gait selection is cast as a look-ahead Markov Decision Process whose transition model and cost explicitly encode platform-specific switching constraints. Indoor and outdoor experiments on a Unitree Go2 platform show reduced late switching, lower time spent using ineffective gaits on rough terrain, and improved roll stability compared to tuned reactive baselines. Overall, the MDP formulation yields anticipative constraint-aware gait switching that improves robustness without course-specific retuning.

</details>


### [56] [Dream What I Can: Sparse Visual Planning for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24981)
*Chongkai Gao,Wenlong Huang,Haonan Chen,Lin Shao,Yilun Du,Jiajun Wu,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamCan 是一种分层策略，通过“稀疏视觉子目标规划”把视频模型落地为可执行的机器人操作。高层规划器由预训练视频模型改造而来，生成在可达性与任务效用间取得平衡的稀疏视觉子目标；底层为视觉运动策略。系统从无动作标注的离线机器人视频中估计上述量，并用动态规划自动选取子目标序列，无需人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型能为通用机器人操作提供丰富先验，但其生成的“看似合理”的视觉未来并不总是可执行；同时密集轨迹预测与人工标注成本高、可达性差。作者希望弥合“视觉预测合理”与“动作可执行”之间的鸿沟，并减少监督需求。

Method: 采用分层结构：高层规划器在预训练视频模型基础上改造，输出稀疏视觉子目标而非密集轨迹，并以“可达性 + 任务效用”双准则进行选择；这两个量从离线、无动作的机器人视频中估计，再通过动态规划自动搜索子目标序列。底层使用视觉运动策略（仅 0.1B 参数）执行到子目标的动作。推理时可增加测试时计算量，也可利用额外的次优视频数据。

Result: 相较均匀间隔子目标，选出的子目标更易被可靠到达；整体系统优于语言规划、密集视频规划与均匀子目标规划基线。在 Language-Table 任务上取得高规划成功率，在 LIBERO-Long 上以 0.1B 参数底层策略达到 91.2% 成功率。加入次优视频与测试时计算可进一步提升成功率，并在真实世界双机械臂任务（盒子折叠、含未见物体组合的装箱）上表现良好。

Conclusion: 以稀疏视觉子目标为接口、用可达性与任务效用驱动的动态规划进行自动子目标选择，是一种有效且有数据效率的视频模型“落地/grounding”方式：既保留视频模型的通用先验，又保证子目标可执行，并在仿真基准与真实双臂操作任务上验证了可扩展性与泛化性。

Abstract: Video models provide rich priors for generalizable robotic manipulation, but plausible visual futures are not always executable. We introduce DreamCan, a hierarchical policy that grounds video models through sparse visual planning: a high-level planner adapted from a pre-trained video model and a low-level visuomotor policy. Rather than predicting dense trajectories, the high-level planner generates sparse visual subgoals that balance reachability and task utility. From offline, action-free robot videos, we estimate these quantities and use dynamic programming to select subgoal sequences without manual annotations. The resulting subgoals are more reliably attained than uniformly spaced alternatives, and the full system outperforms language, dense-video, and uniform-subgoal planners, reaching high planning success rates on Language-Table tasks and 91.2% success on LIBERO-Long with a 0.1B-parameter low-level policy. Additional suboptimal videos and test-time compute further improve success. The system also performs challenging real-world bimanual tasks, including box folding and packing with unseen object combinations. More results are available at https://dreamcan.github.io/.

</details>


### [57] [Scalable, Intuitive, Mixed Reality-Based Semi-Autonomous Robotic Telemanipulation for the Execution of a Fine-Grained Bimanual Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25006)
*Shifei Duan,Zhe Wang,Francesco De Pace,Minas Liarokapis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合现实（MR）头显的可扩展、直观的半自主机器人遥操作系统：系统自主完成大部分任务轨迹，操作员仅在关键阶段做细微调整，从而以更低的数据采集负担获得高质量示教数据并训练出全自主策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人遥操作是人类示教采集机器人学习数据的重要手段，示教质量直接影响策略性能。但现有系统高度依赖人工操作，大规模采集数十至数百条示教是繁重过程，操作员易疲劳，尤其是需要持续专注的灵巧任务。因此需要一种在保留人在回路控制的同时降低操作员负担的半自主遥操作方案。

Method: 设计并实现一套可扩展、直观的半自主机器人遥操作系统，利用MR头显进行在机数据采集。系统自主执行任务轨迹的主要部分，仅要求操作员在关键阶段进行精细调整，形成人机协同的示教采集流程。

Result: 在高精度、细粒度的双臂轴孔装配（peg-in-hole）任务上采集数据并训练模仿学习策略进行验证：以更低的数据采集投入，获得了可用于训练全自主执行策略的高质量示教数据。

Conclusion: 半自主遥操作结合MR头显能够有效降低大规模示教采集的人工负担，同时保证示教质量，使全自主策略训练成为可能，为可扩展的机器人示教数据采集提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Nowadays, robotic telemanipulation enables humans to control robots so as to execute tasks in a manner similar to how they perform them, and serves as an essential approach for collecting on-robot data for robot learning by human demonstrations. The quality of collected demonstrations directly impacts the performance of the learned policy. However, existing telemanipulation systems rely heavily on human operation, particularly for large-scale data collection. Collecting dozens or hundreds of demonstrations is a demanding process, as operators tend to fatigue quickly, especially during the execution of dexterous tasks that require sustained concentration. Recently, semi-autonomous robotic telemanipulation has emerged as a promising direction that reduces operator workload while preserving human-in-the-loop control for task execution. We therefore introduce a scalable, intuitive, semi-autonomous robotic telemanipulation system that leverages a mixed reality (MR) headset for on-robot data collection. The system autonomously executes the majority of the task trajectories and only requires the operator to make fine adjustments at key stages. We validate the effectiveness of our system by collecting data and training imitation learning policies on the execution of a high-precision, fine-grained bimanual peg-in-hole task. With lower data collection effort, our system obtains high-quality demonstrations that can be used to train fully autonomous task execution policies.

</details>


### [58] [Learning Waypoints and Passage Events for Decentralized Multi-Quadrotor Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25024)
*Yunhan Shi,Jia Deng,Zehao Jia,Lin Cheng,Shengping Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“学习+优化”框架，用神经网络预测空间航路点和有序通过事件，交由各四旋翼独立求解器在统一物理时间网格上规划轨迹，并通过可微序列凸规划（SCP）端到端训练两个网络；在两机24种布局评测中全部通过物理可行性与采样间距检查，完成指标平均改善1.52%，中位前向计算时间21.01 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多四旋翼协同通过规划需同时决定空间路线与通过时序，且受刚体动力学与执行器约束限制；联合优化时间分配、非线性运动与车间交互，对局部轨迹生成造成严重计算负担，因此需要一种能解耦局部规划又能保持全局协调的方法。

Method: 框架分两层：网络层预测空间航路点与有序通过事件；规划层由各四旋翼独立求解，共享统一物理时间网格对齐，中间事件由网络固定，终端进度本地优化以支持不同完成度量。训练上采用可微序列凸规划，嵌入刚体动力学与旋翼推力上下界，使碰撞代价与完成度梯度可经生成轨迹回传，同时训练两个网络。

Result: 两机24种布局评测：在全部5个留出布局上，适配策略通过物理可行性与采样分离性检查；平均完成指标相对继承策略下降1.52%；在留出交换布局上中位前向时间为21.01 ms，且数值轨迹参考相匹配。

Conclusion: 实验表明，学习得到的空间与事件参数能够协调相互独立的刚体动力学规划器，并在所评测场景中保持各车局部约束，验证了“可微优化在环学习”用于多机通过规划的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-quadrotor passage planning requires coordinated spatial routes and passage times under vehicle dynamics and actuation constraints. Optimizing these decisions jointly couples temporal allocation, nonlinear motion, and inter-vehicle interactions, creating a computational challenge for local trajectory generation. We propose a learning-optimization framework that predicts spatial waypoints and ordered passage events for independently solved quadrotor planners. A common physical time grid aligns the vehicles, with intermediate events fixed by the networks and terminal progress optimized locally to permit different completion metrics. Differentiable sequential convex programming incorporates rigid-body dynamics and rotor-thrust bounds, allowing collision and completion gradients to train both networks through the generated trajectories. In a two-quadrotor evaluation across 24 layouts, the adapted policy passes physical feasibility and sampled separation checks in all five held-out layouts. Their mean completion metric decreases by 1.52% relative to the inherited policy. The median forward time is 21.01 ms on the held-out exchange layout with matching numerical trajectory references. These results demonstrate that learned spatial and event parameters can coordinate independent rigid-body planners while preserving local vehicle constraints in the evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [59] [Localization and Neural Splatting on the Edge: An FPGA Scene Memory for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25068)
*Que Haohua,Mingkai Liu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LENS 是在 AMD KR260 FPGA/ARM 平台上实现的边缘端场景记忆加速系统，通过将 ACEsplat 式场景坐标回归（SCR）与 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）渲染两个核心内核与板卡数据通路协同设计，在低功耗条件下实现高精度定位与高质量新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的机器人与自动驾驶车辆需要能在无桌面 GPU 的情况下定位相机并渲染邻近视角的场景记忆。SCR 与 3DGS 因免去 SfM 阶段而适用，但现有 ACEsplat 式流水线以 GPU 为中心，迁移到边缘 FPGA 面临困难：SCR 对位姿敏感，3DGS 渲染不规则且对容量敏感。

Method: 论文不提出新场景模型，而是做软硬件协同设计：一是 INT16 定点 SCR 加速器以保持位姿精度；二是位精确的定点有序 3DGS 渲染器，将 alpha 透射率改写为 log-sum-exp 递推式，并剔除可证明无用的流式高斯。全部延迟、功耗与质量指标均为板上实测。

Result: SCR 引擎在 25.42 ms 下保持 DSAC* 定位精度；板载渲染器在 Cambridge 上达 34.11 dB（ACEsplat 为 33.20 dB），RealEstate10K 新视角合成达 27.84 dB（对比 26.11 dB），全部十个 Wayspots 场景均达到 ACEsplat 渲染目标。在每项部署任务上，LENS 的每瓦性能均优于 Jetson Orin NX，达 1.9x–5.7x，功耗约为其四分之一；在定位和 Wayspots 渲染上绝对延迟也更快。

Conclusion: LENS 证明 ACEsplat 式工作负载可在低功耗 FPGA/ARM 平台上高效落地并超越边缘 GPU。板载训练可达 ACEsplat 级质量，但在时间与能耗上不及 GPU，构成未来训练加速器的剩余边界。

Abstract: Resource-constrained robots and autonomous vehicles need scene memories that localize a camera and render nearby views without a desktop GPU. Scene-coordinate regression (SCR) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) fit because they avoid an SfM stage, but ACEsplat-style pipelines are GPU-centered, and moving them to an edge FPGA is hard because SCR is pose-sensitive and 3DGS rendering is irregular and capacity-sensitive. We present LENS, an edge scene-memory acceleration system on the AMD KR260 that brings an ACEsplat-style workload to a low-power FPGA/ARM platform. Rather than proposing a new scene model, LENS co-designs the two dominant kernels with the board data path: an INT16 SCR accelerator that preserves pose accuracy, and a bit-exact fixed-point ordered 3DGS renderer that rewrites alpha transmittance as a log-sum-exp recurrence and removes provably useless streamed Gaussians. All LENS latency, power, and quality results are board-measured. The SCR engine preserves DSAC* localization accuracy at 25.42 ms, the on-board renderer reaches 34.11 dB on Cambridge against ACEsplat's 33.20, novel-view synthesis on RealEstate10K reaches 27.84 dB against 26.11, and all ten Wayspots scenes reach the ACEsplat rendering target. LENS wins performance per watt over a Jetson Orin NX on every deployment task, by 1.9x to 5.7x at about a quarter of the power, and is faster in absolute latency on localization and Wayspots rendering. On-device training reaches ACEsplat-level quality but not the GPU's time or energy, marking the remaining boundary for a future training accelerator.

</details>


### [60] [Compile Once, Run Cheap: Frozen, Portable Skills for Embodied Agents, and What Recursive Self-Improvement Adds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25114)
*Jingwen Bai,Guochao Bai,Canbo Zhang,Shuangqi Li,Minchao Hu,Weichun Huang,Shuang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Skill-RSI：让强通用模型仅作为离线作者，在仿真中编写、运行、诊断并修复操作技能直到通过冻结后抽取的布局考核，冻结技能以代码或廉价调用者形式复用，实现技能层面的递归自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能缺乏语言模型那样的数据丰富预训练，每获得一项新操作能力都需逐任务演示或每回合调用前沿模型，成本高昂。作者希望探索第三条路径：把强模型的能力一次性"编译"成可冻结、可复用的代码技能，从而大幅降低部署成本。

Method: 定义 Skill-RSI 并提出编译协议（隔离审计的作者、隐藏验证布局、无反馈与仅继承两种对照、防篡改账本）与复用协议（同一批文件换用其他模型家族的执行器运行）。在 7 个 RoboTwin 2.0 杂乱桌面任务上，用 Claude Code 作为离线作者进行多轮编译与修复，并对比"继承上一版本的修复"与"从零重写"，另设仅继承谱系与写出后不再迭代的对照。

Result: 7 个任务中 4 个成功编译为 7 个冻结技能（5 个首稿即通过、仅需资格验证），每技能作者成本 4.29–8.22 美元（计入 13 次失败战役约 55 美元/技能）。其余 3 个任务在 18 次战役中未编译成功，含 5 次 GPT-6 及一次预注册 150 布局运行：最难任务每次得分 78–95/100 但出现 3–16 次安全中止，未达"95% 成功率且零中止"门槛；100 回合考核仅能将中止率约束到 3.6%。隐藏布局上，继承式修复在 8/8 受控战役中胜过从零重写（符号检验 p=0.004）；仅继承谱系表明差距主要来自继承，开发反馈略有增益（3/3，p=0.125）。写出后不再迭代的战役未检出首稿练习效应（最小可检测效应 14 点）。冻结技能在新布局上无模型时保持 148/150 成功，使用 DeepSeek-flash 调用者时 147/150，每回合成本 0.00028–0.00054 美元；未设直接控制基线。

Conclusion: 将强模型限定为离线作者、把技能冻结为代码，可在部分任务上以一次性低成本换取高复用成功率，且继承式修复显著优于重写；但难度较高的任务仍受安全中止制约而难以编译，且缺少直接控制基线，结论的普遍性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Embodied AI lacks the data-rich pretraining that gave language models their agents and skills, so new manipulation capability costs demonstrations per task or a frontier model in every episode. We study a third route: a strong general model acts only as an offline author that writes, runs, diagnoses and repairs a manipulation skill in simulation until it passes an exam on layouts drawn after freezing; the frozen skill then runs as code alone or under a cheap caller that reads only its description. We call this skill-level recursive self-improvement Skill-RSI and specify a compile protocol (isolated, audited author; hidden validation layouts; no-feedback and inherit-only controls; tamper-evident ledger) and a reuse protocol that runs the identical files with executors from another model family. On 7 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with cluttered tables, Claude Code compiled 4 tasks into 7 frozen skills, 5 from a first draft needing only qualification, at USD 4.29-8.22 of author cost each (USD 55 per skill counting the 13 failed campaigns). The other three tasks did not compile in 18 campaigns, including five with GPT-6 and a pre-registered 150-layout run: on the hardest task every campaign scored 78-95 of 100 but had 3-16 safety aborts, failing the gate of 95% success with zero aborts; a 100-episode exam bounds an abort rate only to 3.6%. On hidden layouts, repair that inherits the previous version beat from-scratch rewriting in 8 of 8 controlled campaigns (sign test p = 0.004); an inherit-only lineage attributes most of the gap to inheritance, with development feedback adding a little (3 of 3, p = 0.125). Write-once campaigns found no detectable first-draft effect of practice (minimum detectable effect 14 points). The frozen skills kept 148/150 successes on new layouts with no model and 147/150 with a DeepSeek-flash caller at USD 0.00028-0.00054 per episode; no direct-control baseline was run.

</details>


### [61] [Structure over Reasoning: Unified Zero-Shot Navigation with a Small Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25130)
*Zeqi Li,Xunyi Zhao,Zerui Li,Sihao Lin,Qi Wu,ShiJie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 机器人端小规模视觉语言模型（Qwen3-VL-4B）在真实感仿真VLN中能力不均衡：单视角目标判断可行，但路线推理、距离估计和停止判断不可靠；作者据此提出"以感知为证据、由地图结构承担推理"的智能体，并结合骨架图与两阶段终止器，在VLNVerse子集上取得优于全部零样本基线的平均成功率，并可部署于Unitree Go2四足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实感、物理仿真的VLN场景要求模型进入控制回路逐步决策，而机器人偏好的小模型能力参差不齐：方向/路线推理接近随机、距离估计近乎常数、停止投票不可靠。因此需要一种不依赖模型强推理能力、让结构承担导航推理的框架。

Method: 每步向VLM提两个感知问题（哪个方向更像目标、当前视角是否支持停止），并在含路线指令时增加一次门控前沿查询；将回答视为证据而非动作。用路线无关的语义场在鸟瞰图上以温度化更新累积方向分数，用骨架图提供前沿与测地距离，用两阶段终止器（轨迹一致性门控+预算强制返回）把停止投票转化为终止点。

Result: 在统一环境、统一预算下重跑所有基线，该智能体在两种粒度上的平均成功率均超过VLNVerse子集的四个零样本基线，但与最强粗粒度基线的配对区间包含零。消融显示终止模块是主导杠杆；13次尝试赋予模型更多导航职责均未带来可靠增益。智能体以离板推理运行于Unitree Go2四足机器人。

Conclusion: 对能力不均衡的小模型而言，"结构优于推理"是有效的设计原则：把VLM限定为感知证据源，由地图、图和终止机制承担推理，可获得稳健的零样本导航表现并支持真实机器人部署；同时终止决策是关键瓶颈，单纯扩充模型的导航职责收益有限。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation is moving into photorealistic, physically simulated scenes, where the leading zero-shot agents put a vision-language model in the control loop to choose every move and stop. Robots favour small models, whose abilities are uneven: Qwen3-VL-4B can judge whether a single view looks like the goal, but its route reasoning is at chance, its distance estimates nearly constant, its stop votes unreliable. Our agent therefore asks it two perceptual questions per step, which direction looks like the goal and whether the view justifies stopping, plus one gated frontier query on route-bearing instructions, and treats the answers as evidence, not actions: a route-blind semantic field over a bird's-eye map accumulates the directional scores through a tempered update, a skeleton graph supplies frontiers and geodesic distances, and a two-stage terminator turns stop votes into termini through a trail-consistency gate and a budget-forced return. With every baseline re-run in one installation at one budget, the agent exceeds all four zero-shot baselines of the VLNVerse subset in mean success on both granularities, though the paired interval against the strongest coarse-grained baseline includes zero. Termination is the dominant lever in ablation, thirteen attempts to give the model more of the navigation establish no reliable gain, and the agent runs on a Unitree Go2 quadruped with off-board inference. Code, per-episode logs and a robot video will be released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/structure-over-reasoning-PAGE.

</details>


### [62] [AI-Enhanced Radiation Classification in ROS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25225)
*Youngseo Lee,Cesar Alan Contreras,Tianshu Ruan,Manolis Chiou,Rustam Stolkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了两个ROS节点，基于梯度提升分类器实现七种放射性同位素的识别与在线预测，并在一台搭载C12137辐射探测模块的Jackal无人车上进行了实地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将放射源识别能力集成到机器人操作系统中，使移动机器人能够自主区分不同放射性同位素，并便于数据集的持续扩充。

Method: 构建模型训练节点（Gradient Boosting分类器，区分七种同位素）和预测节点（接收CSV输入并输出分类结果）；将C12137辐射探测器模块安装于Jackal UGV上由操作员接近不同放射源采集数据；另开发将伽马能谱图像转换为CSV文件的方法以支持数据集扩展。

Result: 留出测试集上分类器总体准确率达95%；在三个源位置开展的机器人实测中，42次试验有30次分类正确（71.43%）。Am-241、Co-60、Cs-137、Na-22、Ti-44在各自6次试验中全部正确，而Ba-133和Eu-152在各自6次试验中全部错误。

Conclusion: 机器人现场试验准确率（71.43%）明显低于留出测试集准确率（95%），表明该ROS识别系统在真实机器人场景下的泛化能力仍有待提升。

Abstract: This study presents the development of two Robot Operating System (ROS) nodes with a primary focus on differentiating seven radioactive isotopes. The first node, referred to as the model training node, uses a Gradient Boosting classifier to identify and classify these isotopes. The classifier achieved a reported overall accuracy of 95\% on the held-out test dataset. The second node, known as the prediction node, provides classification results when users input data in CSV format. For the practical demonstration, the C12137 radiation detector module was mounted on the Jackal UGV, and an operator approached different radiation sources to collect data. To support future dataset expansion, we also developed a method for converting gamma-spectrum graph images into CSV files. In a separate robotic evaluation across three source locations, 30 of 42 trials were correctly classified (71.43\%). Am-241, Co-60, Cs-137, Na-22, and Ti-44 were correctly classified in all six trials each, whereas Ba-133 and Eu-152 were incorrectly classified in all six trials each. These results show lower accuracy in robotic trials than in the held-out test set.

</details>


### [63] [GOFusion: Real-Time Gaussian-Occupancy Submap Fusion for Fisher-Informed Autonomous Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25261)
*Boxin Bu,Simon Boche,Haoming Zhang,Jaehyung Jung,Jiaxin Wei,Hannah Schieber,Daniel Roth,Angela P. Schoellig,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOFusion 是一个面向自主机器人导航的实时视觉建图系统，将高斯图元锚定到局部占据栅格地图上，并利用栅格化器内嵌的 Fisher 近似实现信息驱动的高斯剪枝与下一最佳视角规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航既需要用于路径规划的空间/几何感知，又需要高保真外观信息，但现有表示难以同时兼顾；同时 3DGS 大规模优化存在显存受限与位姿误差下几何-外观不一致的问题，且缺少可在线量化不确定性以指导主动感知的手段。

Method: 用视觉惯性 SLAM 的位姿估计与局部占据栅格地图构建 3D 高斯地图，把高斯图元锚定到局部占据地图上；支持位姿校正以保持几何与视觉一致性；仅对活跃子图进行 3DGS 优化以限制 GPU 内存；提出与光栅化器集成的 Fisher 近似，在线量化每个图元的敏感度，作为信息驱动的高斯剪枝与下一最佳视角规划启发式。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实无人机飞行中评估：建图质量优于多个基线，可在规模化场景下实现实时、高保真且不确定性感知的建图；并利用 Fisher 启发式实现了全自主无人机探索以精化高斯地图。

Conclusion: 将高斯图元锚定于占据地图并结合活跃子图优化与在线 Fisher 敏感度，可在资源受限的机器人平台上统一几何、外观与不确定性建模，支撑信息驱动的自主探索与建图。

Abstract: In this letter, we present GOFusion, a real-time visual mapping system for autonomous robot navigation that anchors Gaussian primitives to local occupancy maps, providing both spatial awareness for path planning and high-fidelity appearance that enables information-driven robot navigation. GOFusion builds 3D Gaussian maps from pose estimates and local occupancy maps obtained via visual-inertial SLAM and enables pose corrections to maintain both geometric and visual consistency. Furthermore, it restricts the 3D Gaussian Splatting optimization to active submaps to bound GPU memory usage. Building on the appearance-rich Gaussian map, we introduce a rasterizer-integrated Fisher approximation that quantifies per-primitive sensitivity online, serving as an information-driven heuristic for Gaussian pruning and next-best-view planning. We evaluate GOFusion on public datasets and in real-world drone flights. It not only outperforms several baselines in mapping quality, but also achieves real-time, high-fidelity and uncertainty-aware mapping at scale. Finally, we demonstrate fully autonomous drone exploration for Gaussian map refinement using the proposed Fisher-informed heuristic. Code is available at https://github.com/Bennnoo/GOFusion. We provide a supplementary video demonstration at https://youtu.be/pLQKbxQvb6I.

</details>


### [64] [The Bitter Lesson Comes to Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25287)
*Christopher Leet,Achyutha Menon,Sravanthi Machcha,Sven Koenig,Tzu Kit Chan,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文首次在真实硬件上系统评测了七款前沿多模态语言模型直接控制机械臂的能力，发现它们已能完成多种操作任务，但在实际动作预算内仍不够精确高效；不过能力正快速提升，性能翻倍时间约44.5天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态语言模型日益具备直接控制机械臂的能力，追踪这一进展对理解其潜在收益（如提升工业自动化）与风险（如被恶意利用制造危险化学品或生物制剂）都很重要，但目前缺乏在统一任务套件和真实硬件上的系统比较。

Method: 选取RoboDojo基准中的六项操作任务，在多种推理强度设置下评测七款前沿多模态语言模型，让它们通过指令控制末端执行器位姿来操作真实机械臂，并与专门化方法进行对比分析。

Result: 当前前沿模型能直接控制真实机械臂完成多种操作任务，但精度与效率尚不足以在实用动作预算内完成任务。2026年7月至9月间，基准性能提升对应约44.5天的翻倍时间；最佳执行成功率提升4.4倍，最低执行成本下降6.2倍。

Conclusion: 这些趋势提示"苦涩教训"可能正降临机器人领域，具身通用语言模型的能力未来或可与专门化模型相媲美，因而需持续监测其能力进展及相应风险。

Abstract: General purpose multimodal language models (LMs) are increasingly capable of directly controlling robotic arms (e.g., by commanding end effector poses) to manipulate their environment. Tracking the development of these capabilities is important for understanding their potential benefits, such as improving industrial automation, and risks, such as enabling malicious users to produce dangerous chemical or biological agents. To this end, we investigate the ability of frontier multimodal LMs to directly control physical robotic arms. We evaluate seven models at multiple reasoning-effort settings on six manipulation tasks taken from the RoboDojo benchmark. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic comparison of a broad range of frontier LMs across a common suite of manipulation tasks on physical hardware. We find that current frontier models can directly control real robotic arms to perform a variety of manipulation tasks but are not yet precise or efficient enough to complete them within a practical action budget. However, these capabilities are improving rapidly: performance increases on our benchmark between July and September 2026 (inclusive) correspond to a performance doubling time of 44.5 days, while the best recorded execution rate increased by a factor of 4.4 and the lowest recorded execution cost fell by a factor of 6.2. These trends raise the possibility that the Bitter Lesson is coming to robotics; the capabilities of embodied general purpose LMs may one day be comparable to those of more specialized models.

</details>


### [65] [Vision as Proprioception: Estimating Humanoid Robot Pose from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25288)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Matthias Heyrman,René Zurbrügg,Marco Hutter,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HumanoidPose 框架，把人形机器人视频当作本体感觉（vision as proprioception），仅从单目视频估计全身关节在世界坐标系下的构型；用人体姿态预训练特征+机器人运动学统一建模，将人体动作重定向到 16 种人形本体以合成数据训练，并用无标注网络视频自训练获得 2D 机器人姿态检测器；在 3 个真实人形平台上关节角误差较最优基线降低 18–32%，并改善全局定位与对未见本体的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人交互时其机载本体状态常因隐私、所有权或通信限制而不可得；在线与 AI 生成的机器人视频数据丰富却缺少对应关节配置，难以直接用于机器人学习。因此需要仅凭视频恢复人形机器人全身关节状态的方法。

Method: 1) 将预训练人体姿态特征与机器人运动学先验结合于统一模型，为每种本体预测关节构型；2) 将人体动作重定向到 16 种不同人形本体，实现完全基于合成数据的可扩展学习；3) 利用无标注在线视频做自训练，得到 2D 机器人姿态检测器以提升真实世界泛化；4) 在相机/世界空间下输出全身关节位置与角度。

Result: 关节角与关节位置精度均优于所比较的基线；在三个真实人形平台上，关节角误差相对各自最佳基线降低 18–32%；全局定位精度提升，并能泛化到未见过的本体；可支撑交互过程中对其他机器人的视觉理解以及从网络/生成视频中提取机器人运动。

Conclusion: 视频可作为人形机器人的本体感觉来源，HumanoidPose 为“从视频估计机器人内部状态”这一此前未被探索的问题建立了可行且可扩展的范式，兼具真实平台精度与跨本体、跨数据来源（在线视频、生成视频）的实用性。

Abstract: Estimating a humanoid robot's internal state from video has a wide range of real-world applications but remains unexplored. During interactions with a robot, its onboard state information may be unavailable due to privacy, ownership, or communication constraints. Meanwhile, online and AI-generated robot videos provide rich sources of data but typically lack corresponding joint configurations, limiting their direct use in robot learning. To address these issues, we present HumanoidPose, a new framework that treats vision as proprioception to recover full-body joint configurations in world space from video alone. Specifically, we combine pretrained human-pose features with robot kinematics in a unified model that predicts each embodiment's joint configurations. We retarget human motions to sixteen different humanoid embodiments, enabling scalable learning entirely from synthetic data. To improve real-world generalization, we further leverage unlabeled online videos through self-training to develop a 2D robot-pose detector. In our evaluation, HumanoidPose improves joint-angle and joint-position accuracy over the evaluated baselines and reduces joint-angle error by 18-32% relative to the best-performing baseline on each of the three real humanoid platforms. We further show improved global localization and generalization to unseen embodiments. HumanoidPose enables visual understanding of other robots during interaction and extraction of robot motion from online and generated videos.

</details>


### [66] [TILDE: Teacher-in-the-Loop Distillation from Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25376)
*Yuxiang Lu,Yantong Liu,Jinghua Hou,Xueli Sun,Ruihua Han,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TILDE 框架与 AutoDAgger 自动数据聚合流程，将大规模动作预训练的具身基础模型能力蒸馏到无预训练的学生策略中，无需人工监控即可采集纠错轨迹，任务性能最高达行为克隆的 4.1 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模动作预训练能让具身基础模型（EFMs）获得强的操作能力，尤其在泛化和闭环鲁棒性方面突出；但复现这类预训练需要大量机器人数据和算力。因此希望把预训练 EFMs 的能力迁移到没有经过动作预训练的策略上。

Method: 提出 TILDE（Teacher-In-the-Loop Distillation from EFMs）：在学生诱导的状态上做在线策略蒸馏，并结合学习教师执行的恢复轨迹；进一步提出 AutoDAgger 自动数据聚合流程，用机器人奖励模型监控任务进度并触发教师干预，从而无需人工监控或手动操控即可采集恢复经验。方法统一了从示教学习、在线策略教师监督与教师纠错行为三类学习信号。

Result: AutoDAgger 相比人在环方法将恢复轨迹采集速度提升 2 倍以上；在三个真实世界任务中，TILDE 相比行为克隆将任务性能最高提升 4.1 倍，并支持有效的跨架构蒸馏。

Conclusion: TILDE 与 AutoDAgger 提供了一条无需动作预训练即可继承预训练 EFMs 操作能力的低成本路径，同时实现自动化纠错数据采集与跨架构蒸馏，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large-scale action pretraining equips Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) with strong manipulation capabilities, with particular strengths in generalization and closed-loop robustness, but reproducing such pretraining requires substantial robot data and computation. To transfer these capabilities from pretrained EFMs to policies without action pretraining, we introduce TILDE (Teacher-In-the-Loop Distillation from EFMs), which synergizes on-policy distillation at student-induced states with learning from teacher-executed recovery trajectories. We further propose AutoDAgger, an autonomous data aggregation pipeline in which a robotic reward model is utilized to monitor task progress and trigger teacher intervention, thereby collecting recovery experience without human monitoring or manual control. AutoDAgger accelerates recovery-trajectory collection by more than $2\times$ compared with the human-in-the-loop method. By unifying learning from demonstrations, on-policy teacher supervision, and teacher's corrective behavior, TILDE improves task performance by up to $4.1\times$ over behavior cloning across three real-world tasks and supports effective cross-architecture distillation.

</details>


### [67] [Visual-Inertial Odometry for Ground Robots with Dynamic Camera-IMU Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25397)
*Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对视觉惯性里程计中相机-IMU刚性安装假设的局限，提出基于D-H约定的动态外参标定方法，结合机器人运动学模型与两步初始化策略，实现在线估计时变相机-IMU外参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉惯性里程计系统通常假设传感器刚性安装，无法适应存在结构运动（如关节运动）的实际场景，限制了适应性，因此需要研究时变相机-IMU外参标定。

Method: 1）引入机器人运动学模型，提出基于Denavit-Hartenberg（D-H）约定的动态相机-IMU外参标定方法；2）设计两步初始化策略，高效估计初始关节角度，为后续外参优化提供可靠初值，从而支持时变相机-IMU外参的在线估计。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该框架能够有效适应相机-IMU外参的变化，同时保持有竞争力的定位精度。

Conclusion: 通过将运动学模型与D-H参数化引入标定流程，可突破刚性安装假设，实现时变外参的在线标定，为结构运动场景下的视觉惯性里程计提供可行方案；目前验证限于仿真。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry systems are often constrained by the assumption that sensors are rigidly mounted, which limits their adaptability in practical scenarios involving structural motion. To address this limitation, this paper investigates time-varying camera-IMU extrinsic calibration. First, a dynamic camera-IMU extrinsic calibration method based on the Denavit-Hartenberg convention is proposed by incorporating the robot kinematic model. Second, a two-step initialization strategy is developed to efficiently estimate the initial joint angles, providing a reliable starting point for subsequent extrinsic parameter optimization and enabling online estimation of timevarying camera-IMU extrinsic parameters. Simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can effectively accommodate variations in camera-IMU extrinsic parameters while maintaining competitive localization accuracy.

</details>


### [68] [Energy-Score Stabilized Attentive Mixture-Density Policy: A Lightweight Approach to Multimodal Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25401)
*Sachiya Fujita,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Shigeki Sugano,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带空间注意力的混合密度策略，用Energy Score（对角协方差下1D CRPS之和）替代NLL训练，单次前向即可显式输出高斯混合分布，实现低推理成本、多模态且泛化的运动生成，并在8自由度机械臂上优于Diffusion Policy 15-25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际运动生成需要同时满足三点：处理多模态性（同一观测下多个正确动作）、保持低推理成本、以及在少量演示下的强泛化能力。扩散类策略推理开销大，而传统混合密度网络用NLL训练易出现奇异性和梯度尺度不稳定。

Method: 设计显式空间注意力与混合密度输出耦合的策略网络：单次前向输出关节命令（可选注意力点）的高斯混合参数；采用Energy Score优化，即在对角协方差假设下计算1D CRPS之和作为损失，取代负对数似然，以避免MDN奇异性和梯度尺度不稳定。

Result: 在8自由度机械臂上能生成有区分度的避障轨迹并稳定避障学习；在随机化移动物体抓取任务中，面对需要时空多模态与快速时序决策的场景，在多种未见条件下比Diffusion Policy高15-25%，展现更好的泛化性与实时可行性。

Conclusion: 显式概率分布建模加Energy Score训练可在单次前向推理下兼顾多模态、低延迟与有限数据泛化，是一种实用且优于扩散策略的运动生成方案。

Abstract: For practical motion generation, it is essential to handle multimodality (multiple actions can be correct for the same observation) while maintaining low inference cost and strong generalization from limited demonstrations. We introduce an attentive mixture-density policy trained with the Energy Score that represents future visuomotor trajectories as explicit probability distributions with a single forward pass. Concretely, we output Gaussian mixture parameters for joint commands (and optionally attention points) and optimize them with the Energy Score (computed as a sum of 1D CRPS under a diagonal covariance assumption) instead of the negative log-likelihood (NLL) to avoid MDN singularities and gradient-scale instabilities. Our architecture couples explicit spatial attention with mixture-density outputs, enabling robust multimodal behavior selection under visual noise and small-data regimes. On an 8-DOF manipulator, the method generates distinct avoidance trajectories and stabilizes learning in obstacle avoidance. Moreover, in a randomized moving-object grasping task, where spatiotemporal multimodality and fast timing decisions are required, the proposed model outperforms Diffusion Policy by 15-25% under several unseen conditions, demonstrating superior generalization and real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [69] [AeroDASH: Asynchronous Vision-Language-Action Control for Continuous UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25407)
*Zhe Liu,Yuxuan Zheng,Jinzhe Qin,Ziqi Gao,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异步VLA框架AeroDASH，通过样条策略初始化、轨迹融合与执行感知GRPO，缓解机载推理延迟造成的轨迹与飞行运动错配，在UAV-Flow上达到76.56%成功率并提升飞行平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机机载推理存在延迟，导致VLA策略预测的轨迹与实际持续的运动之间产生错配；同步式的航点序列跟踪在推理期间会中断或产生抖动，不适用于连续的语言条件飞行。

Method: 1）以专家示教做监督微调，初始化B样条策略，在位置与偏航上输出连续轨迹参考；2）执行阶段采用轨迹融合：剔除已过期的预测前缀，将剩余段与当前激活参考以导数连续方式连接，同时限制轨迹形变；3）针对异步执行引入偏差，使用轨迹级奖励的执行感知GRPO进一步适配策略。

Result: 在UAV-Flow测试集上成功率76.56%，分别比OpenVLA-UAV和去掉GRPO的AeroDASH高38.1和20.1个百分点；执行实验表明推理期间仍保持运动，相比异步航点序列跟踪降低了加速度、加加速度和速度波动；并实机演示了机载推理下的语言条件飞行。

Conclusion: 异步执行配合连续轨迹表示与执行感知强化学习，可有效缓解推理延迟带来的错配，实现平滑且连续的无人机语言条件飞行；代码将在论文录用后开源。

Abstract: Onboard inference latency creates a mismatch between the trajectories predicted by vision-language-action (VLA) policies and the ongoing motion of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). We present AeroDASH, an asynchronous VLA framework for continuous language-conditioned flight. A B-spline policy is initialized through supervised fine-tuning on expert demonstrations, providing continuous trajectory references in position and yaw. During execution, trajectory fusion removes the expired prediction prefix and connects the remaining segment to the active reference with derivative continuity while limiting trajectory deformation. To account for deviations induced by asynchronous execution, execution-aware group relative policy optimization (GRPO) further adapts the policy using trajectory-level rewards. On the UAV-Flow test set, AeroDASH achieves a success rate of 76.56%, exceeding OpenVLA-UAV and AeroDASH w/o GRPO by 38.1 and 20.1 percentage points, respectively. Execution experiments show that AeroDASH sustains motion during inference and reduces acceleration, jerk, and speed fluctuations relative to asynchronous tracking of waypoint sequences. We further demonstrate language-conditioned flight with onboard inference and asynchronous trajectory execution. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [70] [T(R,O) Grasp++: Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping in Cluttered Environments at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25410)
*Xin Fei,Zhixuan Xu,Huaicong Fang,Fangjun Chen,Zhaoyu Mu,Rui Yang,Zhexi Luo,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出迄今最大规模的灵巧抓取仿真基准（约0.5B抓取标注、8种灵巧手、约5万物体），并给出可扩展的扩散式跨本体灵巧抓取生成框架T(R,O) Grasp++，在杂乱场景下8种本体平均成功率88%，推理延迟仅0.3s，样本效率提升10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱场景中的灵巧抓取是机器人操作的核心难题：需在高维手部关节空间内同时满足复杂物体-环境约束，且不同灵巧手结构差异导致跨本体迁移困难。现有工作缺乏大规模标注数据与高效的跨本体生成方法。

Method: 一方面构建大规模仿真基准，覆盖8种灵巧手、约5万物体、约0.5B抓取标注；另一方面提出T(R,O) Grasp++——基于扩散模型的跨本体灵巧抓取生成框架，面向杂乱环境中的抓取合成，具备可扩展性与高推理效率。

Result: 在8种灵巧本体上平均成功率达88%，显著优于现有基线；推理延迟仅0.3s，推理样本效率提升逾10倍。

Conclusion: 大规模仿真标注与扩散式跨本体生成框架相结合，可有效解决杂乱场景下的灵巧抓取合成问题，并在成功率与效率上同时取得优势。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in cluttered scenes remains a central challenge in robotic manipulation, as it requires high-dimensional hand coordination under complex object-environment constraints. To address this problem, we introduce one of the largest simulation benchmarks to date, comprising around 0.5B grasp annotations across eight dexterous hands and approximately 50K objects. Furthermore, we propose T(R,O) Grasp++, a scalable diffusion-based framework for cross-embodiment dexterous grasp synthesis in cluttered environments.
