Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average success rate of 88% across eight dexterous embodiments. Meanwhile, our pipeline achieves only 0.3s inference latency while improving inference sample efficiency by over 10 times.

</details>


### [71] [Learning When to Adapt: Reactivity-Conditioned Residual Adaptation under Human-Robot Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25419)
*Immanuel R. Santjoko,Benedictus C. G. Cinun,Jing Chen,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RCRA在线自适应框架,用Transformer估计行人反应性并据此门控LoRA模块,在冻结的保守导航策略上调制残差行为,兼顾安全与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人是否会对机器人作出反应因人而异,但许多强化学习策略假定所有行人均不反应以保证安全,导致策略要么过度保守,要么依赖实际不存在的合作行为。

Method: 以冻结的保守导航策略为骨干,学习残差进行调制;用基于Transformer的估计器从运动历史与机器人上下文推断潜在的行人反应性;将估计结果在空间上聚合为自适应尺度,用于门控LoRA模块,使策略在反应型行人附近表现合作、在非反应型行人附近表现保守。

Result: 估计器的场景平均仿真准确率较假设已知行人目标的贝叶斯基线提升12.62个百分点;在每个场景成功率均超过90%的基线中,RCRA相对保守骨干降低16.7%的场景平均路径长度,相对随机化训练基线降低7.3%;相对反应型训练策略,平均成功率提升14.88个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式估计并利用行人反应性可有效缓解统一假设带来的过度保守或误判合作问题,在保持高成功率的同时显著缩短路径长度。

Abstract: Pedestrians differ in whether they respond to a robot, yet many RL policies assume all are non-reactive to prioritize safety. Policies trained without conditioning on local pedestrians' reactivity may therefore be unnecessarily conservative or rely on cooperation that does not occur. We propose RCRA, an online adaptation framework that modulates a learned residual around a frozen conservative navigation policy according to estimated pedestrian reactivity. A Transformer-based estimator infers latent pedestrian reactivity from motion histories and robot context, providing a more accurate signal for navigation adaptation and improving scenario-averaged simulation accuracy by $12.62$ percentage points over the Bayesian baseline, which assumes known pedestrian goals. These estimates are spatially aggregated into an adaptation scale that gates LoRA modules, enabling cooperative behavior near reactive pedestrians and conservative behavior near non-reactive pedestrians. Among baselines achieving SR above $90\%$ in every scenario, RCRA reduces scenario-averaged path length by $16.7\%$ relative to the conservative backbone and by $7.3\%$ relative to randomized-training baselines. It improves mean SR by $14.88$ percentage points over the reactive-trained policy.

</details>


### [72] [SurGPT: A Multi-Task Foundation Model for Robotic Surgical Skills Assessment and Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25426)
*Parham M. Kebria,Saeid Nahavandi,Mehrdad Saif,Fernando Bello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个机器人手术技能评估基础模型，融合视觉编码器与Transformer，同时完成手术任务分类、技能等级评估和手术动作自然语言描述生成三任务，并在da Vinci技能模拟器数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人手术技能评估依赖人类专家的主观评分，成为外科教育中的瓶颈，且难以规模化扩展，亟需自动化、客观且可解释的评估手段。

Method: 构建视觉编码器+Transformer的基础模型，处理da Vinci手术机器人视频序列；采用多任务学习框架，联合优化三类任务（打结/穿针/缝合等手术任务分类、标准化量表技能等级评估、手术动作自然语言描述生成）；引入自适应不确定性加权平衡各目标，并通过位置编码实现时序理解、注意力机制实现时空特征融合。

Result: 在da Vinci技能模拟器数据集上，手术任务识别达到当前最优性能；技能评估结果与专家评价具有相关性，具备可解释性；模型可生成手术动作的自然语言描述。

Conclusion: 多任务基础模型能够实现自动化、客观且可解释的机器人手术技能评估与动作理解，为自动化外科教育、实时反馈系统和技能认证中的客观评估开辟新可能。

Abstract: The assessment of surgical skills in robotic surgery training has traditionally relied on subjective evaluations by human experts, creating bottlenecks in surgical education and limiting scalability. We present a novel foundation model that automatically analyzes robotic surgical videos to provide comprehensive skill assessment and gesture understanding. Combining a visual encoder with a transformer architecture, the model performs three key tasks simultaneously: 1) surgical task classification (knot tying, needle passing, suturing), 2) skill level assessment on a standardized scale, and 3) natural language generation of surgical gesture descriptions. The model processes video sequences from the da Vinci surgical robot and generates human-readable text descriptions of surgical actions, similar to how language models generate text from prompts, using a multi-task learning framework with adaptive uncertainty weighting to optimize performance across all objectives. Our transformer-based architecture incorporates positional encoding for temporal understanding and attention mechanisms for spatiotemporal feature integration. Evaluated on the da Vinci Skills Simulator dataset, the proposed framework demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in surgical task recognition and provides interpretable skill assessments that correlate with expert evaluations, with the model's ability to generate natural language descriptions of surgical gestures opening new possibilities for automated surgical education, real-time feedback systems, and objective skill assessment in surgical training and certification programs.

</details>


### [73] [Intent-Informed Model Predictive Control for Shared Autonomy in Remote Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25436)
*Lorena Maria Genua,Camilla Tabasso,Mya Schroder,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多模态意图推断信念分布熵的动态仲裁策略，将人类输入与自主控制融合为MPC问题，实现从直接遥操作到完全自主的连续平滑过渡，并在实验中显著优于两个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享自主将人类的认知能力与机器人的精确性结合，以在复杂环境中实现有效的远程操作。核心挑战在于如何利用从人类、任务和/或环境中提取的信息来自适应地调整自主等级。现有方法往往难以在多等级之间实现连续、平滑的自主度切换。

Method: 用动态贝叶斯网络（DBN）建模人类意图，在遥操作中基于实时操作员注视数据和机械臂运动更新对目标的信念分布；进而以该信念分布的熵作为仲裁信号动态调整自主等级，并将人类输入与自主控制的混合建模为模型预测控制（MPC）问题，由信念分布引导系统自主行为。

Result: 与两个基于MPC的共享自主基线方法相比，所提方法在物体获取时间上至少减少61%，轨迹长度减少44%，人类控制输入减少60%。参与者报告了较高的感知控制感、可预测性、意图识别度和响应性，同时工作负荷较低。

Conclusion: 基于多模态意图推断信念熵的仲裁策略能够实现自主等级的连续渐变调节，在效率、人机输入负担和用户体验方面均取得显著改善，验证了熵驱动仲裁在共享自主远程操作中的有效性。

Abstract: Shared autonomy is a control paradigm that combines human cognitive abilities with robot precision to enable effective remote manipulation in complex environments. A key challenge is how to adapt the level of autonomy using information extracted from the human, task, and/or environment. In this paper, we propose a novel arbitration strategy that dynamically adjusts the level of autonomy in the system using the entropy of a belief distribution derived from a multimodal intent inference. Human intent is modeled with a Dynamic Bayesian Network (DBN), which updates beliefs over goals given real-time operator gaze data and robot arm motion during teleoperation. We then formulate the blending of human input and autonomous control as a Model Predictive Control (MPC) problem, where the system's autonomous behavior is guided by the belief distribution. This approach enables continuous, gradual transitions across the full spectrum of autonomy levels, from direct teleoperation to full autonomy. To validate our approach, we conducted experiments to compare the performance of our proposed algorithm against two MPC-based shared autonomy methods. Compared to the baselines, our approach achieved reductions of at least 61% in object retrieval time, 44% in trajectory length, and 60% in human control input. Participants reported high perceived control, predictability, intent recognition, and responsiveness, along with low workload.

</details>


### [74] [Self-Crystallizing Behavior Trees: Amortizing Foundation-Model Reasoning into Deterministic Control for Autonomous Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25455)
*Behnam Moradi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自结晶行为树 SCBT：让基础模型充当"编译器"而非运行时控制器，仅在行为树节点失败时生成最小补丁，经 CMA-ES 参数调优、上下文老虎机成本决策与三阶段准入后存入机队库，最终结晶为确定性行为树；在 AirSim 中仅用 11 次 LLM 调用即把任务成功率从 12% 提升到 97%，接近手写专家树的 98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典机器人控制（FSM、行为树）确定、可审查，但在设计期固定，遇到未预见情形即失效；基础模型能在运行时合成新控制逻辑，却带来延迟与非确定性，难以被安全关键航空系统接受。现有 LLM 驱动方法让模型永久留在回路中并丢弃其产出，成本高且不留资产。因此需要一种"模型离线化、成果可沉淀"的机制。

Method: 核心思想是把基础模型当编译器而非运行时。节点失败时，LLM 提出最小且与任务无关的子树补丁；CMA-ES 在仿真中调优补丁参数；上下文老虎机在"调用存量补丁"与"新发起 LLM 调用"之间做成本权衡；三阶段准入管线（静态检查、不变量监控回放、相对未打补丁树的经验裕度）只接纳可证明恢复的补丁；通过补丁进入机队库，待 LLM 调用率衰减后结晶为确定性行为树。实现基于 Microsoft Project AirSim，采用模式约束 LLM 流水线（Gemini 3.1 Pro）与锁步仿真器，在包含阵风与电池故障的 20 回合学习阶段、三个种子下评测。

Result: SCBT 平均仅用 11±1.4 次 LLM 调用，整体重生成需 26 次、无机队库需 22 次；学习后半段降至每回合 0.33 次调用。结晶树分别 28 与 33 节点、各含两个补丁，在完全关闭学习的情况下达到 97±2% 任务成功率，未打补丁树仅 12%，手写专家树 98%，且离线重新 tick 时可精确复现决策轨迹。两个负结果：回放准入把风致地理围栏越界归因于补丁，误拒了有效的风恢复补丁；纯静态消融反而在留出故障上泛化更好。

Conclusion: 将基础模型限制在设计/修复回路之外、让其产出经准入后固化为确定性行为树，可以在保持可审查性与可复现性的同时获得接近专家手写的性能，并把 LLM 调用成本压到极低。补丁库与结晶机制是效率来源；但准入判定的因果归因与准入阶段的取舍仍是开放问题，需在更广故障谱与真实飞行条件下验证。

Abstract: Classical robot control, Finite State Machines and Behavior Trees (BTs), is deterministic and inspectable but fixed at design time: it fails on situations its designers did not anticipate. Foundation models can synthesize new control logic at runtime, at a cost in latency and non-determinism hard to accept in safety-critical aerial systems. Existing LLM-driven approaches keep the model in the loop permanently and discard what it produces. We propose Self-Crystallizing Behavior Trees (SCBT), in which the foundation model is a compiler rather than a runtime. When a BT node fails, an LLM proposes a minimal, mission-agnostic subtree patch; CMA-ES tunes its parameters in simulation; a contextual bandit decides whether a stored patch or a new LLM call is worth its cost; and a three-stage admission pipeline (static check, invariant-monitored replays, an empirical margin over the unpatched tree) admits only patches that demonstrably recover. Admitted patches are stored in a fleet library and, once the LLM call rate decays, crystallized into a deterministic BT that no human wrote. We implement SCBT on Microsoft Project AirSim with a schema-constrained LLM pipeline (Gemini 3.1 Pro) and a lockstep simulator. Over three seeds of a 20-episode learning phase with wind-gust and battery failures, SCBT used $11\pm1.4$ LLM calls against 26 for whole-tree regeneration and 22 without a library, with 0.33 calls per episode in the second half. The crystallized trees, 28 and 33 nodes with two patches each, reached $97\pm2\%$ mission success with no learning running, against 12\% for the unpatched tree and 98\% for a hand-coded expert tree, and reproduce decision traces exactly when re-ticked offline. Two negative results: replay admission rejected useful wind recoveries by attributing wind-induced geofence excursions to the patch, and a static-only ablation generalized better to held-out failures.

</details>


### [75] [BiDex-100: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation Across 100+ Simulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25459)
*Yong Wang,Kecheng Zheng,Xing Zhu,Chao Xu,Yujun Shen,Shengze Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 BiDex-100，一个面向双臂灵巧操作的仿真基准，包含 106 个任务、五个能力维度的评测、两种高自由度灵巧手具身以及 8,080 条成功验证的遥操作演示数据，并对七个近期 VLA 模型进行了统一评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人动作模型研究虽日益关注双臂灵巧操作，但缺乏专门针对灵巧操作的基准，导致现有模型的灵巧操作能力无法被充分刻画与比较。

Method: 构建仿真基准：106 个任务沿泛化、指令跟随、少演示、长时序、记忆五个能力轴组织；每个任务配备具有受控视觉与空间变化的域外变体以评估鲁棒性；支持两种具身，提供 8,080 条成功验证的遥操作演示（共 625 万帧）；在统一协议下评测七个近期 VLA 模型。

Result: 建立了按能力维度划分的性能基线、鲁棒性基线与数据效率基线，系统刻画了现有 VLA 模型在双臂灵巧操作上的表现。

Conclusion: BiDex-100 填补了灵巧操作基准的空白，提供广泛任务覆盖、多维评测与高自由度手具身，为后续机器人动作模型研究提供可复现的评测平台与参照基线。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation with dexterous hands has attracted growing attention in research on robotic action models. However, few studies have developed benchmarks specifically tailored for dexterous manipulation, leaving the dexterous manipulation capabilities of existing models insufficiently characterized. In this paper, we present BiDex-100, a simulation benchmark for bimanual dexterous manipulation that combines broad task coverage, multi-dimensional evaluation, and high-DoF hand embodiments. Concretely, the benchmark comprises 106 tasks organized along five capability axes: generalization, instruction following, limited demonstrations, long horizon, and memory. Each task is paired with an out-of-domain variant featuring controlled visual and spatial changes, allowing systematic assessment of robustness. In addition, the benchmark supports two embodiments and provides 8,080 success-validated teleoperated demonstrations, totaling 6.25 million frames. We evaluate seven recent Vision-Language-Action models under a unified protocol, establishing baselines for capability-specific performance, robustness, and data efficiency. The website is available at https://BiDex-100.github.io/BiDex-100/.

</details>


### [76] [CusADi2: Latency-Optimized Batch Computation of Robot Kinematics, Jacobians, and Hessians on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25476)
*John Viljoen,Lasse Peters,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CusADi2：在批内并行以降低轨迹优化中动力学函数批量评估的延迟，在 MuJoCo Menagerie 全部 67 个机器人上优于现有 GPU 框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹优化（从零阶采样到一阶、二阶梯度方法）需大量评估动力学系统函数（正运动学及其 Jacobian、Hessian）。这些评估在采样轨迹或时间步之间彼此独立，可批量化。在线决策要求低延迟，即每批须尽快完成。现有 GPU 框架主要对批实例做并行以优化吞吐，而非延迟。

Method: 提出软件库 CusADi2，在并行批实例之外，额外并行化单个批实例内部的计算，以降低批量评估延迟。

Result: 在 MuJoCo Menagerie 全部 67 个机器人（机械臂、灵巧手、四足、人形、生物力学模型）上基准测试正运动学、Jacobian 与 Hessian，对比 MuJoCo XLA、MuJoCo Warp、frax、CusADi 与 CasADi：批大小 64–4096 时延迟均低于所有基线，更大批时性能持平；并在采样型控制应用中展示优势。

Conclusion: 通过批内并行可显著降低批量函数评估延迟，适合低延迟在线轨迹优化；代码已开源。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization methods, from sampling-based zero-order to gradient-based first- and second-order approaches, require many evaluations of dynamical system functions, i.e., functions derived from a robot's dynamical model, such as forward kinematics and their Jacobians and Hessians. These evaluations are largely independent, e.g., across sampled trajectories or time steps, and can thus be evaluated in parallel as a batch. When used to make decisions online, these methods require low latency, i.e., each batch must complete in as little wall-clock time as possible. Existing frameworks utilize modern GPU hardware primarily to parallelize across batch instances, thereby optimizing throughput but not latency. We present CusADi2, a software library that reduces the batch evaluation latency by additionally parallelizing computation within each batch instance. We benchmark forward kinematics, Jacobians, and Hessians for all 67~robots in the MuJoCo Menagerie, including arms, hands, quadrupeds, humanoids, and biomechanical models, against MuJoCo XLA, MuJoCo Warp, frax, CusADi, and CasADi. CusADi2 achieves lower latency than all baselines at batch sizes of 64 to 4096, while matching them at larger batch sizes. We also demonstrate the performance advantage of CusADi2 in a sampling-based control application. All code is open-source at: https://github.com/johnviljoen/cusadi2

</details>


### [77] [AQUAACT: A Joystick-Level Underwater Teleoperation Dataset and Closed-Loop Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25534)
*Zhenqi Wu,Trung Tien Dong,Aditya Penumarti,Mohamed A. Fouad,Menna A. Fouad,Mingyang Mao,Jane Jaejeong Shin,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文发布首个公开的语言标注水下机器人数据集AQUAACT，将相机图像与人类摇杆指令配对，用于研究水下视觉-语言-动作（VLA）自主控制，并给出闭环基准表明任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人任务（结构巡检、珊瑚礁测绘、伴潜等）目前多依赖人工操控，而陆地VLA模型已能从语言指令实现自主作业，因此有潜力替代驾驶员。但水下缺乏训练此类模型的数据，故需构建带语言标注的示教数据集。

Method: 借鉴现有飞行员操作方式（观看相机图像、用摇杆操控、飞控将指令转为运动），录制人类驾驶员操作，构建AQUAACT数据集：1.9小时真实世界演示 + 17.9小时仿真演示，均含相机图像、人类摇杆指令及惯性测量。随后开展仿真到真实迁移实验，并在仿真中建立七个策略（模仿学习基线至VLA模型，均用默认配方训练）的闭环基准。

Result: 仿真演示可迁移到真实数据，并大幅减少策略所需的真实演示数量；闭环基准显示没有策略完成超过五分之一的任务回合；在陆地数据上预训练的大型VLA模型相对从头训练的小策略并无优势。

Conclusion: 水下自主操控任务仍远未解决，陆地预训练的VLA模型在水下存在尚待弥合的差距；AQUAACT为该方向提供了公开的数据基础与评测基准。

Abstract: Underwater robots support a growing range of work, from inspecting offshore structures and pipelines to surveying reefs and accompanying divers. Most of these tasks, however, are still flown by hand by a pilot, which raises the question of whether they can be carried out autonomously instead. On land, vision-language-action (VLA) models already let robots carry out tasks autonomously from language instructions, which makes them a natural candidate for taking the pilot's place. Underwater, however, there is little data to train such models. We take inspiration from how these tasks are flown today: the pilot watches the camera image and steers with a joystick, whose commands the flight controller turns into motion. We therefore record the pilots and introduce AQUAACT, to our knowledge the first publicly available language-annotated underwater dataset pairing camera images with human joystick commands. It comprises 1.9 hours of real-world and 17.9 hours of simulated demonstrations, both flown by human pilots, together with the vehicle's inertial measurements. Experiments with AQUAACT show that its simulated demonstrations transfer to real data and greatly reduce the real demonstrations a policy needs. A closed-loop benchmark of seven policies in simulation, from imitation-learning baselines to VLA models, each trained with its default recipe, shows that these tasks remain far from solved: no policy completes more than a fifth of the episodes. Pretraining on land data gives large VLA models no advantage over small policies trained from scratch, a gap that land-trained models have yet to close underwater.

</details>


### [78] [Adaptive Bendpoint Formation in Octopus-Inspired Soft Continuum Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25585)
*Jessica Gumowski,Julian Finn,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,David Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受章鱼启发的腱驱动软体臂，通过在臂上任意位置充气膨胀外壳来"卡住"腱，形成临时锚点，从而自适应地改变驱动段长度与曲率，实现单腱即可分区调控的高灵巧度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 章鱼依靠柔软身体展现出强大的具身智能，其关键机制之一是能在臂上任意位置形成弯曲点，从而实现灵巧行为。传统单腱连续体机械臂的驱动段固定，工作空间与灵巧性受限；而多段多驱动器方案虽能分区控制，却带来显著的机械复杂度。因此作者希望以简单结构复现章鱼"任意位置设锚点"的能力。

Method: 在腱驱动软体臂上集成一组可充气外壳阵列。对某一位置的外壳充气，使其膨胀并增大该处摩擦，从而夹紧（jamming）腱，形成临时的新锚点，等效于改变腱的锚定端点，进而改变臂的曲率。该设计不破坏驱动段与松弛段之间臂的连续性，仅凭单根腱和单一驱动即可通过选择性充气实现不同区域的独立曲率调节。

Result: 实验表明，驱动段呈现出非恒定的钟形曲率，与真实章鱼臂在伸展（reaching）运动中的曲率特征相似。与常规单腱连续体操作臂相比，该设计通过选择性曲率调节可独立塑形臂的不同区域，因而具有更大的工作空间和更高的灵巧性，同时避免了多段、多驱动器系统典型的机械复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过充气外壳在臂上任意位置形成可变的腱锚点，是一种以极低机械代价获取类章鱼弯曲点能力的有效途径；单腱即可实现分区曲率调控、钟形弯曲形态与扩展的工作空间，为高灵巧、低复杂度的软体操作臂设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Octopuses exhibit a powerful form of embodied intelligence from a highly adaptable soft body. One key mechanism is the ability to instantiate bending points at any location along the arm, which enables performant and dexterous behaviours. Taking inspiration from the octopus for its dexterity, this study focuses on changing the curvature of a tendon-actuated arm by adaptively changing the anchoring end-point along the arm. An array of inflatable shells is integrated into the arm, where inflating a shell causes an increase in friction at the location, jamming the tendon and creating a temporary new anchoring point. The novel soft actuator does not break the continuity of the arm between the actuated segment and the loose segment. Experiments show that the actuated segment has a non-constant bell-shaped curvature which is comparable to that of a real octopus arm during a reaching motion. Compared to conventional single-tendon continuum manipulators, the proposed design offers an expanded workspace and enhanced dexterity through the ability to independently shape different regions of the arm through selective curvature tuning. At the same time, it avoids the mechanical complexity typically associated with multi-segment, multi-actuator systems.

</details>


### [79] [Vector-Quantized World Model: Latent Feedback Motion Planning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25600)
*Xuezhou Xu,Chung-Pang Wang,Zhenzhen Li,Benjamin Riviere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VQ-WM，将离散潜空间长程搜索与连续视觉反馈控制结合，并用首达时间作为准度量替代欧氏距离，实现长程机器人操作规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型在机器人规划上有潜力，但长程操作面临三类问题：预测误差累积、搜索空间复杂度大、以及低层控制器难以可靠到达潜空间规划出的目标。

Method: 框架分为两部分：离散潜预测器建模向量量化潜状态间的转移，使规划器可通过离散搜索远距离前瞻并找到通往目标的最短路径；连续学习控制器基于视觉反馈依次到达规划状态并纠正偏差。此外，用首达时间估计作为准度量（而非常用欧氏距离）来衡量潜空间距离，使规划抽象与控制器实际能力对齐。

Result: 在物理机器人汉诺塔任务上，现有VLA、WAM等基础模型仅在训练数据包含解法轨迹时成功，而本方法仅用短程演示数据即达到100%成功率；在涵盖操作与移动的仿真基准上，比最强世界模型高出13.3–85.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 离散长程规划与连续反馈控制的解耦结合，配合面向控制器能力的准度量，可有效缓解误差累积并组合出超越演示轨迹的长程解，实现可靠执行。

Abstract: Visual world models have shown strong potential for robot planning, yet long-horizon manipulation remains challenging due to compounding prediction errors, large search complexity, and latent plans that the low-level controller cannot reliably reach. In this work, we introduce Vector-Quantized World Model (VQ-WM), a latent feedback motion planning framework that integrates discrete long-horizon search with continuous visual feedback control: a discrete latent predictor models transitions between vector-quantized latent states, allowing the planner to look far ahead and find a shortest path to the goal through discrete search and a continuous learned controller sequentially reaches the planned states through visual feedback, correcting deviations to mitigate compounding errors. Whereas most vector quantization uses Euclidean distance, we use a novel estimator of the first-passage-time as a quasi-metric, which aligns the planning abstraction with the controller's capabilities. We evaluate on the physical robot manipulation of the Tower of Hanoi puzzle: whereas existing state-of-the-art foundation models (VLAs, WAMs) only succeed when the solution trace in their training dataset, our method achieves 100\% success rate with only short-play data, demonstrate its ability to compose long-horizon solutions beyond the short demonstrated trajectories and execute them reliably. Our solution also outperforms strongest world-models by 13.3--85.3 percentage points on simulation benchmarks spanning manipulation and locomotion, demonstrating consistent progress and new capabilities.

</details>


### [80] [Humanoid SRL: Augmenting Humanoids with Supernumerary Limbs for Coordinated Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25628)
*Fukang Liu,Feiyang Wu,Paul Barsa,Jaehwi Jang,Akshat Jain,Zhigen Zhao,Yipu Chen,Zhaoyuan Gu,Nikhil Vemuri,Anshul Chandaliya,Kaijie Zhu,Sean Lublinsky,Matthew Zhu,Tony G. Chen,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将一条超numerary机械臂集成到人形平台上，探讨形态增广如何重塑其可行动作空间，并用轨迹优化+强化学习实现仿真到硬件的零样本迁移的三臂全身移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的双臂构型虽通用，但当双臂被占用或需要超出可达范围的额外接触点时，其移动操作能力会受到形态约束；超numerary机械臂（SRL）有望突破这一限制，但需与全身运动与平衡协同，其集成方法尚待研究。

Method: 在两种构型下进行定量分析与硬件实验；将超numerary臂集成到人形平台；采用基于模型的轨迹优化（TO）为目标条件强化学习（RL）控制器生成动力学可行运动；控制器在仿真中训练并零样本迁移到硬件；并用由硬件示教数据训练的扩散策略实现部分任务的视觉引导自主执行。

Result: 两种构型的定量分析与硬件实验表明，附加肢体通过协调操作与支撑，将全身交互能力拓展到原生双臂构型约束之外；所提TO+RL流程实现了接触丰富三臂移动操作的协调全身技能，并零样本迁移至硬件；基于扩散策略的视觉引导自主执行在选定任务上得到验证。

Conclusion: 人形形态不必局限于类人形态，而可被重新构想为可扩展的机器人增广设计空间。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are envisioned to perform diverse human-level tasks through their anthropomorphic morphology. Their dual-arm configuration enables a broad range of everyday actions. Yet, as in humans, this morphology can constrain loco-manipulation when both arms are occupied or additional contacts are required beyond their reach. Supernumerary robotic limbs (SRLs) offer a way to extend these capabilities, but their integration requires the added limb to be coordinated with whole-body motion and balance. In this study, we develop and integrate a supernumerary arm into a humanoid platform to investigate how morphological augmentation reshapes its feasible action space. Through quantitative analysis and hardware experiments across two configurations, we show how the additional limb expands whole-body interaction capabilities by enabling manipulation and support to be coordinated beyond the constraints of the native dual-arm configuration. To control this augmented morphology, we leverage model-based trajectory optimization (TO) to generate dynamically feasible motions for goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) controllers. The controllers are trained in simulation and transferred to hardware in a zero-shot manner, enabling coordinated whole-body skills for contact-rich tri-arm loco-manipulation. We further show vision-guided autonomous execution on selected tasks using diffusion policies trained from hardware demonstrations. Our findings suggest that humanoid morphology need not be restricted to human-like forms, but can be reimagined as an extensible design space for robotic augmentation.

</details>


### [81] [SteerAct: Unlocking Action Models through Few-Shot Temporal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25632)
*Kaichen Zhou,Zhiyan Li,Chongkai Gao,Yuhan Wang,Wenlong Huang,Maggie Wang,Fangneng Zhan,Manling Li,Qianqian Wang,Mac Schwager,Zhuang Liu,Li Fei-Fei,Mengyu Wang,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SteerAct，一种基于稀疏自编码器特征的少样本“时间引导”框架，用统一的时间描述子在离线阶段选出单个任务相关特征，并在在线执行中决定干预时机与强度，仅用 24 条带结果标签的 rollout 即可在不更新权重的情况下提升预训练机器人动作模型的成功率约 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人动作模型仅凭少量 rollout 在测试时难以改进；激活引导虽轻量（不改权重），但需解决三个难题：改哪些特征、何时改、改多强。现有方法缺乏跨 rollout 对齐的、可比较的特征动态描述手段。

Method: 1) 用预训练稀疏自编码器提供候选特征；2) 设计时间描述子，汇总每个特征局部“激活顺序模式”的分布，实现无需时间步对齐的跨 rollout 比较；3) 离线阶段：用成功/失败参考 rollout，选出时间描述子最能区分两种结果的任务专属特征并构建参考库；4) 在线阶段：将当前执行中该特征的演化描述子与参考库比对，决定干预时机与通过引导系数放大/抑制激活的强度。

Result: 在 π_{0.5}、Fast-WAM、RoboTwin 2.0、DexJoCo 及真实世界操作任务上评估，每任务仅用 24 条带标签 rollout，相比未引导模型任务成功率提升约 10%，且不更新动作模型权重。

Conclusion: 时间结构化的单特征激活引导是预训练机器人动作模型测试时自适应的一种高效、轻量方案；共享时间描述子同时服务于离线特征选择与在线控制，代码与演示已公开。

Abstract: Improving pretrained robot action models at test time from only a few rollouts remains challenging. Activation steering offers a lightweight approach by modifying feature activations while keeping model weights fixed. However, effective steering requires identifying which features to modify and determining the timing and strength of modification. We introduce SteerAct, a few-shot temporal steering framework that uses a shared temporal descriptor of sparse-feature dynamics for offline feature selection and online control. A pretrained sparse autoencoder provides candidate features, and our temporal descriptor summarizes the distribution of local activation-order patterns for each feature, enabling comparisons across rollouts without requiring timestep alignment. Before steering begins (offline), we use successful and failed reference rollouts to select a single task-specific feature whose temporal descriptors best distinguish the two outcomes and construct its reference banks. During each new execution (online), we compare the feature's evolving temporal descriptor with these references to determine when to intervene and how strongly to increase or decrease its activation via a steering coefficient. Using only 24 outcome-labeled rollouts per task, SteerAct improves task success by approximately 10% relative to unsteered models in evaluations spanning $π_{0.5}$, Fast-WAM, RoboTwin 2.0, DexJoCo, and real-world manipulation, without updating action-model weights. Code and demos are available at: https://steeract.github.io/.

</details>


### [82] [InteractAgent: Agentic Human Motion Interaction with Memory-Augmented Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25649)
*Tao Zhang,Senhe Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Yujia Zhang,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 InteractAgent：以单个多模态大模型（MLLM）同时充当规划器与评估器、以冻结的 MotionLLM 作为执行器，通过"规划—执行—观察—反思"闭环实现长时序人—场景交互，并具备在线观察的逐步恢复决策与记忆增强的规划反思，在 UniHSI 上提升任务成功率并降低穿透。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序人—场景交互需要协调的空间推理、物理可行性以及对累积执行误差的恢复能力。现有文本驱动动作模型基本与场景无关且缺少闭环纠错；几何条件方法依赖成对的场景—动作训练数据或显式的三维预处理，因而难以泛化到新布局与开放词汇目标。

Method: 构建代理式多模态 LLM 系统：单一 MLLM 兼作规划器与评估器，冻结的 MotionLLM 作为执行器，形成闭环。包含两个关键模块：（1）在线观察（Online Observation），在每个执行步后给出继续/重做/重规划的恢复决策；（2）记忆增强反思（Memory-Augmented Reflection），进行多轮规划精炼并采用自适应停止策略。

Result: 在 UniHSI 基准上，与免训练基线相比，InteractAgent 提升了任务成功率并减少了与场景的穿透（penetration）。

Conclusion: 将规划/评估与执行解耦、并用闭环观察与记忆反思替代对成对场景—动作训练数据和显式三维预处理的依赖，是一种有效的免训练方案，可提升长时序人—场景交互的鲁棒性与物理合理性。摘要未给出具体量化指标，后续可关注其计算开销与误差累积的定量分析。

Abstract: Long-horizon human-scene interaction requires coordinated spatial reasoning, physical feasibility, and the ability to recover from compounding execution errors. Text-driven motion models are largely scene-agnostic and lack closed-loop correction, while geometry-conditioned approaches depend on paired scene-motion training or explicit 3D preprocessing that limits generalization to novel layouts and open-vocabulary goals. We present InteractAgent, an agentic multimodal LLM system for long-horizon human-scene interaction that plans, executes, observes, and reflects in a closed loop, using a single MLLM as both planner and evaluator and a frozen MotionLLM as the executor. InteractAgent integrates Online Observation for step-wise recovery decisions (continue/redo/replan) and Memory-Augmented Reflection for multi-round plan refinement with adaptive stopping. On UniHSI, InteractAgent improves task success and reduces penetration compared with training-free baselines. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/InteractAgent.

</details>


### [83] [SpaRV: Sparse Radiance Voxels for Scalable Geometrically Consistent LiDAR-Visual Mapping and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25665)
*Jianheng Liu,Yunfei Wan,Fangming Cheng,Xiangyu Hong,Zuhao Zou,Haotian Li,Longji Yin,Chunran Zheng,Jiarong Lin,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaRV——一种稀疏辐射体素地图，通过可微光线步进渲染，并用LiDAR扫描与图像联合监督，解决机器人数据构建辐射场时的几何约束不足、相机位姿/内参误差模糊地图以及GPU显存随场景规模增长三大难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真、导航与巡检需要兼具照片级真实感与几何精度的地图。辐射场能实现照片级真实感，但从机器人数据构建时面临三方面挑战：(1) 机器人轨迹服务于任务而非重建，视角稀少导致几何欠约束；(2) 相机位姿与内参的残余误差会使地图模糊；(3) 地图的GPU显存随场景规模增长，限制了大场景中的细节层次。

Method: 提出SpaRV稀疏辐射体素地图，采用可微光线步进渲染，使不规则LiDAR扫描能与图像共同监督。用LiDAR深度与占据信息锚定欠约束几何，并用法向与平滑正则项保证LiDAR点间的平滑性；为校正相机误差，交替进行地图训练与相机位姿、焦距的光度优化；为限制训练与渲染的GPU显存，每次仅以全分辨率持有一个子地图，其余保持在较粗的八叉树层级。

Result: 实验表明SpaRV在远离采集轨迹处仍保持几何一致，并通过优化相机参数使纹理更锐利。在单张24 GB GPU上，SpaRV重建了由63,582张图像捕获的大学校园场景，并仿真其他相机与LiDAR。

Conclusion: SpaRV通过LiDAR与图像的联合监督、相机参数的光度优化以及八叉树分层显存管理，实现了大尺度场景下的几何一致、纹理清晰且显存可控的辐射场重建与传感器仿真。

Abstract: Robotic simulation, navigation, and inspection need photorealistic and geometrically accurate maps. Radiance fields achieve photorealism, but building them from robot data raises three challenges. A robot's trajectory serves its task, not reconstruction, so images from few viewpoints leave the geometry under-constrained. Residual errors in camera poses and intrinsics blur the map. The map's GPU memory grows with scene size, which limits its level of detail in large scenes. We present \textbf{SpaRV}, a \textbf{Spa}rse \textbf{R}adiance \textbf{V}oxel map rendered by differentiable ray marching, which lets irregular LiDAR scans supervise it alongside images. LiDAR depth and occupancy anchor the under-constrained geometry, and normal and smoothness regularizers keep it smooth between LiDAR points. To correct camera errors, SpaRV alternates map training with photometric optimization of the camera poses and focal lengths. To bound GPU memory in training and rendering, SpaRV holds one submap at a time at full resolution and the rest at coarser octree levels. Experiments show that SpaRV keeps the geometry consistent away from the captured trajectory and sharpens textures by optimizing the camera parameters. On one 24\,GB GPU, SpaRV reconstructs a university campus captured in 63{,}582 images and simulates other cameras and LiDARs. Our code will be released at \url{https://github.com/hku-mars/SpaRV}.

</details>


### [84] [Revealing and Bridging the Physical Executability Gap in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25687)
*Gang He,Zhile Cheng,Kepeng Xu,Zhenyang Liu,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 现有连续VLN评测以“到达目标”为成功标准，但宽松碰撞模型会掩盖物理不可行动作，造成“物理可执行性差距”。作者提出VeSTA框架，将策略与物理可执行的未来轨迹对齐，在严格碰撞评测下显著减少碰撞失败并保持或提升到达成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续视觉语言导航（VLN）通常以目标到达成功率作为评价指标，并假设它反映了智能体执行语言落地行为的能力。然而当前仿真器的接触/碰撞模型过于宽松，使得物理上不可行的动作被"掩盖"，从而在语义能力与具身实现之间产生物理可执行性差距（physical executability gap）。也就是说，高成功率未必意味着策略真正生成可执行轨迹。

Method: 提出Visual-Semantic Traversability Alignment（VeSTA）框架，将VLN策略与物理可执行的未来对齐。将"视觉语义可通行性"定义为：在指令条件下预测语义有效、几何可行且物理鲁棒的轨迹的能力。包含两个互补机制：(1) Trajectory Relative Group Policy Optimization：利用严格接触反馈重塑候选轨迹分布，使其向可执行区域集中，提升可执行支撑度；(2) Traversability Risk Critic：为候选未来学习风险调整后的标量效用，用于多模态未来之间的风险感知选择。

Result: 在R2R与RxR数据集上的实验表明，现有VLN智能体对仿真器接触语义存在显著依赖。在严格接触评测下，VeSTA大幅降低碰撞导致的失败，同时保持或提升目标到达性能；受控的真实机器人实验也表明学到的偏好可迁移到仿真之外。

Conclusion: 仅以到达成功率评价连续VLN存在隐患，宽松接触模型会高估策略的具身执行能力。显式对齐视觉语义可通行性、结合严格接触反馈的分布重塑与风险感知的未来选择，能有效弥合语义能力与物理实现之间的差距，且该能力可迁移至真实机器人。

Abstract: Continuous vision-and-language navigation (VLN) is typically evaluated by goal-reaching success, assuming it reflects an agent's ability to execute language-grounded behavior. We show that this assumption can fail: permissive contact models in current simulators may mask physically infeasible actions, creating a physical executability gap between semantic competence and embodied realization. To bridge this gap, we introduce Visual-Semantic Traversability Alignment (VeSTA), a framework that aligns VLN policies with physically executable futures. We define visual-semantic traversability as the ability to predict instruction-conditioned trajectories that are semantically valid, geometrically feasible, and physically robust. VeSTA addresses this misalignment through two complementary mechanisms: improving executable support in trajectory generation and risk-aware selection among multimodal futures. Specifically, Trajectory Relative Group Policy Optimization uses strict-contact feedback to reshape the candidate distribution toward executable regions, while the Traversability Risk Critic learns a risk-adjusted scalar utility for candidate-wise future selection. Experiments on R2R and RxR reveal substantial dependence of existing VLN agents on simulator contact semantics. Under strict-contact evaluation, VeSTA substantially reduces collision-induced failures while preserving or improving goal-reaching performance, and controlled real-world robot experiments provide evidence that the learned preference transfers beyond simulation.

</details>


### [85] [Vehicle-State-Aware Confidence Estimation for Direct Wind Sensing on a Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25688)
*Sri Arjun Senthil Kumar,April Zi Qian Chan,Chen-Hua Yeow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于飞行器水平/垂直速度的置信度方法，用于评估多旋翼无人机机载风速观测的可靠性，并据此自适应调节二维矢量估计器中的测量权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机机载直接测风可实现局地大气测量，但风速计测量可靠性高度依赖飞行器运动状态，平移等剧烈运动会使观测不可靠，需要有机制量化并抑制这些不可靠观测的影响。

Method: 以水平速度和垂直速度两个特征构建车速状态感知的置信度评分，采用ROC-AUC评估该评分对不可靠观测的判别能力；再将置信度分数用于调节二维风矢量估计器中各观测的影响权重。实验涵盖水平平移、垂直运动以及留出的自主航点飞行场景。

Result: 双特征置信度公式的ROC-AUC达0.995，平移观测中98.84%被识别为不可靠；置信度自适应使平移期间估计器中位增益由0.207降至0.064，同时悬停时中位增益保持在0.151。

Conclusion: 飞行器运动状态可提供有效的本体感知上下文，用于调节机载直接测风观测的影响；置信度感知的自适应策略能在抑制运动扰动的同时保留悬停期的有效观测。

Abstract: Direct wind sensing from a multi-rotor UAV enables localized atmospheric measurements, but the reliability of the anemometer depends on vehicle motion. This work presents a vehicle-state-aware confidence method that uses horizontal and vertical vehicle speed to quantify the acquisition of each wind observation. The resulting confidence score is used to adapt measurement influence to a two-dimensional vector estimator. Experiments were conducted during horizontal translation, vertical motion, and held-out autonomous waypoint flight. The proposed two-feature formulation achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.995, with 98.84% of translation observations identified as unreliable. The confidence-aware adaptation reduced median estimator gain during translation from 0.207 to 0.064 while retaining median gain of 0.151 during hover. The results show that vehicle state provides useful proprioceptive context for regulating the influence of direct airborne wind observations.

</details>


### [86] [ScaleNav: Scalable Internet Video Pretraining for Language-Conditioned Local Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25690)
*Yihui Yang,Yuanhong Yu,Jiawei Wang,Zhongrui Yu,Jia Shi,Kun Zhan,Ruizhen Hu,Xiaowei Zhou,Yu Liu,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ScaleNav 提出统一的世界模型-动作框架，利用互联网第一视角视频学习目标导向的视觉动态，经三阶段训练后用于语言条件下的局部导航，在两个基准和真实四足机器人上均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端导航策略需要多样训练数据以跨场景泛化，但真实机器人数据采集成本高，仿真数据存在 sim-to-real gap；互联网第一视角视频是可扩展的真实世界导航经验来源。

Method: 提出 ScaleNav 统一世界-动作框架，采用三阶段训练：(1) 在互联网第一视角视频上进行指令条件下的视频预训练，学习相机接近指定目标时观测的演化规律；(2) 动作对齐，利用带动作标签的仿真数据训练模型预测可执行的动作块；(3) 联合微调，共同优化所有可训练组件以完成导航任务。

Result: 在 Single-view 适配版 Short-Horizon OVON 上成功率达 86.5%，在自建 HabitatGS-LN 基准上达 94.9%，均优于所评估的基线；并在真实四足机器人上完成部署评估，结果支持其泛化到真实世界导航的能力。

Conclusion: 从互联网第一视角视频中学习目标导向视觉动态，可有效支撑机器人动作生成与语言条件导航，兼具可扩展性与真实世界泛化能力。

Abstract: We study language-conditioned local navigation, where a robot must navigate to a text-specified target visible in its initial observation and stop at the described location. End-to-end navigation policies require diverse training data to generalize across scenes, yet real-robot data is costly to collect and simulated data leaves a sim-to-real gap. Internet egocentric videos offer a scalable source of real-world navigation experience. Our key insight is to learn goal-directed visual dynamics from Internet egocentric videos, and use these dynamics to support robot action generation. To this end, we propose ScaleNav, a unified world-action framework with three-stage training. We first perform instruction-conditioned video pretraining on these videos to learn how observations evolve as the camera approaches instructed targets across diverse real-world scenes. Action alignment then uses action-labeled simulated data to train the model to predict executable action chunks. Joint fine-tuning subsequently optimizes all trainable components together for navigation. ScaleNav achieves success rates of 86.5% on a single-view adaptation of Short-Horizon OVON and 94.9% on our HabitatGS-LN benchmark, outperforming the evaluated baselines on both benchmarks. We further deploy and evaluate ScaleNav on a real quadruped robot, with results supporting the model's ability to generalize to real-world navigation. Code and videos are available at https://zju3dv.github.io/ScaleNav/

</details>


### [87] [ReDOA: Real-World Distillation via Observation Alignment for Image-Based Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25691)
*Kotaro Kajitsuka,Masaki Murooka,Fumio Kanehiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReDOA框架，将控制学习与感知学习解耦，把仿真中训练的状态教师策略部署到真机采集轨迹，再通过"观测对齐"蒸馏成基于图像的策略，无需人类示教即可在真机上学习图像策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作的数据驱动学习面临数据采集成本与真实环境适应性之间的根本权衡：模仿学习依赖昂贵的人类示教，而仿真学习存在动力学与传感器观测的sim-to-real差距。

Method: 解耦控制学习与感知学习：先在仿真中训练基于状态的教师策略，在真实机器人上执行以获得成功且高效的轨迹及真实视觉观测；再用"观测对齐"（Observation Alignment）解决数据采集与部署之间的观测不匹配，将控制知识蒸馏到基于图像的学生策略中，全程不需人类专家示教。

Result: 在三个非抓握式操作任务（含双臂操作）的真机实验中，同时考虑任务成功率与执行效率时，整体性能优于基于人类示教的模仿学习和仿真中的策略蒸馏。

Conclusion: 通过解耦控制与感知并以观测对齐弥合sim-to-real观测差距，可在无人类示教条件下于真实世界获得高效的图像策略，兼顾成功率与执行效率。

Abstract: Data-driven learning for general-purpose robotic manipulation presents a fundamental trade-off between data-collection cost and adaptability to real-world environments. Imitation learning (IL) requires costly human demonstrations, whereas simulation-based learning is vulnerable to sim-to-real gaps in dynamics and sensor observations. We propose ReDOA (Real-World Distillation via Observation Alignment), a framework that decouples control learning from perception learning to learn image-based policies in the real world. A state-based teacher policy trained in simulation is executed on a real robot, and its control knowledge is distilled into an image-based student policy using the collected trajectories. Observation Alignment resolves observation mismatches between data collection and deployment. This framework collects successful, efficiently executed trajectories paired with real-world visual observations, without requiring demonstrations from human experts. In real-robot experiments on three non-prehensile manipulation tasks, including bimanual manipulation, our method achieves better overall performance when jointly considering task success and execution efficiency than both IL based on human demonstrations and policy distillation in simulation. https://isri-aist.github.io/redoa-project-page/

</details>


### [88] [Validation of Human Motor Development Scales with Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25701)
*Fanxing Meng,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何用人类婴儿运动发展量表指导人形机器人学习一系列运动技能，并评估哪些量表足以作为自主的粗大运动技能（至站立和行走）开发指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望为人形机器人找到一个系统、可自包含的运动技能课程来源。人类婴儿运动发展量表经过长期临床验证、阶段划分自然，可能直接作为机器人技能学习的课程蓝图，从而避免手工设计训练顺序。

Method: 将每个运动发展量表表示成一张"发展图"（developmental graph），用以指导人形机器人训练；再把该图嵌入强化学习框架，使机器人按量表描述的婴儿运动发展自然阶段依次学习技能。

Result: 基于四种主流儿科运动量表所推导的发展图中，有三种能成功引导人形机器人发展出目标运动技能；第四种因缺少若干中间里程碑而导致部分失败，在图中补上缺失的里程碑后，机器人也能成功习得这些技能。

Conclusion: 儿科运动发展量表可作为人形机器人粗大运动技能学习的有效课程指导，但量表的完整性（尤其是中间里程碑的覆盖程度）对训练成败至关重要；缺失里程碑的量表经补全后同样可行。

Abstract: We investigate how humanoid robots can learn a series of motor skills defined by human infant motor development scales, and we evaluate which scales are comprehensive enough to serve as self-contained guides for developing gross motor skills up to standing and walking. We represent each motor development scale as a developmental graph that guides the training of a humanoid robot. We then apply this graph within a reinforcement learning framework so that the robot learns skills following the natural phases of human motor development described by the scale. Our work demonstrates that graphs derived from three out of four popular pediatric motor scales can successfully guide humanoid robots to develop these skills. The fourth scale misses some intermediate milestones that the other scales have, which causes some failures. After adding a missing milestone in its graph, it also enables the robot to develop those skills successfully.

</details>


### [89] [SWAP: Test-Time Steerable Humanoid Whole-Body Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25711)
*Zeji Yi,Guanqi He,Xiaofeng Guo,Guannan Qu,Pieter Abbeel,Yan Duan,Karen Liu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架SWAP：高层感知条件扩散策略生成多模态无碰撞运动提案，低层全身RL控制器跟踪并维持动态平衡；用"事后标注"构造可扩展监督，部署时用蒙特卡洛采样与简单代价信号引导生成，在仿真与Unitree G1真机上实现静态/动态障碍避让，测试时可控定制使目标奖励平均提升34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在以人为中心的环境中需全身协同穿越狭窄杂乱空间并保持平衡。现有方法难以同时兼顾高维感知输入下的动态稳定性与碰撞避免；且人形机器人的安全行为本质上是多模态的（同一障碍存在多种可行穿越方式），单一确定性策略难以表达。

Method: 采用分层架构：高层为感知条件扩散策略，输出多模态、碰撞感知的全身运动提案；低层为全身强化学习控制器，跟踪提案并维持动态平衡。训练高层策略时使用事后标注（hindsight labeling）获取可扩展监督：在随机指令下rollout低层策略，将障碍仅放置在已执行运动未扫过的空间，使运动天然无碰撞，再重新渲染机器人本应看到的感知。部署阶段用蒙特卡洛采样配合简单代价信号，将扩散模型引导向安全且符合任务的行为，无需重度奖励调参。

Result: 在仿真和真实Unitree G1人形机器人上均实现可靠的静态与动态障碍避让。测试时引导支持行为定制，针对速度、朝向、姿态等目标奖励平均提升34%，同时保持较高的避障成功率。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有测试时可引导性的人形机器人全身避障提供了实用方案，说明将多模态生成式提案与低层稳定控制解耦、并以事后标注获取监督，是解决高维感知下全身避障与多模态安全行为的有效途径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in human-centric environments must coordinate the entire body to traverse tight, cluttered spaces while maintaining balance. Existing methods often struggle to jointly consider dynamic stability and collision avoidance from high-dimensional perceptual inputs. Moreover, for humanoids, safe behavior is inherently multimodal: the same obstacle may admit multiple valid traversals, which is difficult to represent with a single deterministic policy. To address these challenges, we propose SWAP (Steerable Whole-body Avoidance Policy), a hierarchical framework that separates collision-aware decision making from stabilization. A high-level, perception-conditioned diffusion policy generates multimodal, collision-aware motion proposals, while a low-level whole-body RL controller tracks these proposals and maintains dynamic balance. To efficiently train the high-level policy, we obtain scalable supervision via hindsight labeling: we roll out the low-level policy under randomized commands, place obstacles only in the space that each executed motion did not sweep, so the motion is collision-free by construction, and re-render the perception the robot would have seen. At deployment, Monte Carlo sampling with simple cost signals steers the diffusion model toward safe, task-aligned behaviors without heavy reward tuning.
  Experiments in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid show reliable avoidance with static and dynamic obstacles. Test-time steering further enables customization, improving targeted rewards (e.g., speed, orientation, or posture) by 34% on average while maintaining a high collision avoidance rate. Together, these results support a practical solution to humanoid whole-body collision avoidance with test-time steerability. Project website: https://iscoyizj.github.io/SWAP-steerable-humanoid-oa-website/

</details>


### [90] [Enhancing Lunar PNT Performance Through Surface Station Enabled Radiometric Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25720)
*Toshiki Tanaka,Satoshi Ikari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由月面站辅助的辐射测量导航架构，联合双差伪距/多普勒观测与星间/面间双单向测距（DOWR）及巡视器航位推算，可将月面巡视器99.7%垂直定位误差从29.9 m降至4.8 m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月面任务需要自主巡视器的可靠定位，而月面导航卫星系统（如早期部署的LCRNS）受卫星可见性与观测几何限制，垂直定位精度不足，尤其在南极等区域。

Method: 构建月面站（SS）与巡视器之间的相对定位架构：利用导航卫星伪距与多普勒进行双差处理，并引入双单向测距（DOWR）提供额外距离观测，再与巡视器航位推算信息融合；同时提出DOWR硬件偏差估计方法，并用蒙特卡洛仿真验证（5颗LCRNS代表卫星、南极Connecting Ridge附近的巡视器轨迹）。

Result: 月面站显著改善水平与垂直相对定位，DOWR进一步增益。平均99.7%垂直定位误差由无月面站的29.9 m降至双差伪距+多普勒的5.5 m，加入DOWR后为4.8 m；未校正的DOWR硬件偏差会严重恶化定位，而所提偏差估计方法最终误差为0.058±0.057 m；DOWR收益依赖观测几何。

Conclusion: 月面站可通过直接辐射测距提升垂直相对定位能力，作为其通信与导航支持角色的有益补充。

Abstract: Future lunar surface missions require reliable positioning for autonomous rover operations. Lunar navigation satellite systems may provide limited vertical positioning accuracy due to satellite availability and observation geometry, particularly during early deployment. This study investigates a radiometric navigation architecture in which a lunar surface station (SS) improves rover positioning through double-differenced satellite observations and additional surface-to-surface ranging. Pseudorange and Doppler measurements from lunar navigation satellites are used for relative positioning between the SS and rover, while dual one-way ranging (DOWR) provides an additional range observable. The radiometric solution is integrated with rover dead-reckoning information. Monte Carlo simulations were conducted using five satellites representative of NASA's Lunar Communications Relay and Navigation Systems (LCRNS) constellation and a rover trajectory near Connecting Ridge at the lunar south pole. The results show that the SS improves horizontal and vertical relative positioning, with further gains from DOWR. In the simulated scenario, the mean 99.7% vertical positioning error decreased from 29.9 m without the SS to 5.5 m with double-differenced pseudorange and Doppler processing, and to 4.8 m with DOWR. An uncorrected DOWR hardware bias significantly degraded positioning, whereas the proposed bias-estimation method achieved a final estimation error of 0.058 +/- 0.057 m. The benefit of DOWR also depended on observation geometry. These results demonstrate the potential of a lunar surface station to improve vertical relative positioning through direct radiometric ranging, complementing its communication and navigation support roles.

</details>


### [91] [Learning from Fleet Experience: A Collective Learning Paradigm for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25736)
*Chao Yu,Shu'ang Yu,Hao Lin,Hongzhi Zang,Yuxiang Yan,Chenxi Jiang,Zhen Guo,Chuankang Li,Yihao Chen,Xin Xu,Xu Fu,Jiakai Zhou,Liangzhi Shi,Hongyi Ren,Yiwei Liu,Jing Wu,Yuanqing Wang,Chen Wu,Bo Dai,Chunyu Yang,Hongliang Huang,Shuoyu Yue,Weiqi Yin,Yinuo Chen,Ruihua Chen,Chunyang Zhu,Han Xiao,Yanghao Li,Ruipu Wu,Zhikai Cai,Xiu Chen,Xu Tan,Yuchen Yan,Jialin Zhang,Wen Zeng,Yiming Zhou,Zixiao Huang,Guanghui Ren,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Maoqing Yao,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出集合经验学习（CEL）范式与FERT方法：机器人集群共享执行经验，仅用失败上下文从成功经验中检索相关轨迹片段作为监督信号，无需额外专家示教或纠正动作；配合容错异步训练系统FATS，在5个真实操作任务上达93.3–99.2%成功率，且集群越大效果越好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署经验可用于提升策略可靠性，但依赖人类纠正的学习需要操作员持续监控并在失败时介入，随着机器人集群规模扩大难以满足。

Method: 提出集体经验学习（CEL）范式：部署中的机器人共享执行经验以改进共同策略。具体实现为失败引导的经验检索微调（FERT），利用失败上下文从集群共享的成功经验中检索相关轨迹片段，以其动作为监督更新策略，无需额外专家示教或纠正动作；并设计容错异步训练系统（FATS）协调经验收集、策略优化与策略分发，以应对集群内部中断。

Result: 在5个真实操作任务上，策略成功率为93.3–99.2%，每任务120次试验，且测试机器人未参与训练数据收集。每台机器人采集时间固定时，更大集群提供更多经验，FERT在评估规模下总体成功率更高。在多阶段手机包装任务中，使用96台机器人学习将成功率从79.2%提升至98.3%。

Conclusion: 集体经验可作为可扩展机器人学习的实用基础，为部署期间在不收集额外专家纠正动作的情况下提升策略可靠性提供路径。

Abstract: Experience generated during deployment offers an opportunity for robots to improve policy reliability through learning from their own executions. However, learning through human corrections requires teleoperators to monitor execution and intervene when policies fail, a demand that is difficult to meet as robot fleets grow. We introduce collective experience learning (CEL), a learning paradigm in which deployed robots share their execution experience to improve a common policy. We realize CEL through failure-guided experience retrieval fine-tuning (FERT), which uses failure contexts from robot executions to retrieve relevant trajectory segments from the fleet's shared successful experience and uses their actions as supervision for policy updates, without collecting additional expert demonstrations or corrective actions. A fault-tolerant asynchronous training system (FATS) coordinates experience collection, policy optimization and policy distribution despite local interruptions within the robot fleet. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, the resulting policies achieve success rates of 93.3-99.2%, evaluated over 120 trials per task on robots excluded from training data collection. With collection time per robot held fixed, larger fleets provide more experience and FERT generally achieves higher success rates across the evaluated sizes. On multi-stage phone packaging, learning with 96 robots increases success from 79.2% to 98.3%. These results support collective experience as a practical basis for scalable robot learning and a path towards improving policy reliability during deployment without collecting additional expert corrective actions.

</details>


### [92] [TrajWAM: An Efficient Trajectory-Guided World Action Model for Dynamic Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25763)
*Haohan Geng,Sha Zhang,Weishuo Sun,Guanting Ye,Wenhao Yu,Yuang Xie,Qicheng Zeng,Peng Ye,Jiajun Deng,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrajWAM 是一种轨迹引导的世界动作模型：用目标物体历史掩码预测未来掩码轨迹，并以此显式引导视频生成与动作预测，配合解耦蒸馏实现一步高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体操作需要机器人在执行中预判目标运动并及时重规划。现有方法要么只用快速反应式控制而缺少显式未来目标状态，要么仅隐式编码预测运动；World Action Models 虽有前景，但场景级视频目标对操作关键的目标动力学表征不足，且迭代式视频-动作去噪延迟高。

Method: 核心思路是让未来视觉想象显式锚定在预测的目标物体运动上，并用该未来表征条件化动作生成，且在加速后保留该预测通路。具体地：1) 基于短历史物体掩码的历史条件运动预测器预测未来掩码，编码目标轨迹；2) 轻量 Trajectory ControlNet 引导预训练视频模型生成轨迹一致的未来；3) 未来表征条件化动作专家输出可执行动作块；4) 提出解耦蒸馏方案实现一步推理：对视频骨干做分布匹配、Trajectory ControlNet 重对齐、动作 rollout 蒸馏，从而在压缩未来表征下准确一步预测动作。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的综合评估中，TrajWAM 在动态物体操作任务上取得 state-of-the-art 性能，并支持高效的一步视频-动作推理。

Conclusion: 将未来视觉想象显式建立在预测的目标轨迹上，并通过解耦蒸馏保留该预测通路，可在显著降低时延的同时提升动态操作表现，表明轨迹级显式预测是连接世界模型与实时操作的有效接口。

Abstract: Dynamic object manipulation requires robots to anticipate target motion while replanning fast enough to avoid executing outdated actions. Existing approaches either rely on fast reactive control without an explicit future target state or encode anticipated motion only implicitly. World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising alternative by coupling future visual prediction with action generation, but scene-level video objectives may underrepresent target dynamics that are critical for manipulation, while iterative video-action denoising introduces substantial latency. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-guided world action model for efficient dynamic robotic manipulation. Our key insight is to explicitly ground future visual imagination in predicted target-object motion and use the resulting future representation to condition action generation, while preserving this anticipation pathway after acceleration. Given a short history of object masks, a history-conditioned motion predictor forecasts future masks encoding the target object's trajectory. A lightweight Trajectory ControlNet then steers a pretrained video model toward trajectory-consistent futures. The resulting future representation conditions an action expert to predict executable action chunks. To enable one-step inference without breaking this interface, we introduce a decoupled distillation scheme that combines distribution matching for the video backbone, Trajectory ControlNet realignment, and action rollout distillation to support accurate one-step action prediction conditioned on the compressed future representation. Comprehensive evaluations in simulation and the real world show that TrajWAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on dynamic object manipulation tasks while enabling efficient one-step video-action inference. Project page: https://kevingeng3.github.io/TrajWAM/.

</details>


### [93] [Long-Horizon Multi-Object Rearrangement via Distilled Tree Search over Continuous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25785)
*Kallol Saha,Yining Hong,Amber Li,Yejin Choi,Maxim Likhachev,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DISCO 通过将物理仿真中的渐进加宽 MCTS 长程规划周期性蒸馏进策略-价值模型，实现无需测试时搜索的连续动作空间长程操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程操作任务中，学习型策略易产生复合误差并需保持未来可行性；基于搜索的规划虽能显式推理未来后果，但在高维连续动作空间中代价高昂。核心问题是如何把搜索得到的长程推理能力迁移到连续动作空间的策略中。

Method: 提出 DISCO（Distilled Tree Search over Continuous Actions）：在物理仿真中使用渐进加宽（progressive-widening）MCTS 生成长程规划；搜索时由学习到的策略提出连续动作，学习到的价值函数按任务完成预期进度对动作打分；借助 GPU 并行仿真，周期性将 MCTS 生成的规划蒸馏进策略-价值模型，从而改进后续搜索所用的动作提议与价值估计；最终部署学习到的策略-价值模型，测试时无需仿真器搜索。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的重排（rearrangement）任务上，DISCO 优于行为克隆、强化学习以及基于搜索的基线方法。

Conclusion: 搜索的长程推理能力可以通过迭代蒸馏有效迁移到连续动作空间的策略中，使测试阶段仅靠策略-价值模型即可获得优于各类基线的长程操作性能。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in policy learning, long-horizon manipulation remains difficult because learned policies must avoid compounding errors while preserving future feasibility. While search-based planning can explicitly reason over such future consequences, it quickly becomes expensive in high-dimensional continuous action spaces. This raises a central question: how can long-horizon reasoning from search be transferred into policies in continuous action spaces? To this end, we propose DISCO: Distilled Tree Search over Continuous Actions. DISCO uses progressive-widening MCTS in physics simulation to generate long-horizon plans. During search, a learned policy proposes continuous actions, while a learned value function scores actions by their expected progress toward task completion. Using GPU-parallelized simulation, DISCO periodically distills MCTS-generated plans into the policy-value model, improving the action proposals and value estimates used in subsequent searches. Finally, the learned policy-value model can be directly deployed without the need for simulator-based search at test time. Across simulated and real-world rearrangement tasks, DISCO outperforms behavior cloning, reinforcement learning, and search-based baselines.

</details>


### [94] [OV-PEAR: Observation-Verified Pose-Scale Estimation and Reconstruction for Unknown Industrial Manipulation Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25840)
*Guoqin Tang,Qingxuan Jia,Yichen Tan,Zihang Lv,Ruiquan Wang,Zeyuan Huang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OV-PEAR 提出一种"观测验证"的三阶段框架，在无需 CAD 模型或对象特定训练数据的前提下，对未知工业物体完成 3D 重建与位姿-尺度估计，并在 BOP-Industrial 与真实机器人装配任务上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业精细操作需要 6D 位姿、尺度以及可直接用于操作的 3D 模型。现有工业位姿估计依赖 CAD 模型、对象专属训练数据或稳定的工作单元环境，难以处理未见物体；而零样本方法又存在单视角遮挡、生成模型偏差和尺度不确定等问题。

Method: 提出三阶段以物体为中心的流水线：(1) 分割 RGB-D 实例并生成物体级 3D 假设；(2) 通过外观感知视角检索、显式尺度搜索与可见性敏感配准，将假设锚定到机器人坐标系；(3) 把多视角 RGB-D 证据反投影到物体规范坐标系，进行体素占据验证，抑制幻觉并产出可复用的可信物体模型。

Result: 在 BOP-Industrial 上取得 0.657 的平均 AR；在真实场景中提升了位姿与体素级几何一致性；真实机器人连接器装配任务达到 99.67% 抓取成功率和 84.00% 装配成功率；借助模型库与位姿-尺度日志，可实现 0.02 ms/帧的短期物体级状态维护。

Conclusion: 多视角观测验证能够有效抑制零样本 3D 生成的偏差并解决尺度模糊，使未知工业物体的重建、位姿估计与操作形成闭环，具备实时性与工程可用性。

Abstract: Fine industrial robotic manipulation requires 6D pose, scale, and an operation-ready 3D model. Existing industrial pose estimation relies on CAD models, object-specific training data, or stable workcells, limiting use with unseen objects; zero-shot methods suffer from single-view occlusion, generative bias, and scale uncertainty. We present OV-PEAR, an observation-verified framework for 3D reconstruction and pose-scale estimation of unknown industrial objects. Its three-stage object-centric pipeline: (1) segments RGB-D instances and generates object-level 3D hypotheses; (2) anchors them to the robot frame via appearance-aware view retrieval, explicit scale search, and visibility-sensitive registration; (3) back-projects multi-view RGB-D evidence into the object-canonical frame to verify voxel occupancy, suppress hallucinations, and produce reusable trusted object models. On BOP-Industrial, OV-PEAR achieves 0.657 average AR and improves pose and voxel-level geometric consistency in real scenes; real-robot connector assembly reaches 99.67% grasp and 84.00% assembly success. The model library and pose-scale log enable short-term object-level state maintenance at 0.02 ms/frame.

</details>


### [95] [AeroIntent: A Synchronized Multimodal Benchmark for UAV Mission Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25869)
*Yutong Chen,Jingxin Mao,Zhiwei Wei,Bing Li,Jianzhong Ni,Rongqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的多模态无人机任务意图识别基准AeroIntent（990条序列、22个轨迹程序、5类意图），并给出结构化多模态意图推理器AeroMIR，在程序不相交协议下平均准确率71.56%、Macro-F1 69.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空空域监视需要对无人机任务级意图进行可靠识别，但现有多模态无人机数据集主要面向检测、跟踪与定位，难以支持在未见轨迹程序和不完整雷达历史条件下的意图识别评估。

Method: 构建仿真基准AeroIntent，提供雷达、RGB、红外、深度四种观测，并设计程序不相交、雷达缺失、观测前缀三类评估协议；在此基础上提出AeroMIR，融合雷达轨迹、衍生行为特征、筛选后的视觉证据与任务知识，进行候选式意图识别。

Result: 在严格的程序不相交协议下（三随机种子），AeroMIR平均准确率71.56%、平均Macro-F1 69.84%，较最强纯轨迹基线高7.79个Macro-F1点，较常规雷达-RGB晚期融合基线高19.32个点；在受控雷达缺失与早期意图识别场景下同样表现良好。

Conclusion: AeroIntent与AeroMIR为多模态无人机意图识别提供了可复现的基础，基准已匿名开源。

Abstract: Reliable recognition of mission-level UAV intent from multimodal observations is essential for low-altitude airspace surveillance and security. Existing multimodal UAV datasets primarily support detection, tracking, and localization, leaving limited support for evaluating mission-intent recognition across held-out trajectory programs and incomplete radar histories. We introduce AeroIntent, a simulation-based benchmark comprising 990 synchronized multimodal sequences generated by 22 trajectory programs across five mission intents. The benchmark provides radar, RGB, infrared, and depth observations, together with program-disjoint, radar-gap, and observation-prefix evaluation protocols. Building on this benchmark, we propose AeroMIR, a structured Multimodal Intent Reasoner that integrates radar trajectories, derived behavioral features, selected visual evidence, and task knowledge for candidate-based UAV intent recognition. Under a strict program-disjoint protocol evaluating unseen tactical realizations, AeroMIR achieves a mean accuracy of 71.56% and a mean Macro-F1 of 69.84% across three seeds, outperforming the strongest trajectory-only baseline by 7.79 Macro-F1 points and a conventional radar-RGB late-fusion baseline by 19.32 points. AeroMIR also demonstrates strong performance under controlled radar gaps and early intent recognition settings. AeroIntent and AeroMIR provide a reproducible foundation for multimodal UAV intent recognition. The benchmark is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AeroIntent

</details>


### [96] [Vision-Language-Delta-Action: Learning Compact Deltas as Actionable Transition Interfaces for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25874)
*Lingsen Zhang,Wei Li,Jie He,Henghua Zhang,Kai Mo,Shiyong Li,Yanpeng Wang,Rui Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLDA 范式，将完整未来预测重构为紧凑的"可操作状态转移"建模，用 Delta 变量作为视觉语言理解与动作生成之间的接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法直接学习"观测到动作"的映射，由于语义理解与动作执行之间存在鸿沟，学习负担沉重；而世界动作模型（WAM）虽通过未来预测改善控制，但对完整未来状态建模会把容量浪费在静态外观和与动作无关的变化上。

Method: 核心是捕捉潜在世界状态变化的紧凑 Delta 变量，通过统一目标学习：LT-Filter（局部转移滤波）捕捉任务相关的、以操作为中心的变化并抑制静态重建偏差；GT-Anchor（全局转移锚点）提供粗粒度未来级正则以保持状态变化的整体方向。再由 DDAR（Delta 驱动动作精化）将 Delta 锚定到动作流形上，作为结构化种子引导动作去噪。实现于开源训练框架 LoongForge，利用其模块化与分布式基础设施进行统一的两阶段优化。

Result: 实验表明 VLDA 提升了长时序一致性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: "可操作 Delta"可作为直接动作预测与完整未来状态建模的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is not merely observation-to-action generation, but the realization of task-specified state changes in the physical world. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) methods directly learn observation-to-action mappings, imposing a substantial learning burden due to the gap between semantic understanding and action execution. Although world-action models (WAMs) improve control through future prediction, modeling complete future states wastes capacity on static appearance and action-irrelevant changes. We propose Vision-Language-Delta-Action (VLDA), a paradigm that reformulates full future prediction as compact actionable transition modeling. Its core is a compact Delta variable that captures latent world-state changes, serving as an interface between vision-language understanding and action generation. VLDA learns Delta through a unified objective composed of the Local Transition Filter (LT-Filter) and Global Transition Anchor (GT-Anchor). LT-Filter captures task-relevant manipulation-centric changes while suppressing static reconstruction biases. GT-Anchor provides coarse future-level regularization to preserve the overall direction of state changes. Delta-Driven Action Refinement (DDAR) further grounds Delta in the action manifold and injects it as a structured seed to guide action denoising. We implement VLDA within LoongForge, an open-source training framework for large-scale foundation and embodied models, using its modular and distributed infrastructure for unified two-stage optimization. Experiments show that VLDA improves long-horizon consistency, robustness, and generalization, validating actionable Delta as an alternative to direct action prediction and full future-state modeling.

</details>


### [97] [Policy Search in Code Space for Multi-Agent Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25946)
*Matthias Eder,Pantelis Emmanouil,Vivek Farias,Fabian Fischer,Adam Jozefiak,Tianyi Peng,Thomas Ulz,Raphael Watschinger,Fangyu Wu,Andrew Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"代码空间中的策略搜索"作为多智能体协调策略设计的第三条路径：由人类引导编码智能体迭代地提出、实现、评估、诊断并精化算法，结合仿真反馈与正确性验证。两个案例中该方法均优于人工方案：机器人赛地图上两天的搜索比复现的更强参赛方案提升35.0%，一周搜索在真实仓库仿真中发现全新类别的多智能体路径规划算法，吞吐量比校准的专有方案高58.9%，并证明其在给定假设下无碰撞且有限时间内完成每个被接受目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂多智能体系统的协调策略设计长期依赖两条路径：一是劳动密集的人工算法设计，二是黑箱的、任务专用的强化学习。前者成本高、难以规模化，后者缺乏可解释性与分析可证性。作者希望寻找一条兼顾自动化优化与显式可解释性的第三条路径，并借助近期自主编码智能体的进步使其成为可能。

Method: 作者研究一种"人类引导"的工作流：编码智能体在仿真反馈与显式正确性验证的引导下，迭代地提出、实现、评估、诊断并最终精化算法，即直接在代码空间中进行策略搜索。随后通过两个案例研究评估该工作流：(1) 2024 League of Robot Runners 竞赛中的一张挑战性地图，进行约两天的搜索，并与本地复现的更强竞赛方案对比，同时检验对未见地图实例的泛化；(2) 模拟真实仓库环境（不规则车道、连续机器人几何、异步运动），进行约一周的搜索，与校准过的专有方案比较吞吐量；并在所述假设下对新算法给出无碰撞与有限时间完成性的理论证明。

Result: 两个案例中，该工作流均稳健地优于任何人工工程化方案。在竞赛地图上，两天搜索得到的策略在最密集实例上比本地复现的更强竞赛方案高出35.0%，并可泛化到未见地图实例。在带不规则车道、连续几何与异步运动的模拟真实仓库中，一周搜索发现了一类全新的多智能体路径规划算法，吞吐量比校准的专有方案高出58.9%。此外，在给定假设下证明该新算法无碰撞，且能在有限时间内完成每一个未被撤回或替换的被接受目标。

Conclusion: 两项研究表明，由先进编码智能体驱动的代码空间策略搜索是一条极具竞争力的工程路径，能够构建既有效又具备分析可处理性（可解释、可证明）的真实工程解决方案，为多智能体协调策略设计提供了结合自动化与可解释性的第三条道路。

Abstract: Developing coordination policies for complex multi-agent systems relies on either labor-intensive algorithm design or opaque task-specific reinforcement learning. With recent advances in autonomous coding agents, we show that policy search directly in code space offers a compelling third path that combines automated optimization with explicit interpretability. We investigate a human-directed workflow in which coding agents iteratively propose, implement, evaluate, diagnose, and eventually refine algorithms guided by simulation feedback and explicit correctness verification. We conducted two case studies and found that this workflow robustly outperformed any manually engineered solutions. On a challenging map in the 2024 League of Robot Runners competition, a two-day search discovered a policy that exceeds the stronger locally reproduced competition entry by 35.0% on the densest instance and generalizes to unseen map instances. In simulated real-world warehouses with irregular lanes, continuous robot geometry, and asynchronous motion, a week-long search discovered an entirely new class of multi-agent pathfinding algorithm that outperforms a calibrated proprietary solution in throughput by 58.9%. Under stated assumptions, we further prove that the new algorithm is collision-free and completes, in finite time, every accepted goal that is not withdrawn or replaced. Together, the studies show that policy search in code space powered by state-of-the-art coding agents offers a highly competitive path to building real engineering solutions that are both effective and analytically tractable.

</details>


### [98] [PaceVLA: Adaptive Temporal Action Quantization in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25961)
*Hojin Jeon,Jimin Lee,Taekwan Kim,Nayoung Oh,Taeyoung Kim,Myungkyu Koo,Jinmo Koo,Joonwoo Ahn,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PaceVLA 提出一个面向 VLA 模型的自适应时序执行框架，通过多粒度动作解码器与可量化性引导的模式选择/路由机制，在推理时自适应选择执行粒度，从而兼顾加速与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽具备通用性，但执行时序效率低：策略从遥操作演示中学到细粒度时序模式，需要大量底层动作步。简单的时序动作量化（将连续动作聚合为粗粒度动作）虽能加速，却会带来速度—精度权衡——在精度敏感阶段丢弃关键运动细节。

Method: PaceVLA 包含两个耦合组件：(1) 基于共享动作专家的模式专属动作解码器，支持多种时间粒度与预测时域；(2) 量化性引导的模式选择方案，判断粗粒度执行是否合适，并配合上下文条件路由器在每次推理步选择执行模式。

Result: 在三个多任务仿真基准和六个真实世界任务上，PaceVLA 在多种具身形态（包括长时域与灵巧操作）下提升了执行效率，同时保持任务成功率。

Conclusion: 自适应时序执行是缓解 VLA 执行效率瓶颈的有效途径；通过统一的多粒度解码与量化性感知路由，可在不明显损失成功率的前提下显著加快执行。

Abstract: Fast and reliable manipulation is crucial for practical robot deployment. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown remarkable progress as generalist robot policies, their execution often remains temporally inefficient. A key source of this inefficiency is that policies learn fine-grained temporal patterns from teleoperated demonstrations, requiring many low-level action steps. Temporal action quantization offers a simple way to accelerate execution by aggregating consecutive low-level actions into temporally coarser actions. However, applying action quantization without considering when fine-grained control is required introduces a speed-accuracy trade-off: accelerated actions may efficiently reduce redundant steps in some phases, while discarding critical motion details in precision-sensitive interactions. To address this, we propose PaceVLA, a unified framework for adaptive temporal execution in VLAs that integrates two coupled components: (1) mode-specific action decoders built on a shared action expert, supporting multiple temporal granularities and prediction horizons, and (2) a quantizability-guided mode selection scheme which decides whether coarse execution is appropriate, and a context-conditioned router that selects an execution mode at each inference step. Experiments across three multi-task simulation benchmarks and six real-world tasks show that PaceVLA improves execution efficiency while maintaining task success rate across multiple embodiments, including long-horizon and dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [99] [SkateNav: Learning Energy-Efficient Navigation through Adaptive Skating on Passive-Wheel Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25962)
*Jeil Jeong,Taeyun Kim,Sangjin Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SkateNav——基于强化学习的被动轮四足机器人滑行导航统一框架，通过时间预算目标与自适应滑行周期联合学习转向、推进及时序分配，仿真中成功率更高、运输成本更低，并实现仿真到真机零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮四足机器人依赖"推进—滑行"间歇运动实现高效移动，但导航时需协调转向与间歇推进，并按任务需求调整推进与滑行时序。传统速度跟踪类目标会抑制滑行固有的速度波动，从而削弱其高效滑行优势。

Method: 构建统一的滑行导航强化学习框架：1）采用带时间预算的到达目标（time-budgeted goal-reaching），使策略联合学习转向、推进及二者的时间分配；2）引入自适应滑行周期，结构化组织滑行、推进与恢复三个阶段；3）在每个控制步进行频率调制，动态调整各相位时长以匹配导航需求。并通过消融实验验证各模块作用。

Result: 仿真实验中，平均成功率和运输成本均优于所对比的规划器—控制器基线；消融实验证实周期结构与频率自适应机制均有贡献。真机实验中，在杂乱室内空间、斜坡及铺装室外路面实现从仿真到被动轮Mini Cheetah的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: 将导航目标从速度跟踪转向时间预算下的到达任务，并显式建模滑行周期结构与频率自适应，可有效释放被动轮四足机器人的节能潜力，且策略具备良好的仿真—现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Passive-wheel quadrupedal robots offer energy-efficient locomotion through leg-driven propulsion and momentum-driven gliding. However, navigation requires coordinating steering with intermittent propulsion and adapting push-glide timing to task demands. Velocity-tracking objectives may suppress the speed variations inherent in skating, discouraging efficient gliding. To address these challenges, we propose SkateNav, a unified skating-navigation framework based on reinforcement learning. A time-budgeted goal-reaching objective allows the policy to jointly learn steering, propulsion, and their temporal allocation. An adaptive skating cycle structures gliding, pushing, and recovery, while frequency modulation at every control step adjusts phase durations to navigation demands. Simulation experiments demonstrate higher average success rates and lower cost of transport than the evaluated planner-controller baselines, with ablations confirming the contributions of cycle structure and frequency adaptation. Real-world experiments demonstrate zero-shot transfer from simulation to a passive-wheel Mini Cheetah across cluttered indoor spaces, slopes, and paved outdoor surfaces. Supplementary videos are available on the project page: https://ktw1404.github.io/SkateNav/

</details>


### [100] [Reconstruction and Discrete-Event Simulation Assessment of an Analytical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25967)
*Mohsen Reda Mahmoud Moursi,Ahmed Stohy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个针对仓储巷道内机器人拣选的“解析模型—离散事件仿真（DES）”基准对照，在相同货架几何、运动与拣选条件下复现Lerher等的解析公式；174个主场景中循环时间平均绝对相对偏差为0.8655%，中位数0.6758%，说明二者数值接近但非完全一致，也未构成统计等价或实际仓库验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析模型在实际仓储机器人拣选系统的性能评估中被广泛使用，但其结果缺乏与独立仿真在不同参数规模下的系统性对照。作者希望建立一个可重复的基准，检验公开解析模型在相同输入和相同循环时间定义下能否被仿真复现。

Method: 研究对象为巷道内机器人拣选循环，包含穿梭车水平行走、周转箱搬运与货位处的物品拣选。在相同货架几何、穿梭车运动、搬运与拣选条件及相同循环时间与吞吐量定义下，将DES结果与Lerher等的解析式对比；复现已发表解析表格需采用距离解释dES=1.5 m、dTB=0 m。基准共180个场景（3种货架长度×2种配对运动曲线×访问拣选点n=1~30），每场景10次仿真重复；n=1的6个无“点间行走”分量的场景单独报告。

Result: 在剩余174个场景中，循环时间平均绝对相对偏差0.8655%，中位数0.6758%，最大7.0370%；平均绝对误差2.411 s，RMSE 2.744 s。所有174个主场景的仿真循环时间均值均低于解析值。

Conclusion: 在受测条件下解析模型与DES数值吻合较好，但不存在完全一致，也不构成正式的统计等价性证明，更未针对实际运行仓库进行验证；结果应视为受控条件下的基准对照而非模型验证。

Abstract: This article presents an analytical-DES benchmark for intra-aisle robotic picking in automated warehousing. The investigated cycle combines horizontal shuttle travel, tote handling and item picking at storage locations. Simulation results are compared with the analytical formulation of Lerher et al. under the same rack geometry, shuttle-motion, handling and picking conditions, and the same definitions of cycle time and derived item throughput. Reproducing the published analytical tables requires the distance interpretation dES = 1.5 m and dTB = 0 m. The analytical-DES benchmark comprises 180 scenarios formed from three rack lengths, two paired motion profiles and n = 1 to 30 visited picking locations, with ten simulation replications per scenario. The six n = 1 cases are reported separately because no travel-between component is present. Across the remaining 174 scenarios, the mean absolute relative cycle-time discrepancy is 0.8655%, the median is 0.6758%, and the maximum is 7.0370%. The mean absolute error is 2.411 s and the RMSE is 2.744 s. The simulated scenario-mean cycle time is below the analytical value in all 174 principal scenarios. The results therefore show close numerical correspondence under the tested conditions, but not exact agreement, formal statistical equivalence, or validation against an operating warehouse.

</details>


### [101] [FR-VLA: Implicit Force Reasoning VLA for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25981)
*Jiaju He,Zhiyuan Yang,Ruyang Han,Jiahong Tang,Zhiping Lin,Haiyue Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出FR-VLA，一个视觉-语言-力推理模型：用可学习的力查询token从视觉-语言上下文推断"力相关性/力准入信号"，再用该信号调制外部的token化力通路来生成动作，实现"何时、以多大强度"让力反馈介入动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将力/力矩信息融入VLA模型的工作通常只把力信号当作额外模态、通过固定通路注入，缺乏显式判断当前任务/动作是否需要物理反馈的机制。作者认为关键在于决定物理交互应在何时、以何种强度影响动作生成，因此需要在VLA内部加入一个中间层的"力相关性"机制。

Method: FR-VLA在VLA内部引入力相关性推理：使用可学习的力查询token（force query tokens），基于视觉-语言上下文推导出力准入信号（force admission signal）；该信号被用于调制一条外部的、token化的力通路，最终共同参与动作生成。

Result: 在两个机器人平台、五类接触丰富任务的真实世界实验中，FR-VLA在六个评测阶段上取得最高的平均成功率；轨迹可视化显示其力准入程度随交互阶段而变化。

Conclusion: 力反馈不应被无条件、固定地注入VLA，而应由视觉-语言上下文先行判断其相关性并动态调制；这种"先推理力相关性、再准入力信息"的设计在真实接触型任务中有效。

Abstract: Integrating force awareness into Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models involves not merely encoding wrench measurements as an additional modality, but determining when and how strongly physical interaction should influence robot action generation. Existing VLAs that use force typically inject it through a fixed pathway, without explicitly determining when it is relevant to the current action. This calls for an intermediate force relevance mechanism inside the VLA, in which visual-language context estimates the relevance of physical feedback to the current task before force enters the action pathway. FR-VLA addresses this gap with a Vision-Language Force-Reasoning Model that uses learnable force query tokens to derive a force admission signal grounded in visual-language context, and uses this signal to modulate an external tokenized force pathway for action generation. In real-world experiments on five contact-rich tasks across two robotic platforms, FR-VLA achieves the highest mean success rate over six evaluated stages, and trajectory visualizations show that its force admission varies with the interaction phase.

</details>


### [102] [Self-Supervised Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25988)
*Yuheng Qiu,Yutian Chen,Carlton Cort,Weison Huang,Shibo Zhao,Pieter Abbeel,Rocky Duan,Koushil Sreenath,C. Karen Liu,Carmelo Sferrazza,Junyi Geng,Sebastian Scherer,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"积分自监督"方案：用仅对每个短窗口独立积分IMU的"近视教师"从零训练惯性里程计模型，学生模型反而学会直接预测速度，精度超过教师；再用位姿图蒸馏引入序列一致目标。新平台部署无需真值轨迹，仅需IMU标定与日常数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习惯性里程计通常依赖真值轨迹，而在新平台（行人、无人机、四足、人形等）上采集真值轨迹代价高、难以规模化，成为模型迁移的主要瓶颈。

Method: 1）积分自监督：对每个短窗口独立积分IMU得到短窗速度变化，作为"近视教师"的监督信号，学生模型从零训练；2）观察到学生虽仅被短窗速度变化监督，却自发学会直接预测速度且优于积分教师；3）位姿图蒸馏：在多个窗口上构建位姿图，生成序列一致的目标进一步精炼模型。

Result: 在涵盖行人、无人机、四足机器人和人形机器人的六个数据集上，同一套无速度/位置标签的训练流程所得模型，平均速度误差与同架构有监督基线相差仅0.07 m/s。

Conclusion: 惯性里程计可在无真值轨迹条件下自监督训练，新平台仅需IMU标定与常规运行数据采集即可部署，显著降低数据获取门槛，并具备跨平台通用性。

Abstract: Learning inertial odometry for a new platform usually requires ground-truth trajectories. We show that an inertial odometry model can instead be trained from scratch by a myopic teacher that integrates the IMU over each short window independently, a scheme we call integration self-supervision.Interestingly, although supervised only by short-window velocity changes, the student learns to directly predict velocity, and does so more accurately than its integration teacher. Pose graph distillation then refines the model with sequence-consistent targets from a pose graph over many windows. Training on a new platform therefore no longer requires collecting ground-truth trajectories, but only calibrating the IMU and recording data during normal operation. Across six datasets spanning pedestrians, drones, quadrupeds, and a humanoid, the same recipe with no velocity or position labels trains models that come within $0.07$~m/s average velocity error of a same-architecture supervised baseline.

</details>


### [103] [A Spiking Ring-Attractor Architecture for Representation and Control of Revolute Robot Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26027)
*Aung Htet,Alejandro Rodriguez Jimenez,Ruidong Ma,Alessandro Di Nuovo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全脉冲神经控制架构，用环形吸引子（ring attractor）编码旋转关节状态，使表征符合旋转系统的拓扑结构：稳态调节在分布式表征上计算目标失配，对立增益调制群体将其转换为活动凸包的受控相移，实现SO(2)上的设定点调节，并推导解析傅里叶相位解码器与推挽脉冲实现，推广到多关节SO(3)取向解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转关节机器人系统的状态空间本质是圆形的（SO(2)），但主流控制架构用欧氏变量表示这些状态，忽略了底层几何结构，可能带来表征不一致与调节误差；同时缺乏将脉冲神经表征、调节与动作统一在几何一致基底上的控制框架。

Method: 采用全脉冲神经网络架构：以环形吸引子编码关节状态并保持拓扑一致性；用稳态调节直接在分布式表征上计算目标失配；由对立（opponent）增益调制群体把失配转换为活动凸包的受控相移，从而把设定点调节实现为环上的运动；分析凸包动力学满足SO(2)几何、增益不对称产生带符号相速度；推导解析傅里叶相位解码器并用推挽脉冲群体实现其带符号分量；多关节时由局部环形傅里叶特征组合成乘积表征，通过正运动学线性解码SO(3)取向。

Result: 在NEST仿真中验证了完整的SO(2)调节机制以及针对lift、pitch、yaw的脉冲乘积特征解码器；在Tiago机械臂上的机器人实验表明，解码后的环形动力学可生成关节速度指令，产生有方向的运动，且精度依赖于具体关节。

Conclusion: 结果建立了一个神经形态控制框架，使表征、调节与动作统一于与旋转系统几何相契合的环形吸引子基底之中。

Abstract: Robotic systems with revolute joints evolve on circular state spaces, yet most control architectures represent these states using Euclidean variables that ignore this underlying geometry. We propose a fully spiking neural control architecture in which ring attractors encode joint states, enforcing representations consistent with the topology of revolute systems. Homeostatic regulation computes goal-state mismatch directly over these distributed representations, and opponent gain-modulated populations convert this mismatch into controlled phase shifts of the activity bump, implementing set-point regulation as motion on the ring. We show that the bump dynamics follow an SO(2) geometry, and that gain asymmetries induce a signed phase velocity on the ring. To interface distributed activity with scalar regulation signals, we derive an analytic Fourier phase decoder and implement its signed components using push-pull spiking populations. For multi-joint systems, we demonstrate that local ring-based Fourier features compose into product representations that linearly decode SO(3) orientations through forward kinematics. Experiments in NEST validate the full SO(2) regulation mechanism and the spiking product-feature decoder for lift, pitch, and yaw. Robot experiments on a Tiago arm show that decoded ring dynamics generate joint velocity commands, producing directed motion with joint-dependent accuracy. Together, these results establish a neuromorphic control framework in which representation, regulation, and action are unified within a ring-attractor substrate aligned with the geometry of revolute systems.

</details>


### [104] [OmniInteract: Towards Unified Whole-Body Interaction Control for Dual Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26043)
*Yinghao Li,Tianyu Zhang,Zhuohao Li,Yi-Lin Wei,Fa-Ting Hong,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniInteract通过表面接触感知重定向和可扩展的残差策略学习，实现多样化的双人形机器人全身物理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人协作交互需要协调全身运动与精确接触，但存在两大挑战：一是人机形态差异会破坏重定向过程中的接触界面与空间关系；二是联合学习运动技能与伙伴协调难以在交互多样性增长时保持控制保真度。

Method: 提出两项关键技术：(1) 表面接触感知重定向（Surface Contact-Aware Retargeting），利用稠密的人机表面对应关系联合重定向两个人形机器人，保留显式交互接触界面及其近场几何上下文；(2) 可扩展的流式交互策略（Scalable Flow-based Interaction Policy），将双人形控制分解为可复用的运动基础与交互特定适配——伙伴感知专家在冻结的全身运动先验上学习修正，并用条件流匹配在共享残差动作空间中统一它们。

Result: 大量实验表明，OmniInteract提升了重定向保真度和双人形协调能力，并在技能多样性增长时更好地保持交互性能；真实世界部署验证了成功建立接触、稳定的全身协调以及从外部扰动中恢复的能力。

Conclusion: 表面感知建模与可扩展残差策略学习能有效解决人机形态差异与技能多样性带来的难题，实现多样化、可扩展且可迁移到真实场景的双人形物理交互。

Abstract: Physical interaction is a key capability for humanoid collaboration, requiring coordinated whole-body motion and precise contact. However, learning diverse dual-humanoid interactions faces two major challenges: (1) human-robot morphological differences disrupt contact interfaces and spatial relationships during retargeting, and (2) jointly learning motor skills and partner coordination makes it difficult to retain control fidelity as interaction diversity grows. In this paper, we propose OmniInteract to address these challenges through surface-aware modeling and scalable residual policy learning. First, we introduce Surface Contact-Aware Retargeting, which leverages dense human-robot surface correspondences to jointly retarget both humanoids, preserving explicit interaction contact interfaces and their near-field geometric context. Second, we propose a Scalable Flow-based Interaction Policy for partner-conditioned residual generation.
