It factorizes dual-humanoid control into a reusable motor foundation and interaction-specific adaptations: partner-aware experts learn corrections over a frozen whole-body motion prior, while conditional flow matching unifies them in a shared residual action space. This enables scalable skill consolidation while retaining pretrained motor capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniInteract improves retargeting fidelity and dual-humanoid coordination while better retaining interaction performance as skill diversity grows. Real-world deployment further demonstrates successful contact establishment, stable whole-body coordination, and recovery from external perturbations.

</details>


### [105] [A GPU-Resident Pipeline for Multi-Robot 3D Inspection with Limited Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26106)
*Kryštof Pšenička,Stefan Edelkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个全GPU驻留的多机器人水下结构3D巡检流水线，将联合覆盖路径规划问题分解为五个阶段并全部GPU加速，包含多项算法贡献，并在14个网格上完成实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人携带短距、有限视场相机对水下结构三角网格进行3D巡检时，需要联合优化表面覆盖与makespan/总代价（线性插值目标）的6自由度轨迹，该联合问题NP难，且大规模网格下计算开销大，亟需高效完整流水线。

Method: 将问题分解为五阶段：候选视点生成、可见性计算（GPU批量适配Lien ε-可见性，四个自定义CUDA核）、集合覆盖、min-max车辆路径（MIP + 提升的MTZ子回路消除 + 基于插值目标的β感知单车巡游上界 + Desrochers-Laporte系数提升 + 禁止对割）、以及带连续时间机器人间冲突检测的优先级多智能体路径规划（Zhou与Zeng的GPU并行前沿A*的4D时空扩展）；前后各接网格加载/表面采样与轨迹平滑。全流程GPU端到端集成并公开核设计。

Result: 在14个网格（最大720万面）上实验，消融了路径割与并行前沿搜索，在轨迹平滑后审计无碰撞性与航点到达性，并测量端到端相对解析界与LP松弛下界的差距。

Conclusion: 所提GPU加速五阶段流水线具备端到端可行性与可复现性（代码与原始结果公开），各项算法组件经消融验证有效。

Abstract: We present a complete, GPU-resident pipeline for multi-robot 3D inspection of underwater structures. Given a triangle mesh and a fleet of $K$ robots, each carrying a short-range, limited field-of-view camera, the pipeline computes 6-DOF trajectories that jointly cover a target fraction of the surface while minimizing a linearly interpolated makespan/total-cost objective. The joint problem is NP-hard, so we decompose it into five core stages - candidate viewpoint generation, visibility computation, set cover, min-max vehicle routing solved as a mixed integer program (MIP) with lifted Miller-Tucker-Zemlin subtour elimination, and priority-based multi-agent path finding with continuous-time inter-robot conflict checking - flanked by mesh loading and surface sampling as preprocessing and by trajectory smoothing as postprocessing. Our contributions are: an end-to-end GPU-accelerated integration of the five stages, with its kernel design documented; a GPU batch adaptation of Lien's $\varepsilon$-visibility with four custom CUDA kernels; a $β$-aware per-vehicle tour upper bound derived from the interpolated objective, supplementing the Desrochers-Laporte coefficient lift with forbidden-pair cuts; a 4D space-time extension of Zhou and Zeng's GPU parallel-frontier A*; and an experimental evaluation on 14 meshes (up to $7.2$M faces) that ablates the routing cuts and the parallel-frontier search, audits collision freedom and waypoint attainment \emph{after} trajectory smoothing, and measures the end-to-end gap against analytical and LP-relaxation lower bounds. Code and raw results are publicly available.

</details>


### [106] [Compositional Humanoid Manipulation through Isomorphic Teleoperation and Independent Skill Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26169)
*Yiming Wang,Peixian Yang,Lifeng Zhuo,Lai Wei,Xinling Du,Kun Song,Weiming Wang,Daolin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 HS-MoDP（人形语义混合专家扩散策略），通过独立语义路由在共享专家池上组合分别示教的单臂技能，并配合语义调度器与现有运动控制器交替完成操作与移动；同时开发同构外骨骼 G1Exo 实现关节级直接遥操作。在 Unitree G1 上，技能组合优于基线，G1Exo 示教使柜体任务全流程成功率由 VR-IK 的 35% 提升至 65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人操作需要可复用的手臂技能，但逐一示教所有技能组合不现实；同时全身接触式操作的教学缺少可靠的遥操作方法，末端 VR-IK 在接触场景下示教质量不佳。

Method: 1) HS-MoDP：为每条手臂设置独立语义路由，在共享专家池上选择专家，从而保留单臂技能控制同时共享动作生成参数；2) 语义调度器：在操作阶段与重定位阶段之间交替，把学到的策略与已有运动控制器结合，扩展有效工作空间并支持跨位置技能复用；3) G1Exo：同构外骨骼，提供与机器人一致的关节级手臂构型直控，用于采集全身接触操作示教。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 上技能组合能力强于基线；G1Exo 的接触遥操作比末端 VR-IK 更可靠，其示教在四种策略架构上均提升接触技能学习；对 HS-MoDP，改用 G1Exo 示教使柜体全流程成功率从 35% 提升至 65%。

Conclusion: 语义路由的混合专家扩散策略结合示教采集硬件，可实现可复用、可组合的人形手臂技能与全身接触操作；硬件设计与源码将在评审后开源。

Abstract: Humanoid manipulation requires reusable arm skills, yet demonstrating every possible skill combination is impractical. We present Humanoid Semantic Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Policy (HS-MoDP), which composes separately demonstrated arm skills through independent semantic routing over a shared expert pool. This design preserves arm-specific skill control while sharing action-generation parameters across arms. A semantic scheduler integrates the learned policy with an existing locomotion controller through alternating manipulation and repositioning stages, extending the effective workspace and enabling skill reuse across location changes. To acquire demonstrations of whole-body contact manipulation, we develop G1Exo, an isomorphic exoskeleton that provides direct joint-level control of arm configurations. Experiments on a Unitree G1 show stronger skill composition than the baselines. G1Exo enables more reliable contact teleoperation than end-effector VR-IK, and its demonstrations improve contact-skill learning across four policy architectures. For HS-MoDP, switching from VR-IK to G1Exo demonstrations raises full cabinet workflow success from 35% to 65%. All project hardware designs and source code will be released as open source after the review process.

</details>


### [107] [Data-Efficient Learning from Demonstration for Humanoid Robots via Feature-Level Perception and Prompt-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26170)
*Keyhan Rayati,AliReza Beigy,Anoosheh Saadati,Pourya Shahverdi,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数据高效的模仿学习框架，让人形机器人从紧凑的任务特征（而非原始图像）中学习操作技能：融合分层视觉预处理、基于Kinect的人体关节估计、提示式目标检测与深度信息提取人体运动特征和三维物体位置，再用时序深度模型预测机器人关节角轨迹。在NAO机器人上验证，仅用16个演示在仿真中取得78%的抓取放置成功率，100个演示提升至86%，用8个演示在推任务上达到71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的模仿学习方法通常依赖大规模数据集和资源消耗大的端到端训练，难以在算力有限的人形机器人平台上落地。作者希望降低对数据量和计算资源的依赖，实现数据高效的人形机器人操作技能学习。

Method: 提出一套从演示视频中提取紧凑任务特征的流水线：分层视觉预处理、基于Kinect的人体关节估计、提示式（prompt-based）目标检测、以及深度信息融合，从而获得人体运动特征与物体的三维位置；随后用这些特征训练一个时序深度模型，直接预测机器人关节角轨迹并执行。框架在NAO人形机器人上实现。

Result: 在仿真中定量评估：抓取放置任务仅用16个演示即达到78%成功率，使用100个演示提升到86%；推任务仅用8个演示达到71%成功率。并在真实硬件上进行了演示验证。

Conclusion: 以任务特征替代原始图像输入、配合时序轨迹预测模型，可显著降低数据与训练开销，同时保持较好的技能获取效果，证明了该框架在人形机器人数据高效技能学习方面的有效性。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) allows robots to learn new skills from human examples; however, many vision-based methods require large datasets and resource-intensive end-to-end training. This paper introduces a data-efficient LfD framework for humanoid robots that learns manipulation skills from compact, task-specific features instead of raw images. The proposed approach integrates layered visual preprocessing, Kinect-based human joint estimation, prompt-based object detection, and depth information to extract human motion features and three-dimensional object locations from demonstration videos. These features are used to train a temporal deep learning model that predicts robot joint-angle trajectories for execution. The framework is implemented on a NAO humanoid robot, evaluated quantitatively in simulation, and demonstrated on physical hardware. In simulation, the robot achieves a 78% success rate on a pick-and-place task using only 16 demonstrations, which increases to 86% when trained with 100 demonstrations. The framework also achieves a 71% success rate on a pushing task using eight demonstrations, highlighting its effectiveness for data-efficient humanoid skill acquisition.

</details>


### [108] [K-AoI: A Spatio-Temporal Synchronization Metric for Digital Twin-based Teleoperation in Dynamic Network Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26201)
*Lu Tian,Zhenhui Yuan,Zexin Deng,Wenjuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并验证“运动学信息年龄”(K-AoI)指标：以瞬时笛卡尔速度对AoI加权，把时间陈旧度转换为空间偏差，在6-DOF机械臂DT遥操作平台上跨7种网络条件评估，发现K-AoI与AoI的秩相关仅ρ≤0.65（卫星下降至0.22），说明它并非AoI的简单变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生与物理机器人之间的同步对遥操作至关重要，失配会直接削弱操作员的态势感知与任务表现。但现有同步度量几乎都是基于时间的，难以刻画运动过程中信息延迟所带来的空间后果。

Method: 提出K-AoI，将AoI按瞬时笛卡尔速度加权，把时间陈旧度映射为具有物理意义的位置偏差；搭建DT遥操作测试床（6-DOF机械臂、Unity孪生体），用tc netem 构造7种网络条件，做逐样本分析；并用Ultra-Low AoI (ULAoI)检验AoI尾部分布，验证广义帕累托分布(GPD)假设。

Result: 所有网络条件下K-AoI与AoI的秩相关ρ≤0.65，卫星链路下降至ρ=0.22，说明K-AoI捕捉了仅依赖时间的指标无法分辨的速度相关退化效应；AoI尾部检验拒绝了常用的GPD模型，反映确定性转发与有界抖动破坏了标准极值假设。

Conclusion: K-AoI提供了一个互补且具有物理意义的同步度量，把网络延迟与DT遥操作中任务相关的空间误差联系起来；数据集与测试床软件已公开。

Abstract: Synchronization between a digital twin (DT) and its physical robot is critical for effective teleoperation, as mismatches directly degrade operator awareness and task performance. Existing synchronization metrics are predominantly time-based and rarely capture the spatial consequences of delayed information during motion. In this paper, we introduce Kinematic Age of Information (K-AoI), a spatio-temporal metric that weights AoI by instantaneous Cartesian velocity, translating temporal staleness into physically meaningful spatial divergence. We implement and evaluate K-AoI on a DT teleoperation testbed comprising a six-degrees-of-freedom (6-DOF) manipulator, a Unity-based twin, and controlled network impairments using tc netem across seven network conditions. Per-sample analysis shows that K-AoI exhibits rank-correlations with AoI at $ρ\leq 0.65$ across all networks, dropping to $ρ= 0.22$ under satellite connections. This shows that K-AoI is not a simple transformation of AoI, but instead captures velocity-dependent degradation effects that time-only metrics cannot resolve. We further analyze AoI tail behavior using Ultra-Low AoI (ULAoI) and find that the commonly assumed Generalized Pareto Distribution (GPD) model is rejected. This reflects the impact of deterministic forwarding and bounded jitter, which violate standard extreme-value assumptions. Overall, K-AoI provides a complementary and physically grounded synchronization metric, linking network delay to task-relevant spatial error in digital-twin teleoperation systems. The dataset and testbed software are publicly available at: https://github.com/luziyaotian/kaoi-testbed.

</details>


### [109] [AnchorFlow-VIO: Fixed-Template Monocular Visual-Inertial Odometry on LaMAria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26210)
*Sudhir Pratap Yadav*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源单目视觉惯性里程计系统 AnchorFlow-VIO，并在 LaMAria 上以统一配置进行评测，公开代码与冻结配置，同时揭示启动敏感性与消融结果的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个基于 Basalt 的、配置统一且可复现的开源单目 VIO 系统，并在 LaMAria 基准上系统刻画其性能边界，说明"输出完整、特征数多、挑选成功运行"不足以证明定位稳健。

Method: 系统使用单灰度相机加单 IMU，结合出厂 IMU 校正、固定时间偏移调整、窗口化初始化和固定模板 patch 跟踪；在 LaMAria 全部 63 条测试记录上用服务器统一评测，并在 10 条训练记录上做消融（固定模板 vs 滚动模板、特征密度），另以 10 次前瞻固定 30 s / 120 s 起始偏移的新运行检验启动敏感性。

Result: 测试集控制点得分为 Short 47.2、Medium 37.1、Long 24.8、低光照 35.1、移动平台 15.6；5 m 位姿召回率为 84.6%、69.1%、53.9%、62.9%（四类）。训练集平均得分 41.38，265,694 个时间戳全部获得位姿（含 204 个填补位姿），但有一条平台记录发散得零分。固定模板在四条记录中三条优于滚动模板；提高特征密度结果不一。启动敏感性显著：同一低光照记录 30 s 起始得分 6.14，120 s 起始为 42.00。

Conclusion: 结果界定了该冻结系统的能力上限，并表明完整输出、特征数量与挑选成功运行均不构成稳健定位的证据；代码、冻结配置与可复现提交工具已公开，训练消融与测试评测分开报告。

Abstract: We present AnchorFlow-VIO, an open-source Basalt-derived monocular visual-inertial odometry system, and evaluate a uniform configuration on LaMAria. The system uses one grayscale camera and one IMU, combining factory IMU correction, a fixed timing adjustment, windowed initialization and fixed-template patch tracking. Server evaluation of all 63 test recordings yields control-point scores of 47.2, 37.1, 24.8, 35.1 and 15.6 for Short, Medium, Long, Low light and Moving platform. Test pose recalls at 5 m are 84.6%, 69.1%, 53.9% and 62.9% for the four categories reporting dense recall. On ten training recordings, mean control-point score is 41.38 and all 265,694 image timestamps receive poses, including 204 filled poses. One training platform recording diverges and scores zero. A paired four-record comparison favors fixed over rolling templates on three recordings, while reversing that ranking on the fourth. Increasing feature density also yields mixed results. Ten fresh runs at prospectively fixed 30 s and 120 s offsets expose substantial start sensitivity: a low-light recording scores 6.14 at the earlier start and 42.00 at the later start. These results characterize the limits of the frozen system and show why complete output, feature counts and selected successful runs are insufficient evidence of robust localization. Source code, frozen configuration and reproducible submission tools are publicly available; training ablations are reported separately from the submitted test evaluation.

</details>


### [110] [Robotic Photography System based on Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26287)
*Song-Li Wu,Chubin Zhang,Wenbo Ding,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboPhoto-VLA，一个统一视觉-语言-动作框架，联合建模感知、推理与相机控制，实现自主机器人摄影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人摄影需要在复杂环境中理解拍摄意图、规划相机运动并生成具审美价值的图像，现有方法难以统一建模感知、推理与控制。

Method: 框架包含两部分：PhotoCoT 用于结构化动作规划；Aesthetic Direct Preference Optimization（ADPO）使相机行为与人类审美偏好对齐。同时构建 OpenPhotography 数据集，含语言引导的演示数据，支持训练与评测。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，RoboPhoto-VLA 生成的摄影作品更符合人类审美偏好，并能有效泛化到未见过的拍摄对象、视角、姿态及复杂摄影指令。

Conclusion: 统一 VLA 框架结合结构化规划与审美偏好对齐，可有效提升自主机器人摄影的审美质量与泛化能力。

Abstract: Modern robotic photography requires understanding photographic intent, planning camera motions, and producing aesthetically pleasing images in complex environments. We present RoboPhoto-VLA, a unified Vision-Language-Action framework that jointly models perception, reasoning, and camera control for autonomous robotic photography. RoboPhoto-VLA incorporates PhotoCoT for structured action planning and Aesthetic Direct Preference Optimization (ADPO) to align camera behaviors with human aesthetic preferences. To facilitate training and evaluation, we construct OpenPhotography, a robotic photography dataset with language-guided demonstrations. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that RoboPhoto-VLA produces photographs better aligned with human aesthetic preferences while generalizing effectively to unseen subjects, viewpoints, poses, and complex photographic instructions.

</details>


### [111] [Cartender: Verifiable Language-Conditioned Driving by Learning the Drives Not Taken](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26390)
*Zhiwen Chen,Benjin Zhu,Yan Xie,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cartender 通过视觉语言模型构造"反事实分支"（配对/不配对的指令-动作对）来为驾驶策略提供语言条件监督，并用场景-语言兼容性门控与奖励训练的语言残差重排序来保证指令跟随符合场景约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景是一对多的：同一场景下多种行为可行，而指令只从中选择一个。但日志只记录已执行的动作，缺少对"其他可行行为"和"场景无法支持的指令"的直接监督。这导致忽略语言的策略与无视场景约束地跟随语言的策略可以在同一数据上拟合得同样好，无法区分。

Method: 提出 Cartender，从真实记录中学习"未走的驾驶"：1）Counterfactual Branching——无需仿真器，由视觉语言模型提议替代未来及其对应的选择指令，并以场景不支持的指令作为负标签，构造匹配与不匹配的指令-动作对；2）学习场景-语言兼容性分数，用于调节轨迹生成中的语言条件；3）由奖励训练的语言残差在场景分数上对候选轨迹重排序。分支仅用于训练；指令跟随的评估方式是在相同记录输入下交换指令并测量实际行为，而非与生成目标比对。

Result: 在 WOD-E2E 上，四提案中最佳者在 3,603 个可行指令条件下 RFS 达 8.642；在 8,974 个被接纳的提案中平均 RFS 为 8.203，高于匹配日志轨迹的 7.906，67% 的提案优于其记录对应项。相同比 backbone 的纯匹配模仿，Cartender 将同场景配对指令成功率从 23.67% 提升到 57.00%，RFS 从 6.32 提升到 7.10；其门控将不可行指令执行率从 53.36% 降至 14.18%。

Conclusion: 利用反事实分支提供替代行为的监督、并显式建模场景-语言兼容性，能够同时改善指令跟随的质量与对不可行指令的拒斥，说明"一对多"的驾驶本质需要超越日志模仿的学习信号。

Abstract: Every driving scene is logged once, but driving is one-to-many: the scene determines which behaviors are feasible, while an instruction selects among them. A log records only the action taken, leaving no direct supervision for alternative feasible actions or instructions the scene cannot support. Consequently, a policy that ignores language and one that follows it without regard to scene constraints can fit the same data. We present Cartender, which learns from the drives not taken. Counterfactual Branching constructs matched and mismatched instruction-action pairs from real recordings without a simulator: a vision-language model proposes alternative futures and their selecting instructions, while unsupported instructions provide negative labels. A learned scene-language compatibility score regulates language conditioning in trajectory generation, and a reward-trained language residual re-ranks generated candidates over a scene-based score. The branches are used for training; instruction following is evaluated by swapping instructions under identical recorded inputs and measuring behavior rather than agreement with generated targets. On WOD-E2E, the best of four proposals achieves an RFS of 8.642 across 3,603 feasible instruction conditions. Across 8,974 admitted proposals, mean RFS is 8.203 versus 7.906 for matched logged trajectories, and 67% score higher than their logged counterpart. Compared with matched-only imitation using the same backbone, Cartender improves same-scene paired instruction success from 23.67% to 57.00% and RFS from 6.32 to 7.10; its gate reduces infeasible-instruction execution from 53.36% to 14.18%.

</details>


### [112] [ForagerSim: Evaluating Embodied Map-Based Reasoning in Large-Scale Dynamic Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26395)
*Tomáš Musil,Martin Kotík,Michal Vavrečka,Karla Štěpánová,Sebastian Scherer,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ForagerSim——一个面向"地图式推理"的具身智能跨学科研究平台与仿真环境，涵盖从移动机器人搜救到动物觅食的多类任务，并沿感知、尺度、拓扑、动态性、任务五个独立维度调节难度，用以系统评测现有方法在地图式推理上的短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物能构建大范围环境认知地图并用于导航，甚至可能复用地路回路构建任意任务空间的地图；而当前具身AI要么依赖易失效、局限于度量空间的人工建图流水线，要么使用几乎不形成地图式表征的学习策略。阻碍灵活"类地图"架构发展的关键原因在于：鲜有具身AI基准真正要求地图式推理。

Method: 构建 ForagerSim 仿真平台，具备高模块化设计，可独立调节五个难度轴：感知（抽象2D传感至完整RGB或LiDAR）、尺度（房间级至数公里级）、拓扑（开阔场地至复杂迷宫）、动态性（烟雾、碎片、传感运动失效等）、任务（从简单2D覆盖到受觅食启发的井字交替任务）。在8个示例世界中将强化学习基线与简单工程化机器人栈、人类玩家进行对比评测。

Result: 当感知简化为2D时，学习策略在简单拓扑下完成多目标搜索与井交替任务表现意外良好；但在更大规模或更复杂拓扑的环境中，学习策略明显落后于人类水平，而基于地图的基线方法表现良好，表明地图式推理是这些场景中的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 地图式推理是现有具身AI方法在复杂、大尺度环境中性能受限的核心瓶颈；ForagerSim 为研究可灵活学习构建与使用地图式表征的架构提供了必要的评测平台。

Abstract: Animals build cognitive maps of vast environments, use them to perform map-based navigation, and might even be reusing the same circuitry used in spatial reasoning for creating and using maps of arbitrary task-spaces. In contrast, current embodied AI systems either rely on hand-engineered mapping pipelines, which break in the presence of unmodeled dynamics and are restricted to metric space, or on learned policies that rarely form map-like representations at all. Developing architectures that could learn to build and use map-like representations as flexibly as animals is, however, difficult because few embodied AI benchmarks actually require map-based reasoning. To address this, we present ForagerSim --- an interdisciplinary research platform and simulation environment focused on embodied tasks that require map-based reasoning, with scenarios ranging from mobile-robot search and rescue to animal foraging in both complex mazes and vast, potentially infinite open areas. ForagerSim is highly modular and allows modifying independent axes of difficulty related to perception (abstract 2D sensing to full RGB or LiDAR), scale (room-scale to multi-km scale), topology (open-fields to complex mazes), dynamics (smoke, debris, sensorimotor failures and others) and tasks (from simple 2D coverage to foraging-inspired well-alternation tasks). We compare reinforcement learning baselines with a simple engineered robotics stack and a human player on 8 example worlds which show how different difficulty axes affect existing methods. Our results show that, when sensing is simplified to 2D, learned policies can perform multi-object search and well-alternation tasks surprisingly well in simple topologies, but they fall short of human performance in larger or more complex-topology environments where map-based baselines perform well, suggesting that map-based reasoning is the bottleneck in these scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [RoboMMT: Learning Stable Tracking of Modulated Expressive and Interactive Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26414)
*Till Hielscher,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboMMT 模块化控制架构，将参数化表现性运动合成、基于物理的角色控制与面向空间交互目标的反应式适配相耦合，使机器人在动态不稳定平台上执行表现性动作时保持稳定，并实现零样本仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表现性机器人动作能提升人机交互的自然性、有效性和喜爱度，但在动态不稳定平台上，若表现性与物理控制解耦，反应式执行表现性动作可能使机器人触及稳定性极限。现有方法缺乏在广泛表现性域内同时保证稳定与准确的跟踪能力。

Method: ① 训练一个参考条件（reference-conditioned）的跟踪策略，覆盖广泛的表现性域，使机器人忠实实现指令动作并维持动态稳定；② 训练轻量级、任务特定的交互策略，调制参考生成器的空间目标吸引点，同时将稳定化跟踪策略保留在闭环中，实现目标反应性。

Result: 在四足机器人与两款人形机器人上的评估表明，学习到的跟踪器在整个调制域内保持稳定与准确，而未覆盖该域的跟踪器会失去平衡、损失跟踪精度或两者兼失。在稳定化跟踪基础上叠加闭环目标适配，可实现交互式朝向与指向行为，并保持稳定执行与相当的参考跟踪保真度；机器人能主动重分配体重并采取纠正性步伐。四足机器人在重扰动下的真机实验验证了零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: 将表现性运动合成与稳定化跟踪控制分层解耦、并通过闭环目标调制实现交互反应性，是在不稳定平台上部署表现性机器人动作的可行方案，且具备零样本真机迁移能力。

Abstract: Expressive robot motion can improve naturalness, effectiveness, and perceived likability in human-robot interaction. When deployed on dynamically unstable platforms, however, reactive execution of expressive body motion may bring the robot to its stability limits, especially when expressivity is decoupled from physical control. In this paper, we present RoboMMT (Robot Modulated Motion Tracking), a modular control architecture that couples parameterized expressive motion synthesis, physics-based character control, and reactive adaptation to spatial interaction goals. Our approach trains a reference-conditioned tracking policy across a broad domain of expressivity, enabling it to faithfully realize the commanded motions while maintaining dynamic stability. Goal reactivity is achieved by training lightweight, task-specific interaction policies that modulate the reference generator's spatial goal-attraction point, with the stabilizing tracking policy kept in the loop. Evaluations on a quadruped and two humanoid robots show that the learned tracker remains stable and accurate across the entire modulation domain, whereas trackers trained without this coverage lose balance, tracking accuracy, or both. We show that layering closed-loop goal adaptation over this foundation enables interactive facing and pointing behaviors, while maintaining stable execution and comparable reference-tracking fidelity. The robot learns to proactively redistribute its weight and take corrective steps. Zero-shot sim-to-real transfer is demonstrated through real-robot experiments on a quadruped under heavy disturbances.

</details>


### [114] [A Human-in-the-Loop Comparison of Proportional Control and Adaptive Gravity Compensation in Elbow EMG-Driven Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26445)
*Francesco Pascucci,Eldison Dimo,Davide Costanzi,Mario Sedilesu,Andrea Calanca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究在人体闭环条件下系统比较了上肢外骨骼的五种肌电驱动控制算法（两种不同助力水平的比例肌电控制、纯积分自适应重力补偿、运动学与加速度感知的模型型重力补偿，以及基于递推最小二乘的在线负载估计重力补偿），并通过14名健康受试者的肘部外骨骼目标到达任务发现：基于模型的重力补偿策略能有效抵消外部负载，降低身体与认知负荷并提升运动准确性和任务完成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌电驱动控制使可穿戴机器人能够依据用户神经肌肉活动提供助力，但上肢外骨骼常用的比例肌电控制（PMC）虽放大用户自身努力，却在高助力水平下可能损害稳定性；而肌电驱动的自适应重力补偿（AGC）作为替代方案虽能抵消重力矩并持续适应神经肌肉活动，但两类方法此前缺乏在人体闭环（human-in-the-loop）环境下的系统性对比，本文即针对这一空白展开研究。

Method: 研究比较五种控制算法：(1) 两种不同助力水平的比例肌电控制（PMC）；(2) 纯积分自适应重力补偿（AGC-PI）；(3) 运动学与加速度感知的模型型自适应重力补偿（AGC-AW）；(4) 基于递推最小二乘（RLS）进行在线负载估计的新型模型型重力补偿（AGC-RLS）。实验招募14名健康受试者，使用主动式肘部外骨骼在0 kg与2 kg负载条件下执行目标到达任务，评估指标包括肌肉活动、运动准确性、任务效率与主观负荷。

Result: PMC呈现体力消耗与任务表现之间的权衡：低助力水平在负载下需要更大的体力投入，而高增益则损害任务执行。AGC-PI降低了主动肌活动，但诱发了拮抗肌激活。相比之下，两种基于模型的重力补偿方法均能有效补偿外部负载，降低体力与认知需求，并提升运动准确性与缩短任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 基于模型的自适应重力补偿（尤其结合在线负载估计的方法）在人体闭环对比中优于比例肌电控制和纯积分重力补偿，能够在不诱发拮抗肌过度激活的前提下同时改善物理负荷、认知负荷与任务表现，提示模型型肌电驱动控制是上肢外骨骼助力的更优策略。

Abstract: Electromyography (EMG)-driven control enables wearable robots to assist users based on their neuromuscular activity. Upper-limb exoskeletons typically rely on proportional myoelectric control (PMC), which amplifies user effort but may compromise stability at high assistance levels. EMG-driven adaptive gravity compensation (AGC) offers a promising alternative, counterbalancing gravitational torques while continuously adapting to neuromuscular activity. However, these approaches have not been systematically compared in a human-in-the-loop setting. This study evaluates five control algorithms: two PMC strategies at different assistance levels, a purely integral AGC (AGC-PI), a kinematics- and acceleration-aware model-based (MB) AGC (AGC-AW), and a novel MB AGC approach based on Recursive Least Squares (AGC-RLS) for online payload estimation. Fourteen healthy participants performed target-reaching tasks using an active elbow exoskeleton under 0 kg and 2 kg loading conditions. Performance metrics included muscular activity, movement accuracy, task efficiency, and subjective workload. PMC exhibited a trade-off between physical effort and task performance: low assistance levels required greater physical effort under load, whereas high gains impaired task execution. AGC-PI reduced agonist activity but induced antagonist activation. In contrast, both model-based AGC approaches effectively compensated for external payloads, reducing physical and cognitive demands while improving movement accuracy and completion time.

</details>


### [115] [ALIGN: Aligning Condition Estimation and Expression in Advantage-Conditioned Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26455)
*Zechen Wang,Zhenqi Qiu,Houlong Xiong,Suohang Zhang,Yiyu Ren,Wanting Xu,Hongfei Niu,Chengyang He,Ge Sun,Ran Cheng,Qian Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ALIGN，一种面向视觉-语言-动作策略的"条件对齐"式 Advantage-Conditioned Behavior Cloning (AC-BC) 框架：直接在策略标签空间预测类别化的成对条件，并通过与 flow time 融合的 AdaRMS 调制注入 DiT 动作专家，从而更有效地利用混合质量机器人数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类采集的机器人操作数据常混杂成功行为、重试、停顿、恢复与失败。AC-BC 理论上可利用这类混合质量数据，但前提是条件信号既要从离线轨迹中准确构造，又要被有效纳入策略的动作向量场预测。现有流程存在两处削弱信号的问题：一是将估计器得到的优势分数阈值化成噪声较大的条件；二是通过 prompt 注入条件，对动作向量场影响微弱。

Method: ALIGN 在策略面向的标签空间中直接预测类别化的成对（pairwise）条件，避免阈值化带来的噪声；并将该条件通过 AdaRMS 调制（与 flow time 融合）注入 DiT 动作专家，使条件直接影响动作向量场。在 ARX X5 叠碗与 Franka 挂钥匙两个刻意混合的真实机器人数据集（标准示范 + 重试密集轨迹）上评估。

Result: ALIGN 提升了条件保真度，增强了条件化向量场的可分性；闭环成功率分别为 90.0%（叠碗）与 56.7%（挂钥匙），而最强 AC-BC 基线仅为 56.7% 与 33.3%。

Conclusion: 从条件估计到动作预测的全链对齐，是从成功、恢复与错误共存的数据中提取鲁棒操作策略的有效途径。

Abstract: Human-collected robot manipulation data often mix successful behavior with retries, pauses, recoveries, and failures. Advantage-Conditioned Behavior Cloning (AC-BC) can exploit such mixed-quality data, but only if the conditioning signal is both accurately constructed from offline trajectories and effectively incorporated into the policy's action-vector-field prediction. Existing pipelines can weaken this signal by thresholding estimator-derived advantage scores into noisy conditions, and by routing conditions through prompts that weakly affect the action vector field. We introduce ALIGN, a condition-aligned AC-BC framework for vision-language-action policies. ALIGN directly predicts categorical pairwise conditions in the policy-facing label space and injects them into a DiT action expert through AdaRMS modulation fused with flow time. We evaluate ALIGN on deliberately mixed real-robot datasets for ARX X5 bowl stacking and Franka key hanging, each combining standard demonstrations with retry-heavy trajectories. ALIGN improves condition fidelity, strengthens conditional vector-field separability, and achieves 90.0% and 56.7% closed-loop success, compared with 56.7% and 33.3% for the strongest AC-BC baseline. These results indicate that aligning conditions from estimation to action prediction is an effective way to extract robust manipulation policies from data in which success, recovery and error coexist.

</details>


### [116] [SPAR: Structural Pruning and Action Recovery for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26487)
*Shenli Zheng,Zhou Fang,Yuan Du,Yijiang Liu,Li Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPAR提出"结构剪枝+强化学习恢复"两阶段方案：对VLA模型LLM主干同时剪hidden与FFN中间维度并做剪枝感知微调，再用RL恢复策略性能。在π0.5上把语言主干从2.34B压到0.09B（约减96%）、整模型3.37B→1.12B（约减67%），LIBERO四套件平均成功率由84.55%升至93.45%（+8.90pp），推理加速1.98×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人端侧算力有限，VLA模型推理开销大；而激进压缩会带来监督微调难以弥补的操作性能下降，因此需要一种在强压缩下仍能保持/恢复策略性能的压缩范式。

Method: SPAR（Structural Pruning and Action Recovery）：1) 对LLM主干的hidden维度与FFN中间维度同时进行结构化剪枝，并配合pruning-aware tuning；2) 剪枝后用强化学习对策略（动作生成）进行性能恢复。

Result: 在π0.5上，语言主干2.34B→0.09B（约96%参数削减），整模型3.37B→1.12B（约67%）；该压缩率下RL将LIBERO四个套件的平均成功率从84.55%提升到93.45%（+8.90个百分点）；压缩策略相对未剪枝模型获得1.98×推理加速。

Conclusion: VLA模型中间维度存在大量冗余；剪枝后引入RL可有效恢复紧凑策略的操作性能，实现强压缩与高性能、快推理的兼顾。

Abstract: The limited onboard computing resources of robots make the inference demands of vision-language-action (VLA) models costly to accommodate. Reducing these demands through aggressive compression, however, can degrade manipulation performance beyond what supervised fine-tuning alone recovers. We present SPAR (Structural Pruning and Action Recovery), which simultaneously prunes the hidden and feed-forward intermediate dimensions of the large language model (LLM) backbone through pruning-aware tuning, then applies reinforcement learning (RL) to recover policy performance. Applied to $π_{0.5}$, SPAR reduces the language backbone from 2.34B to 0.09B parameters and the full model from 3.37B to 1.12B, removing approximately 96% and 67% of their parameters, respectively. At this compression level, RL raises average success across the four LIBERO suites from 84.55% to 93.45%, an 8.90-percentage-point gain. The compressed policy achieves a $1.98\times$ full-policy inference speedup over the unpruned model. These results identify substantial intermediate-dimension redundancy and show that post-pruning RL can recover manipulation performance in compact policies.

</details>


### [117] [Learn the Task, Not the Schedule: Reducing Spurious Cross-Arm Coupling in Bimanual Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26507)
*Shuyuan Hu,Deng-Ke Wu,Yifan Jin,Qianhang Luo,Mingchen Ju,Kai Ye,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Constraint-preserving Temporal Recombination (CTR)，一种训练期数据干预方法：在保留双臂各自局部传感器运动内容的前提下重排时间，生成满足任务必需跨臂约束的可行调度，从而避免模仿学习把"对侧手臂进度"当作捷径线索，消除虚假的跨臂时序耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂示范同时规定了"做什么"和"何时各臂动作"的一个特定调度。标准模仿学习无法区分任务必需的跨臂约束与偶然的调度选择，容易利用对侧手臂的进度作为捷径线索，在当前任务阶段并不要求相对时序时也学出虚假的跨臂耦合。

Method: CTR 通过保留记录的臂局部传感器运动内容，对相应片段重新定时（retiming），生成满足任务必需跨臂约束的多种可接受调度，作为训练期数据增强/干预。作者用 CTR 训练 Diffusion Policy、Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) 和 π_{0.5}，并在 4 个仿真任务与 4 个真机任务上评估。

Result: 在 6 种真机双臂进度状态初始化下，CTR 将最差状态成功率从最强非 CTR 调度混合基线的 56% 提升到 76%。在 Independent 任务上使用 π_{0.5} 时，4 s 暂停在 Concurrent 调度分布下会把 2.56 s 延迟传导到对侧手臂，而 CTR 仅为 0.25 s。真机上 CTR 仅 0–4% 的试验违反任务约束，而窄调度基线为 36–98%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦"任务必需约束"与"偶然调度选择"，CTR 有效抑制了双臂策略中的捷径学习与虚假跨臂耦合，显著提升各进度状态下的鲁棒成功率并大幅降低约束违反率，且可推广到多种主流策略架构。

Abstract: A bimanual demonstration specifies both what the robot should do and one particular schedule for when each arm should act. Standard imitation learning does not distinguish task-required cross-arm constraints from incidental scheduling choices, and may therefore exploit opposite-arm progress as a shortcut cue, creating spurious cross-arm coupling even where relative timing is irrelevant for the current task phase. We introduce Constraint-preserving Temporal Recombination (CTR), a training-time data intervention that preserves recorded arm-local sensorimotor content while retiming the corresponding segments to realize admissible schedules that preserve task-required cross-arm constraints. We use CTR to train Diffusion Policy, Action Chunking with Transformers, and $π_{0.5}$, and evaluate them on four simulated and four real-robot tasks. Across six real-robot bimanual progress-state initializations, CTR raises worst-state success from 56% for the strongest non-CTR schedule mixture to 76%. With $π_{0.5}$ on an Independent task, a 4 s pause transfers 2.56 s of delay to the opposite arm under the Concurrent schedule distribution, but only 0.25 s with CTR. On real robots, CTR violates task constraints in only 0--4% of trials, versus 36--98% for narrow-schedule baselines. Code will be released at https://github.com/MINT-SJTU/CTR.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Geometry-Aware Planning Representations for Monocular Flight with Cross-Modal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26508)
*Wencan Feng,Bailing Tian,Yulin Hui,Zhiyu Li,Junjie Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何感知的单目视觉规划框架，通过跨模态蒸馏把深度专家学到的规划相关特征迁移给RGB规划器，实现无需真实微调的高速避障飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微型飞行器高速导航多依赖深度相机、双目或激光雷达获取显式几何信息，重量与成本较高；单目相机更轻更便宜，但RGB图像缺乏直接度量线索且外观随环境变化大，使单目飞行困难。

Method: 以两帧图像、其相对位姿及MAV状态为输入预测轨迹。不显式重建深度，而是分三步：先训练基于深度的几何专家；再用配对RGB-D观测，通过跨模态蒸馏把专家的规划相关特征迁移到单目分支；最后使规划器适配下游轨迹规划目标，从而学到对避障有用的几何特征。同时构建3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）仿真器用于渲染观测、采集数据、提供配对RGB-D监督与仿真评估。

Result: 仿真中训练的规划器无需真实世界微调即可直接部署，在真实环境中实现最高6.5 m/s速度下的避障飞行。

Conclusion: 特征级几何监督与跨模态蒸馏可替代显式深度重建，使单目规划器具备几何感知能力；3DGS仿真为数据采集与监督提供有效支撑，验证了高效率、低成本的单目高速导航路径的可行性。

Abstract: Existing high-speed navigation systems for micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) commonly rely on depth cameras, stereo vision, or LiDAR to obtain explicit geometric information, while monocular cameras offer a lighter and lower-cost alternative. However, monocular flight remains challenging because RGB images offer no direct metric cues and visual appearance varies considerably across environments. We propose a geometry-aware monocular visual planning framework that predicts trajectories from two image frames, their relative pose, and the MAV state. Without explicitly reconstructing depth, the proposed method complements the downstream planning objective with feature-level geometric supervision by transferring the planning-relevant representation learned by a privileged depth expert to the RGB planner through cross-modal distillation. The framework first trains a depth-based geometric expert, then distills its planning features into a monocular branch using paired RGB-D observations, and finally adapts the planner to the downstream trajectory planning objective. This design encourages the policy to learn planning-relevant geometric features, enabling collision avoidance. We further use a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) simulator to render visual observations for data collection, paired RGB-D supervision, and simulation evaluation. The planner trained in simulation is then deployed in real-world environments without real-world fine-tuning, achieving obstacle avoidance at speeds of up to 6.5 m/s.

</details>


### [119] [ReInsVLN: Revisiting Historical Evidence for Training-Free Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26545)
*Siwei Cheng,Hao Wu,Zitong Qi,Xinming Zhang,Haoyu Xi,Peiran Yin,Haozhe Hu,Xudong Wang,Xiaoyu Shen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出诊断基准ReInsBench与免训练框架ReInsVLN，通过压缩历史视觉上下文并按计划提醒导航器回顾，在冻结导航器上提升成功率并降低延迟与显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航（VLN）依赖保留并利用相关观测，但历史增长带来冗余，且被保留的信息可能未被充分利用。作者希望诊断历史利用率问题并加以改进。

Method: 先用ReInsBench通过受控续接试验检验历史利用情况，发现"回顾历史式重新指令"可利用现有任务上下文恢复导航失败；据此提出免训练框架ReInsVLN，包含覆盖与指令感知压缩（时间冗余削减、覆盖感知保护、指令引导的token选择）以及遗忘启发的历史提醒（按预设计划引导回顾，无需额外模型调用）。

Result: 在R2R Val-Unseen上，ReInsVLN相对原生StreamVLN和InternVLA-N1分别提升成功率8.48与3.37个百分点，达到64.27%和64.44%；视觉压缩在匹配观测负载下基本保持导航性能，prefill延迟与KV缓存内存最多降低21.77%和32.25%；在人形与四足机器人上的真实试验支持其适用性。

Conclusion: 历史信息的有效回顾与紧凑化表示能显著提升冻结导航器的性能与效率，ReInsBench提供了诊断视角，ReInsVLN为无需训练、可直接部署的通用方案。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) relies on retaining and using relevant observations. Yet growing histories introduce redundancy, and retained information may remain underused. We introduce ReInsBench, a diagnostic benchmark that examines history utilization through controlled continuation trials. Its results show that history-review reinstruction can recover navigation failures using the existing task context. Motivated by this finding, we propose ReInsVLN, a training-free framework supporting memory construction and utilization in frozen navigators. Coverage- and Instruction-Aware Compression constructs compact visual context through temporal redundancy reduction, coverage-aware protection, and instruction-guided token selection. Forgetting-Inspired History Reminders guide navigators to revisit retained observations according to a predefined schedule, without additional model calls. On R2R Val-Unseen, ReInsVLN improves success rates by 8.48 and 3.37 percentage points over the native StreamVLN and InternVLA-N1, reaching 64.27% and 64.44%, respectively. Visual compression largely preserves navigation performance while reducing prefill latency and KV cache memory by up to 21.77% and 32.25%, respectively, on matched observation workloads. Real-world trials on humanoid and quadruped robots further support the applicability of ReInsVLN to sequential instruction following and ordinal navigation.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Wheel Motion Uncertainty for Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR-Inertial Odometry on Quadrupedal Wheel-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26550)
*Hairuo Wei,Yunfan Ren,Renda Yang,Qiangsheng Huang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧耦合LiDAR-惯性-运动学里程计框架，学习每轮、随运动方向变化的过程协方差，并配合退化感知投影，在高度退化的室内序列上显著降低定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式腿足机器人的轮运动学可在LiDAR几何约束较弱的场景下辅助里程计，但打滑与接触丢失使滚动预测不可靠；仅靠接触状态无法判断不同运动方向上预测的可信度。

Method: 保留解析滚动预测，从本体感知历史中学习逐轮、方向相关的过程协方差；估计器维护移动轮心状态，使学习到的不确定性经正运动学校正来调节滚动预测影响；引入退化感知投影（DAP）抑制几何弱方向上的LiDAR约束；协方差网络（CovNet）通过可微滤波器在仿真速度监督下训练，无需微调即部署于真实机器人。

Result: 在室内数据集中退化比最高的六个房间序列上，平均单序列位置RMSE较FAST-LIO2降低66.2%，较固定协方差变体降低31.0%；严重退化走廊场景下终点位置误差0.198 m，FAST-LIO2为7.531 m。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在挑战性几何与轮运动条件下里程计的鲁棒性；作者计划公开代码与多模态数据集。

Abstract: Wheel kinematics can support odometry for quadrupedal wheel-legged robots when LiDAR geometry provides weak motion constraints. However, slip and contact loss make rolling predictions unreliable, and contact state alone cannot determine their reliability across motion directions. We propose a tightly coupled LiDAR-inertial-kinematic odometry framework that retains analytical rolling predictions and learns per-wheel, direction-dependent process covariances from proprioceptive history. The estimator maintains moving wheel-center states, allowing the learned uncertainty to regulate the influence of rolling predictions through forward-kinematic corrections. Degeneracy-aware projection (DAP) complements this mechanism by suppressing LiDAR constraints along geometrically weak directions. The learned covariance network (CovNet) is trained through a differentiable filter using simulated velocity supervision and deployed on the real robot without fine-tuning. On the six room sequences with the highest degeneracy ratios in our indoor dataset, the method reduces mean per-sequence position root mean square error (RMSE) by 66.2% relative to FAST-LIO2 and by 31.0% relative to a fixed-covariance variant. In a severely degenerate corridor run, it achieves an endpoint position error of 0.198 m, compared with 7.531 m for FAST-LIO2. These results demonstrate robust odometry under challenging geometric and wheel-motion conditions. We plan to publicly release our code and multimodal dataset. Project website: https://weihairuo.github.io/wheel-motion-uncertainty/

</details>


### [121] [HAF-VLA: Hierarchical Action Flow Framework for Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26584)
*Langzhe Gu,Chengkai Hou,Meng Li,Xinhua Wang,Xinyuan Lv,Shuanghao Bai,Guangrun Li,Jingyang He,Ziluo Ding,Zhiyuan Xu,Ning Ding,Kuan Cheng,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HAF-VLA，一种基于预训练流匹配 VLA 的分层动作流框架，将全身动作生成分解为"移动+头部→腰部精修→全身操作"三个渐进阶段，并通过跨阶段 KV 缓存条件机制保持阶段间结构依赖，在六项真实任务上显著提升人形机器人全身移动操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用 VLA 策略迁移到真实人形机器人的全身移动操作仍然困难：与固定基座或轮式机械臂不同，人形机器人需在高维全身动作空间中协调移动、姿态、注视与双臂操作，基座或腰部的微小偏差容易引发上身剧烈且不稳定的补偿动作，而单次生成整段动作的方式难以刻画这种层级依赖。

Method: 在预训练流匹配 VLA 基础上继承视觉-语言前缀编码器与动作专家权重，引入可学习阶段嵌入与跨阶段 KV 缓存条件。不再一次性生成完整动作块，而是对共享的全身动作空间渐进式去噪：先移动与头部指令，再腰部精修，最后全身操作；后续阶段利用前一阶段的动作表示进行细化与增补。

Result: 在六项真实世界任务上，该框架显著提升了已有模型在人形机器人移动操作任务中的表现。

Conclusion: HAF-VLA 为将强力的移动操作 VLA 迁移至人形全身移动操作提供了一条有效路径，验证了分层动作流与跨阶段条件机制在真实人形控制中的价值。

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold great promise as general-purpose agents in human-centered environments, yet adapting generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policies to real-world humanoid loco-manipulation remains challenging. Unlike fixed-base or wheeled manipulators, humanoids must coordinate locomotion, posture, gaze, and dual-arm manipulation over a high-dimensional whole-body action space, where inaccurate base or waist motions can easily induce unstable and aggressive upper-body compensation. We propose HAF-VLA, a hierarchical action-flow framework for humanoid VLA control. Built upon a pretrained flow-matching VLA, HAF-VLA inherits the vision-language prefix encoder and action expert weights, and adapts them to humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation with learnable stage embeddings and cross-stage KV-cache conditioning. Instead of generating the full action chunk in a single pass, HAF-VLA progressively denoises a shared whole-body action space: first locomotion and head commands, then waist refinement, and finally full-body manipulation. To preserve structural dependencies across stages, we introduce a cross-stage KV cache conditioning mechanism, enabling subsequent action generation stages to refine and augment their outputs based on the action representations from preceding stages. Experimental results across six real-world tasks demonstrate that our framework significantly enhances the performance of existing models in humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, demonstrating an effective pathway for adapting strong mobile-manipulation VLAs to humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [122] [A Probabilistic, Rotation-Preserving Kernel Operator Surrogate for Continuum-Robot Design Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26605)
*Branden Frieden,Alan Kuntz,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于非平稳高斯过程（GP）核的算子学习代理模型，用于肌腱驱动连续体机器人（TDCR），可跨设计泛化并预测完整平衡构型与后验不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子TDCR模型通常绑定特定设计与驱动方式，泛化能力差且缺乏不确定性估计；需要一种能跨机器人设计与驱动泛化、并同时给出可信不确定性度量的代理模型。

Method: 构建由浅层神经网络从"力学感知设计坐标"中学习的非平稳GP核度量，使核能刻画设计空间中的相似性变化；该核定义设计到位姿的算子及其后验不确定性，并给出序列化仿真数据采集准则（核覆盖）。以空间核表示骨干位置与旋转应变，直接在SO(3)上重建姿态以保持有效材料坐标与肌腱几何；采用带Nyström预条件的ASkotch实现可扩展核算子求解，并用独立Nyström近似后验协方差。

Result: 核覆盖采集较IID采样将平均轨迹误差降低约15%；对越来越缺乏支持的设计，后验方差相应增大（不确定性校准合理）；相比已有神经算子TDCR模型，精度高30-50%，所需训练设计数量约减少5倍，且额外提供后验不确定性。

Conclusion: 学习度量型非平稳GP核是一种有效的跨设计TDCR算子学习方法，兼顾精度、样本效率与不确定性量化，可用于设计空间探索与主动仿真数据采集。

Abstract: We develop an uncertainty-aware, operator-learning-based surrogate for tendon-driven continuum robots (TDCRs) that generalizes across robot design and actuation while predicting the complete equilibrium configuration. Our central idea is a nonstationary Gaussian-process (GP) kernel whose metric is learned by a shallow neural network from mechanics-aware design coordinates. The network learns how similarity changes across design space, while the resulting kernel defines the design-to-pose operator, its posterior uncertainty, and a criterion for sequential simulator acquisition. We represent backbone position and rotational strain with spatial kernels and reconstruct orientation directly on SO(3), preserving valid material frames and tendon geometry. We use ASkotch with Nystrom preconditioning for scalable kernel operator solves and a separate Nystrom approximation for posterior covariance. Experiments show that kernel-coverage acquisition reduces mean trajectory error by approximately 15% relative to IID sampling, while posterior variance increases for increasingly unsupported designs. Compared with prior neural-operator TDCR models, the method is 30-50% more accurate using approximately 5x fewer training designs while additionally providing posterior uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [123] [CAOS-SLAM: Class-Agnostic Object-Aware Real-Time vSLAM for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26633)
*Gaia Borghi,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Angelo Bratta,Shahab Heshmati-Alamdari,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAOS-SLAM 在 ORB-SLAM3 基础上引入混合感知流水线，实时检测、跟踪并过滤已知与未知动态物体，从而提升动态场景下的视觉 SLAM 定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的运动物体会产生错误观测、破坏地图重建并降低定位精度；现有方法依赖预定义语义类别或高开销逐像素分类，鲁棒性与实用性受限，尤其难以处理未见过的动态物体。

Method: 构建按需触发的全景分割用于物体发现，对未分类区域通过深度聚类实例化候选未知物体；在分割更新之间，利用轻量检测（可用时）或基于世界坐标系卡尔曼预测、深度一致性与外观相似性的时序掩码传播维持持久物体轨迹；再由估计的物体运动剔除 SLAM 中的动态特征点。

Result: 在多个动态 RGB-D 数据集与真实实验中定位精度优于现有动态 vSLAM 方法：未知动态物体场景误差最多降低 59%，严重遮挡场景最多降低 88%，笔记本上中位处理速率达 30 FPS；跟踪模块还可输出物体轨迹与速度估计。

Conclusion: 混合感知与按需分割策略可在不依赖预定义类别的前提下有效处理未知动态物体，兼顾精度与实时性，并为感知、导航与运动规划等下游任务提供物体运动信息。

Abstract: Robust visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (vSLAM) in dynamic environments faces challenges due to the presence of moving objects that may introduce erroneous observations, corrupt the map reconstruction and overall degrade localization accuracy. Existing vSLAM approaches often rely on predefined semantic classes or costly pixel-wise classification, limiting their robustness or applicability in real-world scenarios. This paper presents CAOS-SLAM, a dynamic vSLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM3 with a hybrid perception pipeline to detect, track, and filter both semantically known and previously unseen moving objects in real-time. On-demand panoptic segmentation is performed for object discovery, with candidate unknown objects instantiated through depth-based clustering of unclassified regions. Between panoptic updates, persistent object tracks are updated using lightweight detection when available or temporal mask propagation based on world-frame Kalman prediction, depth consistency, and appearance similarity. The estimated object motion is then used to reject dynamic features from the SLAM pipeline. Experimental results on multiple dynamic RGB-D datasets and real-world experiments demonstrate improved localization accuracy over state-of-the-art dynamic vSLAM approaches, achieving up to 59% lower localization error in scenes characterized by unknown dynamic objects and up to 88% lower error in heavily occluded scenes, while achieving a median processing rate of 30 FPS on a laptop. Furthermore, the tracking module provides estimates of object trajectories and velocities, supporting potential future applications in perception, navigation, and motion-planning tasks in dynamic environments. Code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [124] [EgoRayFormer: Ray-Space Stereo Refinement for Egocentric 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26641)
*Haozhe Wang,Songyuan Wu,Jihai Xu,Jie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoRayFormer：在射线空间中耦合双目特征采样与几何精化的因果位姿估计器，在UnrealEgo2/RW上取得17.64 mm与49.55 mm的MPJPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头戴式双目鱼眼相机可紧凑地观测佩戴者身体，但强畸变、部分可见与短基线使3D位姿估计困难。现有精化解码器在各视图中独立采样图像特征，不要求采样点对应同一3D假设，缺乏显式双目约束。

Method: 提出因果位姿估计器EgoRayFormer，在射线空间中耦合双目特征采样与几何精化。对每个关节，将共享的3D假设沿其视线射线及其周围投影到两幅图像中；几何分支修正投影关节位置并求交对应射线；再用学习门控将几何估计与学习的位姿更新融合。

Result: 官方设备相对测试划分上三随机种子平均MPJPE：UnrealEgo2为17.64 mm，UnrealEgo-RW为49.55 mm，使用UnrealEgo2预训练后提升至47.57 mm。配对验证消融中，去除几何分支在两种输入分辨率下三个种子均使MPJPE上升。相同ResNet-18编码器与输入分辨率下，较设备相对的EgoPoseFormer基线降低RW测试MPJPE 9.09 mm。另有协议分析量化设备相对评估与oracle-root评估的差异。

Conclusion: 射线空间中的共享假设投影与射线求交引入显式双目约束，同时在射线深度约束较弱时通过门控保持灵活性；消融与基线对比验证了各模块有效性。

Abstract: Head-mounted stereo fisheye cameras provide a compact view of the wearer's body, but strong distortion, partial visibility and a short baseline make 3D pose estimation difficult. Existing refinement decoders sample image features independently in each view, without requiring the samples to correspond to a common 3D hypothesis. We introduce EgoRayFormer, a causal pose estimator that couples stereo feature sampling and geometric refinement in ray space. For each joint, the decoder projects shared 3D hypotheses along and around its viewing ray into both images. A geometric branch then corrects the projected joint locations and intersects the resulting rays; a learned gate blends this geometric estimate with a learned pose update. This formulation introduces an explicit stereo constraint while retaining flexibility when the rays provide weak depth constraints. In local evaluations on the official device-relative test splits, EgoRayFormer achieves three-seed mean MPJPEs of 17.64 mm on UnrealEgo2 and 49.55 mm on UnrealEgo-RW, improving to 47.57 mm with UnrealEgo2 pre-training. In paired validation ablations, removing the geometric branch increases MPJPE in all three seeds at both input resolutions. With a common ResNet-18 encoder and input resolution, our system reduces RW test MPJPE by 9.09 mm relative to the device-relative EgoPoseFormer baseline. A separate protocol analysis quantifies the difference between device-relative and oracle-root evaluation.

</details>


### [125] [RoboValue: A Fine-Grained Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Unified Evaluation of Robotic Value Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26663)
*Shengbang Liu,Zhengye Du,Zhilong Wan,Honghao Su,Chenxiang Xia,Chang Ge,Jinyang Xiao,Nan Wang,Zhipeng Hu,Huining Yuan,Shu'ang Yu,Yue Chen,Tianxing Chen,Chuankang Li,Maoqing Yao,Wenbo Ding,Yu Wang,Guanghui Ren,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboValue——一个面向机器人价值模型（value model）的细粒度仿真+真实基准，从四个互补能力维度统一评测价值反馈的可靠性，并发布数据集与公开榜单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人价值模型评估只覆盖执行理解的少数侧面，无法充分检验价值反馈的可靠性；需要更细粒度、覆盖多维度、兼顾仿真与真实场景的统一评测。

Method: 以共享接口构建四维评测框架：任务状态理解、时序进度监控、失败与恢复推理、价值一致性；用诊断性轨迹与指令变体检验价值判断是否准确反映任务需求与执行过程变化；构建 RoboValue-Dataset，覆盖 15 个仿真与 20 个真实世界操作任务，提供专家示范用于训练、带标注轨迹用于细粒度评测；在零样本与单样本设定下，于标准条件及机器人本体、环境偏移条件下评测代表性价值模型。

Result: 实验显示：即使在结果判断与前进进度打分上表现较强的模型，仍存在细粒度指令落地错误、依赖历史的进度跟踪错误和恢复评估错误；相同的语义子任务在不同合法执行顺序下会得到差异显著的价值增益。

Conclusion: 这些发现为价值监督指明了具体改进方向；数据集与公开榜单将持续支撑对反馈可靠性的评估。

Abstract: Robotic value models assess task execution from visual observations and instructions, providing feedback for policy learning and execution monitoring. However, existing evaluations cover only selected aspects of execution understanding, leaving the reliability of value feedback insufficiently tested. We introduce RoboValue, a fine-grained sim-and-real benchmark for unified evaluation of robotic value models. Its framework evaluates four complementary capability dimensions through shared interfaces: task-state understanding, temporal progress monitoring, failure and recovery reasoning, and value consistency. Diagnostic trajectories and instruction variations test whether value judgments accurately reflect task requirements and changes throughout execution. RoboValue-Dataset covers 15 simulation and 20 real-world manipulation tasks, providing expert demonstrations for training and annotated trajectories for fine-grained evaluation. We evaluate representative robotic value models in zero-shot and one-shot settings under standard conditions and separate shifts in robot embodiment and environment. Our results reveal errors in fine-grained instruction grounding, history-dependent progress tracking, and recovery assessment, even in models with strong outcome and forward-progress scores. The same semantic subtask can also receive substantially different value gains across valid execution orders. These findings identify concrete targets for value supervision, while the dataset and public leaderboard support continued assessment of feedback reliability. The website is available at https://robovalue-benchmark.github.io/.

</details>


### [126] [Robotic Physical Comparison of Biologically Motivated and Counterfactual Sensory Arrangements using Biomimetic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26680)
*Akihiro Miki,Shun Hasegawa,Yoshimoto Ribayashi,Yuta Sahara,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究开发分层仿生机器人皮肤，将传感器模拟物嵌入柔性软介质中以复现人体皮肤组织结构，进而对比"仿生"与"反事实"两种感受器空间排布在三种被动触觉任务上的表现，发现仿生排布性能更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体皮肤中Merkel细胞、Pacinian小体等机械感受器具有特定的空间组织，其形成受分子信号与神经诱导调控，但在活体系统中难以受控地检验这种空间组织的功能意义；尤其难以物理性地构建并比较生物学上并不存在的替代排布方案。

Method: 构建分层仿生机器人皮肤：在柔性软介质中嵌入感受器类比的传感器，复现人体皮肤组织的关键结构特征；在该平台上实现受生物学启发的排布与反事实排布，并定量评估其在三种被动触觉任务中的表现（弱接触检测、类两点辨别评估、纹理识别）。

Result: 反映生物学启发式感觉组织关键特征的排布方案，在所考察的替代配置中表现更优，即在弱接触检测、两点辨别类评估与纹理识别任务上取得更高性能。

Conclusion: 该研究以仿生机器人方法回应了"空间组织如何影响触觉功能"这一生物学问题，提供了可对活体系统难以研究的仿生与反事实感受器排布进行物理与实证比较的平台。

Abstract: Human skin contains diverse mechanoreceptors, including Merkel cells and Pacinian corpuscles, each localized in specific regions. Although their spatial organization is known to be shaped by molecular signaling and neural induction during development, experimentally examining its functional significance under controlled conditions in living systems remains challenging. In particular, physically comparing alternative receptor arrangements that do not occur biologically is difficult. In this study, we developed a layered biomimetic robotic skin designed to mimic key structural features of human skin tissue by embedding receptor-analog sensors within compliant soft media. Using this platform, we implemented both biologically motivated and counterfactual sensory arrangements and quantitatively evaluated their performance across three passive tactile tasks: detection of weak contacts, a two-point discrimination-like assessment, and texture identification. Arrangements reflecting key features of the biologically motivated sensory organization exhibited higher performance than the alternative configurations examined. This study addresses a biological question concerning how spatial organization influences tactile function by presenting a biomimetic robotic approach that enables physical and empirical comparison of biologically motivated and counterfactual sensory arrangements that are difficult to investigate in living systems.

</details>


### [127] [Robot Learning with Vision-Language-Action Models for Agricultural Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26717)
*Hamid Hafezi,Javad Mohammadpour Velni,Mohammadreza Davoodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于农业采摘（接近与抓取）的仿真到真实机器人学习框架，结合MuJoCo仿真环境、遥操作示教采集与预训练通用机器人策略（Octo、π0.5）的适配，并比较两者在分布内、视角/空间偏移和分布外条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业环境中的机器人操作面临植株几何形态变化、遮挡和相机视角变化等挑战；预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）与通用策略有望通过多模态观测和语言指令学习操作行为来应对，但适配到农业任务需要特定领域示教数据，而在真实机器人上采集成本高、耗时长。

Method: 构建基于MuJoCo的农业仿真环境，包含多样化的番茄植株结构以支持示教采集与受控评估；通过手柄遥操作、由底层控制器执行动作来采集专家示教；用仿真示教适配Octo与π0.5两个预训练通用策略，随后在实体Franka Research 3机械臂上用真实机器人示教进一步微调；在仿真中于分布内、视角/空间偏移和分布外条件下评估，并进行真机实验，同时考察本体感知信息对策略性能的影响。

Result: π0.5在任务性能得分上高于Octo，且在分布偏移下性能退化更小。

Conclusion: 将预训练通用策略与任务特定示教学习相结合，在农业机器人操作中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in agricultural environments remains challenging due to variations in plant geometry, occlusion, and camera viewpoints. Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) and generalist robot policies offer a promising approach to addressing these challenges by learning manipulation behaviors from multimodal observations and language instructions. However, adapting these policies to agricultural tasks requires domain-specific demonstrations, which are costly and time-consuming to collect on physical robots. In this work, we develop a simulation-to-real robot-learning framework for agricultural reaching and grasping that integrates task-specific demonstration collection and pretrained policy adaptation. A MuJoCo-based agricultural environment is developed with diverse tomato plant structures to support demonstration collection and controlled evaluation. Expert demonstrations are collected through joystick teleoperation, with a low-level controller used to execute robot actions. Two pretrained generalist robot policies, Octo and $π_{0.5}$, are adapted using simulated demonstrations, followed by further fine-tuning on a physical Franka Research 3 manipulator using real-robot demonstrations. The adapted policies are evaluated under in-distribution, viewpoint/spatial-shift, and out-of-distribution conditions in simulation, followed by physical-robot experiments. The influence of proprioceptive information on policy performance is also investigated. Results show that $π_{0.5}$ achieves higher task-performance scores than Octo and exhibits less degradation under distribution shifts. These findings demonstrate the potential of integrating pretrained generalist policies with task-specific demonstration learning for agricultural manipulation.

</details>


### [128] [Tactile In-Hand Object Rotation: A Comparative Study of Hand Morphology in Simulation and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26759)
*Wenhao He,Max Yang,Chenghua Lu,Yijiong Lin,David A. W. Barton,Nathan F. Lepora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究连续手内旋转任务中手部几何布局与触觉反馈的作用，通过在拟人型与对称型LEAP Hand上集成TacTip视觉触觉传感器并使用统一强化学习框架，结合仿真（12个未见物体）与硬件（10个物体、多种手部朝向）实验，分析全触觉、二值接触、纯本体感知三种观测的影响。结果显示全触觉显著提升旋转进度并降低指尖载荷，且对称手在旋转计数、速率与操作时长上优于拟人手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续手内旋转需要接触的维持与更新协调进行，因此操作性能与手部几何结构及触觉反馈密切相关，但二者的具体关系尚不清晰，本文旨在通过可控实验厘清手指布局与触觉信息对旋转表现、手指参与度和指尖载荷的影响。

Method: 在低成本LEAP Hand的拟人型与对称型两种手指布局上集成TacTip视觉触觉传感器，采用共享的强化学习框架。仿真中在12个未见物体上对比全触觉、二值触觉与纯本体感知三种观测；硬件实验中在10个物体、多种手部朝向下评估全触觉策略，并记录接触与指端力数据用于分析手指参与度与累计载荷分布。

Result: 仿真中全触觉观测相比纯本体感知将每回合平均归一化旋转量提升94.8%（拟人手）与66.9%（对称手），同时回合时长更长、平均指尖总载荷更低。硬件接触测量显示拟人手各手指参与度差异更明显，对称手累计载荷分布更均匀；对称手平均旋转圈数高32.0%、总体旋转速率高25.8%，操作时长更长，指尖平均总载荷低19.9%。

Conclusion: 手指布局会影响接触组织方式与载荷分担，对称布局带来更均匀的载荷分配与更高的旋转产出；同时在两种实体形态下，更丰富的触觉信息均能在更低平均载荷下支持更高的任务进度，说明触觉反馈对高效手内操作具有一致的正向作用。

Abstract: Continuous in-hand rotation requires coordinated contact maintenance and renewal, linking manipulation performance to both hand geometry and tactile feedback. We investigate these relationships through rotational progress, finger participation, and fingertip loading on two versions of the low-cost LEAP Hand with anthropomorphic and symmetric finger arrangements. Both hands are integrated with TacTip vision-based tactile sensors and use a shared reinforcement learning framework. Simulation evaluates full tactile, binary touch, and proprioception-only observations on 12 unseen objects; hardware experiments evaluate full tactile policies on ten objects under multiple hand orientations. In simulation, full tactile observations increase mean normalized rotation per episode over proprioception alone by 94.8% for the anthropomorphic hand and 66.9% for the symmetric hand, with longer mean episode durations and lower mean total fingertip loads. Hardware contact measurements reveal more differentiated finger participation in the anthropomorphic hand, while cumulative loading is more evenly distributed across the symmetric hand's fingers. Overall, the symmetric hand achieves a 32.0% higher mean rotation count and a 25.8% higher aggregate rotation rate, together with longer manipulation duration. Additional hardware force recordings show a 19.9% lower mean total fingertip load for this layout. These findings relate finger arrangement to contact organization and load sharing, and show that richer tactile information supports greater task progress at lower mean loading in both simulated embodiments.

</details>


### [129] [DexOAK: Object-Aware Kinematic Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26762)
*Federica Spinola,Robert Katzschmann,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexOAK 是一个基于学习的物体感知运动学重定向框架，从人体动捕数据生成接触一致的机器人手部参考，通过前馈网络实现免逐帧优化、可零样本迁移，并在仿真与真机上显著提升灵巧操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体动捕数据蕴含丰富灵巧操作信息，但直接迁移到机器人手存在手型与尺寸差异。传统方法多只复现人手姿态，忽略了手-物交互几何关系，导致接触不一致、穿模与物理仿真不稳定，且逐帧优化代价高、难以扩展到新数据。

Method: 1）在联合的手-物图上最小化交互图拉普拉斯目标，使机器人复现物体相对交互几何而非单纯人手姿态，得到逐帧种子参考；2）以人体关键点和物体点云为条件，训练前馈网络学习重定向函数，将种子解摊销；3）进行物体感知微调，引入接触、非穿透与自碰撞损失，将手指置于演示接触位置并减少破坏物理精炼的伪影；4）训练完成后仅需推理即可迁移至未见数据，无需逐帧优化。

Result: 在双臂任务、刚性与铰接物体上均优于SOTA动态重定向：Inspire 手上 OakInk-v2 成功率由 39.5% 提升至 48.4%，ARCTIC 上 ADD-AUC 由 62.7% 提升至 77.9%。消融实验表明增益来自物体感知运动学重定向与带接触损失的摊销网络；OakInk-v2 上训练后对 ARCTIC 零样本迁移，微调后接近域内性能。硬件上五个任务共 46/56 次开环回放成功。

Conclusion: 物体感知的运动学重定向结合摊销网络与接触感知微调，可高效、高保真地将人体动捕转化为机器人手部参考，支持零样本跨数据集迁移与真机开环回放，为从人类数据生成灵巧机器人操作数据提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce DexOAK, a learning-based object-aware kinematic retargeting framework for generating contact-consistent robot hand references from human motion-capture data. DexOAK first minimizes an interaction-graph Laplacian objective over a joint hand-object graph, so the robot reproduces the demonstrated object-relative interaction geometry rather than the human hand pose alone. These per-frame solutions form seed retargeted references, from which a feed-forward network conditioned on human keypoints and object point clouds learns the retargeting function. Object-aware finetuning then refines the network under contact, non-penetration, and self-collision losses that position the fingers on the demonstrated contacts and reduce artifacts destabilizing physics-based refinement. Once trained, the network transfers to unseen data by inference alone, without per-frame optimization. DexOAK references improve dynamic retargeting over the state-of-the-art across bimanual tasks with rigid and articulated objects. On the Inspire hand, success rises from 39.5% to 48.4% on OakInk-v2, and ADD-AUC rises from 62.7% to 77.9% on ARCTIC. Ablations trace the gains to object-aware kinematic retargeting and the amortized network refined with contact losses. Trained on OakInk-v2, it transfers to ARCTIC zero-shot and nears in-domain performance after fine-tuning. On hardware, 46/56 open-loop replays succeed across five tasks, enabling dexterous robot data generation from human data. Visualizations and interactive results are at: https://fspinola.github.io/dexoak/.

</details>


### [130] [HandsOn: A Large-Scale Simulation Benchmark for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26765)
*Tianwei Luo,Yiming He,Hongjin Wu,Ting Pan,Debin Ren,Haorui Guo,Junlin Yang,Yuxin Zuo,Zhehao Kou,Kaisen Yang,Zhuoqun Lai,Yi Ding,Jiale Yu,Yuchen Zhang,Zijie Qiu,Haozhan Li,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HandsOn：一个可组合的跨本体灵巧操作仿真平台与基准，包含5种机械臂、6种灵巧手、约200个刚体/铰接/可变形资产，提供1.8万条人类演示与66个经人工校验的任务（Core-50、Transfer-16），并给出多种主流方法及GPT-6零样本基线的评测结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作需在跨本体、跨物体、跨任务需求下保持可靠行为，但社区缺少统一、可组合的评测基座，难以衡量灵巧操作的泛化能力与物理交互的可靠性边界。

Method: 构建可组合仿真平台与基准：支持5种机械臂、6种灵巧手、约200个刚体/铰接/可变形资产及随机化场景；提供18,000条精选人类演示与66个人工校验任务。其中Core-50用于基于演示的学习与执行测试；Transfer-16评估资产与关系迁移、技能组合及可变形操作，且不提供目标任务训练演示。评测覆盖扩散策略、视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型及提示式VLM方法，另用GPT-6通过move_eef控制掌与指做免微调测试，并配以真机上线验证与分阶段诊断。

Result: 最佳训练型多任务策略在Core-50上成功率29.1%；未做基准微调的GPT-6在660次试验中，Core-50为5.2%、Transfer-16为1.9%。真机rollout定性展示sim-to-real迁移可行。分阶段诊断显示失败集中在接触建立、动作协调与已达目标的保持，组合任务中还存在序列错误。

Conclusion: HandsOn为研究操作泛化与可靠物理交互的极限提供了共享基座，同时表明当前方法在跨本体灵巧操作上仍远未可靠，接触与协调层面的失败是主要瓶颈。

Abstract: General-purpose manipulation requires reliable behavior across embodiments, objects, and task demands. We introduce HandsOn, a composable simulation platform and benchmark for cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation. It supports five robotic arms, six dexterous hands, and approximately 200 rigid, articulated, and deformable assets in randomized scenes. The benchmark pairs 18,000 curated human demonstrations with 66 human-verified tasks: Core-50 supports demonstration-based learning and execution tests, while Transfer-16 evaluates asset and relation transfer, skill composition, and deformable manipulation without target-task training demonstrations. Qualitative real-robot rollouts illustrate sim-to-real transfer. Across diffusion, vision-language-action, world-action, and prompted VLM-based methods, the best trained multi-task policy achieves 29.1% Core-50 success. Without benchmark finetuning, GPT-6 using move_eef for palm-and-finger control achieves 5.2% on Core-50 and 1.9% on Transfer-16 over 660 trials. Trained-policy stage diagnostics expose failures in contact establishment, action coordination, and maintaining achieved goals, with additional sequencing errors in composed tasks. HandsOn provides a shared basis for investigating manipulation generalization and the limits of reliable physical interaction.

</details>


### [131] [Bio-inspired Thrust-Assisted Trotting on a legged-Aerial Robot via Real-Time QP-MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26810)
*Shreyansh Pitroda,Eric Sihite,Alireza Ramezani,Morteza Gharib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在Harpy（带推进器的双足机器人）上实现了基于二次规划（QP）的模型预测控制器，用于实现推进器辅助的小跑步态，同时调节地反力与推力矢量，并以500 Hz实时运行，验证了控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受鹌鹑等鸟类利用"翅辅助运动"通过推力矢量操控地反力的启发，作者希望将这一生物机理引入推进器辅助双足机器人，实现地接触力与推力矢量的同时调节，并验证其在真实硬件上的实时可行性。

Method: 将推进器辅助的弹簧负载倒立摆（SLIP）动力学表述为紧凑的QP型模型预测控制问题；采用解耦控制架构：矢状面动力学由QP-MPC控制，额状面动力学由级联姿态稳定控制器调节；在Harpy机器人上进行0 m/s与0.1 m/s两种前向参考速度的实验。

Result: 瞬时速度虽有振荡，但平均速度能跟踪各自参考值；关节跟踪误差保持在1度以内（同时跟踪腿部轨迹控制器给出的期望腿长与Raibert启发式给出的摆动轨迹）；QP-MPC以0.18 ms稳定求解，远低于2 ms计算预算，并满足地反力摩擦锥约束。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑QP-MPC在推进器辅助双足机器人硬件上可实时、可靠地实现地反力与推力矢量的协同调节，支持推进器辅助小跑步态，具备实用的计算裕度和约束满足性。

Abstract: This paper presents the hardware implementation of a quadratic program-based controller on Harpy: thruster-assisted bipedal robot to perform thruster-assisted trotting. This is inspired by wing-assisted locomotion observed in birds such as the chukar partridge, which employ thrust vectoring to manipulate the ground reaction force. The proposed controller formulates the thruster-assisted spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics as a condensed QP-based model predictive control problem, enabling simultaneous regulation of ground contact force and active thrust vectoring at 500 Hz. A decoupled control architecture is used, where the robot's sagittal dynamics are controlled using QP-MPC while the frontal dynamics is regulated using a cascade orientation stabilization controller. Two experiments were conducted with different forward reference velocities of 0 m/s and 0.1 m/s. While the instantaneous velocity oscillates, the mean velocities can track their respective reference. Additionally, joint tracking error remains within 1 deg while tracking the desired leg length given by the leg trajectory controller and desired swing trajectory given by Raibert's heuristic. The QP-MPC problem is consistently solved with 0.18ms, which is well within the 2ms computation budget while satisfying the GRF friction cone constraint.

</details>


### [132] [GPT-6 Astra Is a Native Visuo-Tactile Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26846)
*Chen Yang,Linzhe Shi,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个演示表明，GPT-6 Astra 可直接通过视觉接口解读视触觉（vision-based tactile）图像，并将其接触线索用于机器人操作；在 19 个仿真任务上取得 93.8% / 81.8% 的平均成功率，显著超过最强专用策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型的进展使 GPT-6 Astra 可通过上下文学习完成机器人操作，但富接触控制与触觉感知长期被视为专用策略的优势领域；Astra 能否直接理解并使用触觉观测仍是开放问题。作者希望检验通用基础模型能否"外借"视觉接口来消化触觉信息，从而挑战专用策略在富接触操作中的独占地位。

Method: 将视触觉 RGB 图像直接输入 Astra 已有的视觉接口，无需改造模型结构；在 UniVTAC 与 OpenViTac 共 19 个仿真任务上进行少样本闭环控制评测，每种设置采样 210 个场景种子；与已报道的专用策略基线对比，并分析成功/失败轨迹中的行为模式。

Result: 视触觉输入下平均成功率为 93.8%（UniVTAC）与 81.8%（OpenViTac），分别高出对比中最强专用基线 22.9 与 13.1 个百分点；纯视觉输入亦领先这些基线，达 76.3% 与 80.6%。轨迹分析显示模型表现出主动探测、谨慎抓取、接触引导对齐与恢复等行为，但在切向加载与纠错执行上存在困难。

Conclusion: 结果表明专用策略在富接触操作中的独占性受到挑战，细粒度视觉可部分补偿触觉缺失；视触觉感知可作为基础模型扩展接触理解的一种接口，同时也暴露出基准饱和的迹象。作者据此提出联合视触觉预训练、更难的富接触评测以及真机验证等后续方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal models have enabled GPT-6 Astra to perform robotic manipulation through in-context learning. Yet contact-rich control and tactile perception are often regarded as strengths of specialist policies, leaving open whether Astra can directly understand and use tactile observations. We provide, to our knowledge, the first demonstration that Astra directly interprets vision-based tactile images and uses their contact cues for manipulation. Tactile RGB images enter through its existing visual interface, enabling few-shot closed-loop control on nineteen simulated UniVTAC and OpenViTac tasks with 210 sampled scene seeds per setting. With visuo-tactile input, Astra achieves mean success rates of 93.8% and 81.8%, exceeding the strongest reported specialist baselines in our comparison by 22.9 and 13.1 percentage points, respectively. Vision-only performance also leads these baselines, reaching 76.3% and 80.6%. Trajectory analysis reveals active probing, cautious grasping, contact-guided alignment, and recovery, alongside difficulties with tangential loading and corrective execution. Our findings challenge the exclusivity of specialist policies in contact-rich manipulation and show that fine-grained vision can partially compensate for missing touch. They also identify vision-based tactile sensing as an interface for scaling contact understanding with foundation models and reveal signs of benchmark saturation. These results motivate joint visual-tactile pretraining, harder contact-rich evaluations, and physical-robot validation.

</details>


### [133] [Where is Physical RSI capability from? An Observational Scaling Law of LLM-as-policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26848)
*Zekai Li,Jiayi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Luyang Niu,Zekun Qi,Jun Zheng,Xuchuan Chen,Dairu Liu,Yintianrun Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究物理环境下的递归自我改进（Physical RSI），提出五轴评估框架 PhysRSI-eval 揭示"物理木桶假设"，并分析可观测缩放规律与改进的沉淀形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对物理场景中递归自我改进的机制、缩放行为与泛化能力的系统刻画，公共基准平均分也难以区分能力相近的系统，无法回答"智能体能多快、多远地改进"。

Method: 提出 PhysRSI-eval，用五轴指标（首次尝试表现、效率、成功率、预算、失败后的改进）在固定交互预算下评测多种 VLM 于 18 个操作任务；从"由公共能力预测相对表现"和"由早期物理反馈预测后续进展"两个视角研究可观测缩放；并通过工作区干预（移除历史、冻结工作区、跨任务迁移）分析改进所留下的痕迹。

Result: 五轴指标能区分公共基准平均分接近的系统，并显示高分不一定对应早期或高效增益；移除历史降低后期成功率，冻结工作区在未见布局上仍有用，三个跨任务案例更早取得成功。

Conclusion: 场景特定适应与可复用经验可以共存，改进的机制具有分桶/瓶颈式的多因素约束特征，需多维度指标与干预式分析才能完整刻画物理 RSI。

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically investigate the mechanisms, scaling behavior, and generalization of physical Recursive self-improvement. We introduce PhysRSI-eval to quantify physical RSI through a five-axis metric: First-Attempt Performance, Efficiency, Success, Budget and Improvement from failure. Different VLMs are evaluated on 18 manipulation tasks under a fixed interaction budget. The analysis of experimental results motivates the Physical Barrel Hypothesis: different capabilities may constrain different parts of improvement. The five-axis metrics also distinguish systems with close public benchmark averages and show that high scores need not come with early or efficient gains. We study observational scaling from two perspectives: predicting the relative performance of VLM agents from their public capabilities, and forecasting later progress from early physical feedback. Together, these analyses ask how quickly and how far an agent can improve. Moreover, we conduct workspace interventions and then analyze what improvement leaves behind. Removing history reduces late-round success, frozen workspaces remain useful on unseen layouts, and three cross-task cases achieve success earlier. The results suggest that scene-specific adaptation and reusable experience can coexist.

</details>


### [134] [CoopUQ: Robust Multi-Robot Social Navigation via Uncertainty-Aware Cooperative Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26852)
*Zehao Wang,Jianpeng Yao,Yuping Wang,Yiping Li,Jiaqi Li,Zhixu Li,Kanghoon Lee,Jinfeng Wang,Cong Liu,Zhaowei Tan,Jinkyoo Park,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoopUQ 多机器人社交导航框架，将不确定性感知的跨机器人占用预测与约束多智能体强化学习结合，在遮挡和分布外场景下提升安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单机器人强化学习导航方法直接扩展到多机器人时，难以保证安全与效率，尤其在遮挡和分布外（OOD）场景下，单个机器人容易忽视潜在风险或做出错误预测；如何有效利用机器人间的协作来同时改进预测与导航仍是主要挑战。

Method: CoopUQ 通过跨机器人时空特征融合来预测未来占用栅格地图，并将预测结果及其不确定性用于引导策略；策略采用约束多智能体强化学习训练，代价项惩罚穿过被预测为占用或高不确定性区域的行为，从而在分布偏移下自适应调整风险敏感度。

Result: 在多个基于 Unity3D 的仿真环境中（含分布内与 OOD 设置，覆盖不同布局、行人密度和 rushing 行为），预测性能与安全性优于代表性基线；真实机器人实验也验证了其在多种场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将不确定性感知的协作占用预测与约束式多智能体强化学习相结合，是一种可行且有效的多机器人社交导航方案，能够在分布偏移与遮挡条件下保持较好的安全性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables mobile robots to make fast and scalable navigation decisions in constrained human-shared environments. However, directly extending single-robot approaches to multi-robot settings often fails to ensure safety and efficiency, especially under occlusions and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, where individual robots may overlook potential risks or make inaccurate predictions. Leveraging cooperation effectively for both prediction and navigation remains a major challenge. We propose CoopUQ, a multi-robot social navigation framework that bridges uncertainty-aware cooperative occupancy forecasting and robust decision making. CoopUQ predicts future occupancy grid maps through cross-robot spatiotemporal feature fusion and further leverages forecasts and their uncertainty to guide policy via constrained multi-agent reinforcement learning, where the cost penalizes navigation through regions predicted to be occupied or highly uncertain. This design enables the policy to adapt its risk sensitivity under distribution shifts. Experimental results across multiple Unity3D-based simulation environments with both in-distribution and OOD settings, including diverse layouts, pedestrian densities, and rushing behavior, demonstrate improved prediction performance and safety over representative baselines. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate its robustness across various scenes. Our code and videos are available at https://coopuq.github.io/.

</details>


### [135] [Physical Words: A Vocabulary for Structuring Latent Reasoning in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26854)
*Jiaming Liu,Xinghao Zhu,Yiming Zhang,Shanghang Zhang,Yinxi Wang,Yichu Yang,Zhuoyang Liu,Dantong Niu,Soha Pouya,Juekun Li,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysWord，以原子动作动词作为"Physical Words"语义锚点，对齐连续物理动态隐表示，构建时序结构化多模态推理空间，实现"先推理后行动"的机器人基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理对机器人可靠动作生成很重要，但现有两类方法各有短板：语言推理能表达任务语义却难以刻画细粒度物理动态；隐空间推理能建模未来状态演化，却缺乏将动态组织为可复用推理原语的结构。

Method: 以原子动作动词作为共享语义锚点表示连续物理动态；预测未来原子动作词，并将其上下文隐状态与由对应未来观测导出的隐目标对齐；在此基础上构建时序结构化的PhysWord空间，为每个动作词提供视觉及可选触觉变体；自回归地预测未来各时间步的Physical Words，捕捉执行阶段间转换，为动作生成提供结构化多模态上下文。

Result: 在视触觉UniVTAC上成功率达90.3%，视觉LIBERO-Long上达99.6%，均为SOTA；在双臂夹爪与灵巧手真实任务上优于对比方法；进一步评估表明该结构化隐推理框架可泛化到未见场景并支持长程操作。

Conclusion: 将动作语言语义与连续物理动态隐表示通过Physical Words统一，可兼顾结构性与细粒度动态建模，为机器人基础模型提供可复用、可泛化的推理原语。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning provides robotic foundation models with predictive context needed for reliable action generation. However, language-based reasoning captures task semantics but struggles to express fine-grained physical dynamics, while latent reasoning models future state evolution but offers limited structure for organizing these dynamics into reusable reasoning primitives. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysWord, a reasoning-before-acting foundation model built on Physical Words, which use atomic action verbs as shared semantic anchors for continuous representations of physical dynamics. Specifically, PhysWord predicts upcoming atomic action words and directly aligns their contextual hidden states with latent targets derived from the corresponding future observations. Building on these primitives, we construct a temporally structured PhysWord space with visual and optional tactile variants of each action word. Autoregressively predicting Physical Words across future timesteps captures transitions between execution stages, providing structured multimodal context for action generation. PhysWord achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 90.3\% on visuotactile UniVTAC and 99.6\% on vision-based LIBERO-Long, and outperforms competing methods on real-world tasks using bimanual grippers and dexterous hands. Further evaluations demonstrate that our structured latent reasoning framework generalizes to unseen scenarios and enables long-horizon manipulation.

</details>
